### [1] [Geometry-Aware Representation Denoising for Robust Multi-view 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26230)
*Jin Hyeon Kim,Jaeeun Lee,Claire Kim,Kyoungjin Oh,Paul Hyunbin Cho,Jaewon Min,Yeji Choi,Jihye Park,Hyunhee Park,Minkyu Park,Seungryong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GARD框架，在3D重建模型的特征空间中执行扩散去噪，实现了在退化条件下同时恢复3D场景几何和高保真RGB图像。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有前馈式3D重建模型通常在无退化理想条件下训练，而真实观测常存在各种退化，这些模型鲁棒性不足。

Method: GARD框架在前馈式3D重建模型的特征空间中执行基于扩散的多视图恢复，利用3D重建器的几何感知特征表示来恢复准确场景几何，并辅以RGB图像解码器同时恢复高质量图像。

Result: 在Depth Anything 3 (DA3)基准上验证了GARD框架的有效性。

Conclusion: GARD框架能有效提升前馈式3D重建模型在退化条件下的鲁棒性，并实现几何与图像的联合恢复。

Original Abstract: Multi-view 3D reconstruction has achieved remarkable progress with the advent of feed-forward 3D reconstruction models. However, these models are typically trained and evaluated under ideal, degradation-free imaging conditions, whereas real-world observations often contain degradations that differ significantly from such settings. Improving robustness for multi-view 3D reconstruction under degraded conditions therefore remains an important challenge. We present Geometry-Aware Representation Denoising (GARD), a novel framework that performs diffusion-based multi-view restoration directly in the feature space of a feed-forward 3D reconstruction model. This design exploits the geometry-aware feature representations of the 3D reconstructor to effectively recover accurate scene geometry. Furthermore, by employing an additional RGB image decoder, the refined representations can also be used to restore high-quality RGB images, thereby enabling the simultaneous recovery of 3D scene geometry and high-quality imagery. Comprehensive experiments on the Depth Anything 3 (DA3) benchmark demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed GARD framework.

中文摘要翻译: 多视图三维重建随着前馈式三维重建模型的出现取得了显著进展。然而，这些模型通常在理想的无退化成像条件下进行训练和评估，而真实世界的观测往往包含与此类设置显著不同的退化。因此，提高退化条件下多视图三维重建的鲁棒性仍然是一个重要挑战。我们提出了几何感知表示去噪（GARD），一种新颖的框架，它直接在三维重建前馈模型的特征空间中执行基于扩散的多视图恢复。该设计利用三维重建器的几何感知特征表示，有效地恢复准确的场景几何。此外，通过使用额外的RGB图像解码器，精炼的表示还可用于恢复高质量的RGB图像，从而实现了三维场景几何与高质量图像的同时恢复。在Depth Anything 3（DA3）基准上的综合实验证明了所提GARD框架的有效性。

</details>


### [2] [Not All Modalities Are Equal: Instruction-Aware Gating for Multimodal Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26232)
*Bonan Ding,Umair Nawaz,Ufaq Khan,Abdelrahman M. Shaker,Muhammad Haris Khan,Jiale Cao,Jin Xie,Fahad Shahbaz Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniMVU，一种统一的多模态视频理解框架，通过指令感知的内模态门控和模态级门控实现多模态自适应融合，在六个基准上超越静态融合基线，CIDEr指标提升高达13.5。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的视频大语言模型在视频带有辅助流（如音频、深度图等）时，统一融合会导致模态干扰，无关通道会分散模型注意力，因此需要一种能根据指令自适应平衡各模态重要性的融合方法。

Method: 提出UniMVU框架，包含两个级别的动态门控机制：(1) 内模态门控：强调每个模态内的显著区域；(2) 模态级门控：重新加权整个模态流；两者均以文本指令为条件进行自适应平衡。同时采用跨模态自注意力、基于控制令牌的模态级门控模块，以及对时间对齐流采用快-慢融合方案减少冗余。

Result: 在AVQA、AVSD、Music-AVQA、ScanQA、SQA3D和MVBench六个基准上，UniMVU相比静态融合基线取得一致提升，CIDEr指标最高提升13.5。门控机制与人类可解释的模态相关性一致，消融实验验证了两级门控的有效性。

Conclusion: UniMVU提供了一种简单、统一且无需手工设计融合规则的指令感知多模态视频理解方案，能够扩展到多种模态输入。

Original Abstract: Pre-trained video large language models excel at visual reasoning. However, they struggle when videos arrive with auxiliary streams, such as audio, depth map, or dense temporal evidence. In such a scenario, uniform fusion induces modality interference, allowing irrelevant channels to distract the model. To address this issue, we present a unified multimodal video understanding framework, named UniMVU, that performs instruction-aware fusion across video, audio, depth map, or any other modality inputs via two levels of dynamic gating: inner-modality gates emphasize salient regions within each modality, whereas modality-level gates re-weight whole streams; both are conditioned on the text instruction to adaptively balance modality importance. Our UniMVU combines cross-modal self-attention with instruction-driven inner-modality gating module and a modality-level gating module with control token; for time-aligned streams we further adopt a fast-to-slow fusion scheme that reduces redundancy. Across six benchmarks (AVQA, AVSD, Music-AVQA, ScanQA, SQA3D and MVBench), our UniMVU achieves consistent gains over static-fusion baselines achieving gains as high as 13.5 in terms of CIDEr metric. Further, our analysis shows that the gating mechanism aligns with the human-interpretable modality relevance, and ablations show the contributions of inner-modality and modality-level gating. Our UniMVU provides a simple, unified recipe for instruction-aware multimodal video understanding that scales to diverse modalities without hand-crafted fusion rules.

中文摘要翻译: 预训练的视频大语言模型在视觉推理方面表现出色。然而，当视频带有辅助流（如音频、深度图或密集时间证据）时，这些模型会面临困难。在这种情况下，统一融合会导致模态干扰，使得无关通道分散模型的注意力。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一个统一的多模态视频理解框架UniMVU，它通过两个级别的动态门控机制实现跨视频、音频、深度图或任何其他模态输入的指令感知融合：内模态门控强调每个模态内的显著区域，而模态级门控重新加权整个模态流；两者都以文本指令为条件，自适应地平衡模态重要性。我们的UniMVU结合了跨模态自注意力与指令驱动的内模态门控模块，以及带有控制令牌的模态级门控模块；对于时间对齐的流，我们进一步采用快-慢融合方案以减少冗余。在六个基准（AVQA、AVSD、Music-AVQA、ScanQA、SQA3D和MVBench）上，我们的UniMVU相比静态融合基线取得了一致的提升，在CIDEr指标上最高提升13.5。此外，我们的分析表明，门控机制与人类可解释的模态相关性一致，消融实验验证了内模态和模态级门控的贡献。我们的UniMVU提供了一种简单、统一的指令感知多模态视频理解方案，可以扩展到多种模态输入，无需手工设计的融合规则。

</details>


### [3] [DuoGesture: Neuro-Inspired and Biomechanically Informed Dual-Stream Co-Speech Gesture Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26236)
*Ferdinand Paar,Lanmiao Liu,Aslı Özyürek,Serge Thill,Esam Ghaleb*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DuoGesture 提出了一种神经启发、受生物力学约束的双流方法，将共语手势生成分解为语义流和节拍流，通过语义变分信息瓶颈、运动锚定语义条件和惯性节拍先验实现更优的语义表达与运动平滑性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有整体式手势模型将基于词汇的语义手势与频繁的韵律节拍手势混合在一起，导致语义锚定不足、语音-运动对齐不准确以及运动平滑度差。需要一种解耦的方法来分别处理语义手势和节拍手势。

Method: 提出双流架构 DuoGesture：1) 语义流和节拍流分别生成语义手势和节拍手势；2) 语义变分信息瓶颈作为随机帧级门控，学习何时语义手势应覆盖节拍运动；3) 运动锚定语义条件用运动-语言表示替代纯语言词嵌入，为长尾词汇触发的手势提供运动对齐的语义先验；4) 惯性节拍先验是基于人体测量学的加权臂链模块，减少抖动并改善节拍一致性。

Result: 客观评估和主观实验表明 DuoGesture 优于强基线整体式模型；组件消融实验证实了语义锚定、随机流选择和生物力学正则化的互补作用。

Conclusion: 将共语手势生成分解为耦合的语义流和节拍流，结合神经启发与生物力学约束，能够同时提升语义表达力和运动学平滑性，超越传统整体式方法。

Original Abstract: Co-speech gesture generation requires both semantic expressivity and biomechanically plausible rhythmic motion. Existing holistic gesture models mix lexically grounded semantic gestures with frequent prosody-aligned beat gestures. This limits semantic grounding, speech-motion alignment, and kinematic smoothness. We propose \emph{DuoGesture}, a neuro-inspired and biomechanically informed dual-stream approach that decomposes co-speech gesture synthesis into coupled semantic and beat streams. The two streams are coordinated by a \emph{Semantic Variational Information Bottleneck}, a stochastic frame-level gate that learns when semantic gestures should override rhythmic beat motion. The semantic stream is controlled by \emph{Motion-Grounded Semantic Conditioning}, which replaces purely linguistic word embeddings with motion-language representations to provide motion-aligned semantic priors for long-tailed lexical triggers of gestures. The beat stream is further regularised by an \emph{Inertial Beat Prior}, an anthropometry-weighted arm-chain module that reduces jitter and improves rhythmic consistency without constraining semantic frames. Objective evaluations and subjective experiments show that DuoGesture outperforms strong holistic baselines, while component ablations confirm the complementary roles of semantic grounding, stochastic stream selection, and biomechanical regularisation.

中文摘要翻译: 共语手势生成既需要语义表达力，也需要生物力学上合理的节律运动。现有的整体式手势模型将基于词汇的语义手势与频繁的韵律对齐节拍手势混合在一起，这限制了语义锚定、语音-运动对齐以及运动学平滑性。我们提出了 \emph{DuoGesture}——一种神经启发且受生物力学约束的双流方法，它将共语手势合成分解为耦合的语义流和节拍流。这两个流由一个 \emph{语义变分信息瓶颈}（一种随机的帧级门控机制）来协调，学习何时语义手势应覆盖节律节拍运动。语义流由 \emph{运动锚定语义条件} 控制，该条件用运动-语言表示替代纯语言词嵌入，为手势触发的长尾词汇提供运动对齐的语义先验。节拍流进一步通过 \emph{惯性节拍先验}（一个基于人体测量学的加权臂链模块）进行正则化，在不对语义帧施加约束的情况下减少抖动并改善节律一致性。客观评估和主观实验表明，DuoGesture 优于强基线整体式模型，而组件消融实验证实了语义锚定、随机流选择和生物力学正则化的互补作用。

</details>


### [4] [Revealing the core dimensions underlying representations in brains, behavior and AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26921)
*Florian P. Mahner,Ka Chun Lam,Francisco Pereira,Martin N. Hebart*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出相似性基表示因子分解（SRF），一种从相似性矩阵中恢复低维、非负、可解释嵌入的通用计算方法。在模拟和多种神经、行为、计算数据集上，SRF能从稀疏不完整数据中恢复可解释维度，匹配任务特定模型，预测独立行为属性，提升探索性分析和假设检验效力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前研究表示的方法主要通过刺激间的相似性来比较，但对塑造这些表示的维度的可解释性有限，且方法受限。为了克服这些挑战，需要一种能恢复可解释嵌入的通用方法。

Method: 相似性基表示因子分解（Similarity-Based Representation Factorization, SRF），一种从实测数据导出的相似性矩阵中恢复低维、非负、可解释嵌入的通用计算方法。

Result: 在模拟和多种神经、行为、计算数据集上，SRF能从稀疏不完整的表示数据中恢复可解释维度；这些维度匹配任务特定模型的结果，能预测独立行为属性，改善探索性分析，且相比直接比较相似性矩阵具有更高的假设检验效力。

Conclusion: SRF是一种通用方法，在揭示、理解和利用表示背后的维度方面具有广泛的应用前景。

Original Abstract: The study of representations is widespread across fields, including neuroscience, psychology, and artificial intelligence. While representations are often studied and compared through similarities between stimuli, current methods provide only limited access to the dimensions that shape these representations and are often limited in interpretability. To overcome these challenges, here we introduce Similarity-Based Representation Factorization (SRF), a general computational method for recovering low-dimensional, non-negative, interpretable embeddings from similarity matrices derived from measured data. Across simulations and many neural, behavioral, and computational datasets, SRF recovers interpretable dimensions from diverse forms of representational data, even for very sparsely sampled, incomplete data. The dimensions derived from these datasets match those obtained by task-specific models, predict independent behavioral properties, improve exploratory analysis, and offer higher power for confirmatory hypothesis testing than comparing similarity matrices. Together, these results establish SRF as a general-purpose method with broad applications for uncovering, understanding, and leveraging the dimensions underlying representations.

中文摘要翻译: 表示的研究广泛存在于神经科学、心理学和人工智能等领域。虽然表示通常通过刺激之间的相似性来进行研究和比较，但当前方法对塑造这些表示的维度的可访问性有限，且通常可解释性受限。为了克服这些挑战，本文提出了相似性基表示因子分解（SRF），一种从实测数据导出的相似性矩阵中恢复低维、非负、可解释嵌入的通用计算方法。在模拟和多种神经、行为及计算数据集上，SRF能够从多种形式的表示数据中恢复可解释的维度，即使是针对高度稀疏采样、不完整的数据。从这些数据集中提取的维度与任务特定模型获得的维度相匹配，能够预测独立的行为属性，改善探索性分析，并且在确认性假设检验方面比直接比较相似性矩阵具有更高的统计效力。综合来看，这些结果确立了SRF作为一种通用方法，在揭示、理解和利用表示背后的维度方面具有广泛的应用前景。

</details>


### [5] [Sentinel: Embodied Cooperative Spatial Reasoning and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26239)
*Xiangye Lin,Hongxin Zhang,Ruxi Deng,Qinhong Zhou,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoSaR框架，结合基础模型的高层通信规划能力与经典空间导航算法的精确性，使多个去中心化具身智能体在城市场景中通过自然语言协作找到安全会合点，并避开动态巡逻哨兵，实验表明该方法在聚集速度、路径长度和安全性上均优于基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有具身多智能体系统缺乏在城市场景下进行协同空间推理与规划的基准和有效方法，去中心化智能体在动态环境约束下难以有效协调。

Method: 提出CoSaR（协同空间推理与规划）框架，融合基础模型（LLM/VLM）的高层通信与规划能力与经典空间导航算法（如A*、势场法）的精确性，使智能体间交换情境更新、推理动态空间约束并协作重规划轨迹。

Result: 在14个城市级场景中测试3-5个智能体，CoSaR在聚集速度、路径长度和安全性上均一致领先基线方法。

Conclusion: 将动态通信与空间推理相结合对于鲁棒的多智能体协作至关重要，本文通过形式化新设置和提供可扩展基准，为推动具身多智能体系统中的协同空间智能奠定了基础。

Original Abstract: In this work, we study Cooperative Spatial Intelligence, the ability of decentralized embodied agents to coordinate effectively under dynamic environmental constraints across city-scale outdoor domains. We introduce Sentinel Challenge, a benchmark where multiple decentralized embodied agents must communicate in natural language to agree on a mutually safe and convenient meeting point within large, city-scale outdoor environments. Each agent must then navigate safely while avoiding dynamic sentinels patrolling the area, using a tool that provides coarse spatial information. To address this, we propose CoSaR (Cooperative Spatial Reasoning and Planning), a framework that bridges the high-level communication and planning abilities of foundation models with the precision of classical spatial navigation algorithms. CoSaR enables agents to exchange situational updates, reason over evolving spatial constraints, and collaboratively replan trajectories. Evaluated across 14 city-level scenes with 3-5 agents, CoSaR consistently leads to faster gathering, shorter path lengths, and improved safety. Our results demonstrate that integrating dynamic communication with spatial reasoning is essential for robust multi-agent cooperation. By formalizing this new setting and providing a scalable benchmark, we aim to build a foundation for advancing cooperative spatial intelligence in embodied multi-agent systems. Code and challenge are available at https://github.com/UMass-Embodied-AGI/Sentinel.

中文摘要翻译: 在这项工作中，我们研究了协同空间智能（Cooperative Spatial Intelligence），即去中心化具身智能体在城市尺度的室外领域中，在动态环境约束下有效协调的能力。我们引入了Sentinel Challenge基准测试，其中多个去中心化具身智能体必须通过自然语言进行通信，以就大型城市级室外环境中的相互安全且便利的会合点达成共识。每个智能体必须安全导航，同时避开在该区域巡逻的动态哨兵，并使用提供粗略空间信息的工具。为解决这一问题，我们提出了CoSaR（协同空间推理与规划）框架，该框架将基础模型的高层通信与规划能力与经典空间导航算法的精确性相衔接。CoSaR使智能体能够交换情境更新、对演化的空间约束进行推理，并协作重规划轨迹。在14个城市级场景中评估3-5个智能体，CoSaR一致地实现了更快的聚集、更短的路径长度和更高的安全性。我们的结果表明，将动态通信与空间推理相结合对于鲁棒的多智能体协作至关重要。通过形式化这一新设置并提供可扩展的基准，我们旨在为推进具身多智能体系统中的协同空间智能奠定基础。代码和挑战可在 https://github.com/UMass-Embodied-AGI/Sentinel 获取。

</details>


### [6] [RoMo: A Large-Scale, Richly Organized Dataset and Semantic Taxonomy for Human Motion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26241)
*Jiahao Zhang,Joseph Liu,Young-Yoon Lee,Seonghyeon Moon,Victor Zordan,Guy Tevet,Karen Liu,Stephen Gould,Oren Jacob,Haomiao Jiang,Mubbasir Kapadia,Yizhak Ben-Shabat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoMo是一个大规模、高质量、经过精心筛选的野外人体运动数据集，通过分类感知过滤管道去除静态和伪影序列，并引入三级语义分类体系实现细粒度评估，训练的模型在保真度和多样性上达到SOTA，同时发布Motion Toolbox以标准化评估流程。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D人体运动数据集存在两难选择：要么是规模小但高质量的运动捕捉数据集，要么是大规模但充满静态或低质量序列的野外数据集，缺乏兼具规模与质量的数据资源。

Method: 提出RoMo数据集，构建分类感知过滤管道（taxonomy-aware filtering pipeline）主动去除静态和伪影序列；为每个序列标注详细描述并组织成新颖的三级语义分类体系（three-level semantic taxonomy）；发布Motion Toolbox标准化度量、数据转换和可视化。

Result: 在RoMo上训练的模型在保真度和多样性上达到最先进水平，并且对复杂、细微的文本提示具有更优的理解能力；三级分类体系实现了细粒度的逐类别评估，揭示了全局指标无法体现的模型优缺点。

Conclusion: RoMo解决了3D人体运动数据集规模与质量不可兼得的矛盾，其分级分类体系和标准化工具箱为可复现、可解释的运动生成研究奠定了基础。

Original Abstract: Success in generative modeling across language, image, and video demonstrates that large, well-curated datasets are the key driver for building capable models. 3D Human motion, however, has lagged behind, constrained by an unsatisfying choice between small, high-fidelity motion capture datasets and large-scale in-the-wild collections dominated by static or low-quality sequences. We introduce RoMo, a rich, large-scale, carefully curated dataset of in-the-wild human motions that resolves these tradeoffs. To ensure quality, we introduce a taxonomy-aware filtering pipeline that aggressively removes static and artifact-prone sequences. Every sequence is annotated with detailed captions and organized by a novel three-level semantic taxonomy. This hierarchical structure enables fine-grained, per-category evaluation, that reveals model strengths and weaknesses obscured by global metrics. We demonstrate that models trained on RoMo achieve state-of-the-art fidelity and diversity while gaining a superior understanding of complex, subtle text prompts. Finally, we release the Motion Toolbox to standardize metrics, data conversion, and visualization, establishing a foundation for reproducible and interpretable motion generation research.

中文摘要翻译: 在语言、图像和视频领域的生成建模成功表明，大规模、精心整理的数据集是构建强大模型的关键驱动力。然而，3D人体运动领域却一直落后，受限于一个不尽人意的选择：要么是小规模高保真的运动捕捉数据集，要么是以静态或低质量序列为主导的大规模野外数据集。我们提出RoMo，一个丰富、大规模、经过精心整理的野外人体运动数据集，解决了上述权衡问题。为确保质量，我们引入了一种分类感知的过滤管道，主动去除静态和易产生伪影的序列。每个序列都标注了详细描述，并按照一种新颖的三级语义分类体系进行组织。这种层次化结构支持细粒度的逐类别评估，揭示了全局指标所掩盖的模型优缺点。我们证明，在RoMo上训练的模型在保真度和多样性上达到了最先进水平，同时对复杂、细微的文本提示获得了更优的理解能力。最后，我们发布Motion Toolbox以标准化度量、数据转换和可视化，为可复现和可解释的运动生成研究建立基础。

</details>


### [7] [LongAV-Compass: Towards Unified Evaluation of Minute-Scale Audio-Visual Generation Across T2AV, I2AV, and V2AV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26244)
*Tengfei Liu,Yang Shi,Xuanyu Zhu,Jiafu Tang,Liu Yang,Qixun Wang,Zhuoran Zhang,Yuqi Tang,Fengxiang Wang,Yuhao Dong,Xinlong Chen,Bozhou Li,Bohan Zeng,Yue Ding,Xiaohan Zhang,Jialu Chen,Haotian Wang,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Leye Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了LongAV-Compass，一个面向分钟级音视频生成任务的系统基准，支持多种输入条件，评估超过20个细粒度维度，涵盖11个模型，填补了长时音视频生成评估的空白。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有音视频生成评估主要局限于5-10秒的短片段，缺乏对长时间（分钟级）生成场景的统一评估，也无法有效衡量身份一致性、叙事连贯性和音视频对齐在长时间跨度下的退化情况。

Method: 构建了包含284个测试用例的基准数据集，覆盖T2AV、I2AV、V2AV三种任务；设计了分类法引导的基准构建流程；提出了融合MLLM辅助评估与DINO-v2、ArcFace、CLIP、ImageBind等感知/多模态指标的统一评估框架，从片段内质量、跨片段一致性、全局叙事连贯性、语义对齐和音视频同步等20+维度进行评估。

Result: 在11个代表性模型上进行了实验，并进行了人类对齐验证，揭示了现有系统在分钟级音视频生成中维持连贯性、语义对齐和时间一致性的局限性。

Conclusion: LongAV-Compass为分析当前系统在多模态输入条件下生成长时、连贯、语义对齐的音视频内容的局限性提供了诊断性测试平台。

Original Abstract: Audio-visual generation is rapidly advancing from short clips to minute-long content, while existing evaluation protocols remain largely confined to short-form settings. Existing benchmarks primarily focus on 5--10 second text-conditioned generation and rarely support unified evaluation across text, image, and video conditioning modalities. Moreover, they provide limited insight into how identity consistency, narrative coherence, and audio-visual alignment degrade over extended temporal horizons. To bridge this gap, we introduce LongAV-Compass, a systematic benchmark for minute-long audio-visual generation. LongAV-Compass contains 284 curated test cases spanning text-to-audio-video (T2AV), image-to-audio-video (I2AV), and video-to-audio-video (V2AV), organized by application scenario and generation complexity. The benchmark combines taxonomy-guided benchmark construction with a unified evaluation framework that integrates MLLM-assisted assessment with complementary perceptual and multimodal metrics, including DINO-v2, ArcFace, CLIP, and ImageBind. The framework evaluates more than 20 fine-grained dimensions covering within-segment quality, cross-segment consistency, global narrative coherence, semantic alignment, and audio-visual synchronization. Through experiments on 11 representative models together with human-alignment validation, LongAV-Compass provides a diagnostic testbed for analyzing the limitations of current systems in sustaining coherent, semantically aligned, and temporally consistent minute-scale audio-visual generation across diverse input modalities.

中文摘要翻译: 音视频生成技术正从短片段快速向分钟级内容演进，然而现有的评估协议仍主要局限于短片段场景。现有基准主要关注5-10秒的文本条件生成，很少支持跨文本、图像和视频条件的统一评估。此外，它们在揭示身份一致性、叙事连贯性和音视频对齐在长时间跨度下的退化方面提供的洞察十分有限。为弥合这一差距，我们引入了LongAV-Compass——一个面向分钟级音视频生成的系统基准。LongAV-Compass包含284个精选测试用例，涵盖文本到音视频（T2AV）、图像到音视频（I2AV）和视频到音视频（V2AV）三类任务，并按应用场景和生成复杂度进行组织。该基准结合了分类法引导的基准构建流程与统一评估框架，该框架集成MLLM辅助评估以及DINO-v2、ArcFace、CLIP和ImageBind等互补性感知与多模态指标。该框架评估超过20个细粒度维度，涵盖片段内质量、跨片段一致性、全局叙事连贯性、语义对齐和音视频同步。通过在11个代表性模型上的实验及人类对齐验证，LongAV-Compass为分析当前系统在跨多种输入模态下生成连贯、语义对齐且时间一致的分钟级音视频内容方面的局限性，提供了一个诊断性测试平台。

</details>


### [8] [Dimensional Distribution Emotion State: Leveraging Valence and Arousal as a Common Embedding Space for Visual Emotion Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26262)
*Émile Bergeron,Tadagbé Dhossou,Sébastien Tremblay,Jean-François Lalonde*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DDES（Dimensional Distribution Emotion State）的连续二维情绪空间表示方法，用于预测艺术作品引发的情绪反应，以辅助博物馆策展人设计基于情绪体验的展览，从而增强观众参与度并实现艺术民主化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 博物馆正探索基于情绪体验的展览方式，但人工标注艺术品情绪内容成本高昂且易引入策展人个人偏见，因此需要开发一种能够自动预测艺术作品所引发情绪反应的工具。

Method: 利用连续二维情绪空间增强情感表示和深度学习模型的训练过程，融合现有类别型和维度型情绪表示的优点，提出了一种新的表示方法——维度分布情绪状态（DDES），并设计了多数据集训练的流程。

Result: DDES相比广泛使用的情绪表示方法具有多重优势，同时保持了相似的基线性能。

Conclusion: DDES是一种有效的情绪表示方法，能够在保持与现有方法相当性能的同时提供额外优势，有潜力辅助博物馆策展人设计基于情绪的艺术展览。

Original Abstract: Museums are important sites for the dissemination of culture and art. They are institutions rooted in history and tradition; their exhibitions are often designed to highlight these aspects. Recently, a new approach is being explored in the field: emotion-based exhibitions. These exhibitions are designed specifically to elicit emotions in the visitors, in order to maximize engagement, and as a way to democratize access to art and attract a wider, more diverse audience. To do so, the emotional content of the artworks must first be extracted, however, manually annotating the artworks by experts is a prohibitively labor-intensive process, and risks introducing the personal bias of curators. To assist the museum curators in their design of these exhibitions, we wish to develop a tool that can predict the emotional response evoked by a work of art. In this article, we leverage a continuous bi-dimensional emotion space to enhance emotion representations and the training process of deep learning models. Drawing inspiration from existing categorical and dimensional emotion representations, we introduce a new representation, Dimensional Distribution Emotion State (DDES), along with a pipeline for multi-dataset training. We show that DDES provides multiple advantages compared to widely used representations while exhibiting similar baseline performance.

中文摘要翻译: 博物馆是传播文化艺术的重要场所。作为植根于历史和传统的机构，其展览设计通常侧重于突出这些方面。近期，该领域正在探索一种新方法：基于情绪体验的展览。这类展览专门设计用于激发观众的情绪，以最大限度地提高参与度，并作为实现艺术民主化的一种方式，吸引更广泛、更多样的观众群体。为此，首先需要提取艺术品的情绪内容，然而，由专家手动标注艺术品是一项劳动密集且成本高昂的过程，并且存在引入策展人个人偏见的风险。为辅助博物馆策展人设计此类展览，我们希望开发一种能够预测艺术作品所引发情绪反应的工具。本文利用连续二维情绪空间来增强情感表示和深度学习模型的训练过程。从现有的类别型和维度型情绪表示中汲取灵感，我们提出了一种新的表示方法——维度分布情绪状态（DDES），并设计了多数据集训练的流程。我们证明，与广泛使用的表示方法相比，DDES在保持相似基线性能的同时具有多重优势。

</details>


### [9] [VesselSim: learning 3D blood vessel segmentation without expert annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26277)
*Erin Rainville,Melissa Ananian,Tristan Mirolla,Hassan Rivax,Yiming Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VesselSim框架，通过合成数据和测试时自适应实现无需真实标注的3D血管分割，在零样本设置下取得与现有基础模型竞争的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 血管标注依赖专家且成本高昂，成为深度学习血管分割技术进展的主要障碍，亟需减少对真实标注数据依赖的方法。

Method: 两阶段框架：(1) 基于随机几何驱动的血管模拟框架，建模递归分支、曲率控制和碰撞感知拓扑，并通过域随机化强度合成生成16,500个解剖学合理的3D血管造影体数据；(2) 仅用合成数据训练3D U-Net，推理时通过自监督掩码重建解码器进行测试时自适应，弥合合成域到真实域的差距。

Result: 在涵盖MR和CT模态、脑部和肾脏等多个解剖区域的多个真实世界数据集上进行零样本评估，性能与最先进的血管分割基础模型相当。

Conclusion: 从合成管状结构中学习血管几何形状能够实现鲁棒的跨域泛化，显著减少对真实医学成像数据和专家标注的依赖。

Original Abstract: Blood vessel segmentation is a core task in medical image analysis for the care of vascular diseases and surgical planning, yet the challenges of providing expert vascular annotations pose a major obstacle for the progress of related deep learning techniques. To address this, we propose VesselSim, a two-stage framework for universal 3D blood vessel segmentation that eliminates the need for real annotated data during training. First, we introduce a stochastic, geometry-driven vascular simulation framework that models recursive branching, curvature-controlled growth, and collision-aware topology, followed by domain-randomized intensity synthesis to generate 16,500 anatomically plausible 3D angiographic volumes. Second, a 3D U-Net is trained solely on this synthetic data. To bridge the domain gap from synthetic to real images at inference time, we introduce a test-time adaptation strategy via a self-supervised mask reconstruction decoder, enabling adaptation to unseen clinical scans without prior domain knowledge. We evaluate VesselSim in a zero-shot setting on multiple real-world datasets spanning MR and CT across several anatomical regions, including the brain and kidneys. Despite being trained exclusively on synthetic data, VesselSim achieves performance competitive with state-of-the-art vascular segmentation foundation models. These findings suggest that learning vessel geometry from synthetic tubular structures is effective for robust cross-domain generalization, substantially reducing the reliance on acquired medical imaging data and more importantly, expert annotations.

中文摘要翻译: 血管分割是医学图像分析中用于血管疾病护理和手术规划的核心任务，然而，提供专家级血管标注的挑战严重阻碍了相关深度学习技术的进展。针对这一问题，我们提出VesselSim，一种用于通用3D血管分割的两阶段框架，在训练过程中无需任何真实标注数据。首先，我们引入一个随机的、几何驱动的血管模拟框架，该框架对递归分支、曲率控制生长和碰撞感知拓扑进行建模，随后通过域随机化强度合成生成16,500个解剖学合理的3D血管造影体数据。其次，仅在此合成数据上训练一个3D U-Net。为了在推理时弥合从合成域到真实图像的域差距，我们引入了一种通过自监督掩码重建解码器实现的测试时自适应策略，使模型能够无需先验域知识即可适应未见过的临床扫描。我们在涵盖MR和CT模态、跨越包括脑部和肾脏在内的多个解剖区域的多个真实世界数据集上，以零样本设置对VesselSim进行评估。尽管仅在合成数据上训练，VesselSim仍取得了与最先进的血管分割基础模型竞争的性能。这些发现表明，从合成管状结构中学习血管几何形状对于实现鲁棒的跨域泛化是有效的，显著减少了对真实医学成像数据以及更重要的专家标注的依赖。

</details>


### [10] [A multifractal-based masked auto-encoder: an application to medical images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26287)
*Joao Batista Florindo,Viviane de Moura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多重分形测度（Renyi熵）优化的掩码自编码器（MO-MAE），通过聚焦高复杂度区域进行掩码，提升医学图像分类性能，在多个数据集上超越基线模型且计算开销极小。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统MAE采用随机掩码策略，可能忽略医学图像中指示疾病的细微关键区域，导致模型无法充分学习诊断相关的特征。

Method: 利用多重分形分析（Renyi熵）量化图像各区域的复杂度和信息量，将掩码过程聚焦于高复杂度区域，迫使模型学习重建最具诊断意义的特征。

Result: 在MedMNIST和COVID-CT等多个医学数据集上，MO-MAE的性能超越了基线模型和当前最先进模型，且计算开销极小。

Conclusion: 多重分形优化的掩码策略增强了模型捕获和重建复杂组织结构的能力，为提升医学图像分析的准确性和效率提供了有前景的方向。

Original Abstract: Masked autoencoders (MAE) have shown great promise in medical image classification. However, the random masking strategy employed by traditional MAEs may overlook critical areas in medical images, where even subtle changes can indicate disease. To address this limitation, we propose a novel approach that utilizes a multifractal measure (Renyi entropy) to optimize the masking strategy. Our method, termed Multifractal-Optimized Masked Autoencoder (MO-MAE), employs a multifractal analysis to identify regions of high complexity and information content. By focusing the masking process on these areas, MO-MAE ensures that the model learns to reconstruct the most diagnostically relevant features. This approach is particularly beneficial for medical imaging, where fine-grained inspection of tissue structures is crucial for accurate diagnosis. We evaluate MO-MAE on several medical datasets covering various diseases, including MedMNIST and COVID-CT. Our results demonstrate that MO-MAE achieves promising performance, surpassing other basiline and state-of-the-art models. The proposed method also adds minimum computational overhead as the computation of the proposed measure is straightforward. Our findings suggest that the multifractal-optimized masking strategy enhances the model's ability to capture and reconstruct complex tissue structures, leading to more accurate and efficient medical image representation. The proposed MO-MAE framework offers a promising direction for improving the accuracy and efficiency of deep learning models in medical image analysis, potentially advancing the field of computer-aided diagnosis.

中文摘要翻译: 掩码自编码器（MAE）在医学图像分类中展现了巨大潜力。然而，传统MAE采用的随机掩码策略可能会忽略医学图像中的关键区域——在这些区域中，即使微小的变化也可能指示疾病。为解决这一局限，我们提出了一种利用多重分形测度（Renyi熵）优化掩码策略的新方法。我们的方法称为多重分形优化掩码自编码器（MO-MAE），通过多重分形分析识别高复杂度和高信息量的区域。通过将掩码过程聚焦于这些区域，MO-MAE确保模型学习重建最具诊断相关性的特征。该方法对医学成像尤为有益，因为精细检查组织结构对于准确诊断至关重要。我们在涵盖多种疾病的多个医学数据集（包括MedMNIST和COVID-CT）上评估了MO-MAE。结果表明，MO-MAE取得了优异的性能，超越了其他基线模型和当前最先进模型。由于所提测度的计算直接简便，该方法仅增加了极小的计算开销。我们的发现表明，多重分形优化的掩码策略增强了模型捕获和重建复杂组织结构的能力，从而实现了更准确高效的医学图像表征。所提出的MO-MAE框架为提升深度学习模型在医学图像分析中的准确性和效率提供了一个有前景的方向，有望推动计算机辅助诊断领域的发展。

</details>


### [11] [Evi-Steer: Learning to Steer Biomedical Vision-Language Models through Efficient and Generalizable Evidential Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26292)
*Taha Koleilat,Hassan Rivaz,Yiming Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Evi-Steer框架，通过证据学习的跨模态低维引导机制，仅更新0.11%参数即可实现BiomedCLIP的不确定性感知参数高效微调，在15个生物医学成像数据集上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉-语言基础模型在生物医学图像上缺乏不确定性感知能力，在域偏移和低数据场景下表现脆弱，临床应用中鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出Evi-Steer框架：(1) 在视觉和文本编码器中进行轻量级低维token更新，同时估计认知不确定性；(2) 不确定性估计更新门控残差，实现弱证据下的保守适应；(3) 基于Dempster-Shafer理论进行跨模态置信度融合，抑制冲突或不确定的跨模态更新。

Result: 在涵盖8个器官、8种成像模态的15个生物医学成像数据集上，Evi-Steer在少样本学习和域泛化设置下均一致优于现有方法。

Conclusion: Evi-Steer提供了一种实用且鲁棒的路径，用于在真实临床环境中部署视觉-语言模型，尤其在低数据场景和域偏移条件下表现出优势。

Original Abstract: Parameter-efficient adaptation of vision-language foundation models is crucial for precise multimodal understanding of biomedical images, yet existing methods remain deterministic and often struggle under domain shift or ambiguous image-text alignment. This limitation is particularly critical in the clinic, where models should remain robust in low-data regimes and domain shifts. We present Evi-Steer, an evidential cross-modal low-dimensional steering framework for BiomedCLIP that enables uncertainty-aware parameter-efficient fine-tuning while updating only 0.11% of total model parameters. Our approach performs lightweight low-dimensional token updates in both vision and text encoders while simultaneously estimating epistemic uncertainty. These uncertainty estimates update gate residuals, allowing the model to adapt conservatively when evidence is weak. Furthermore, we introduce cross-modal confidence fusion based on Dempster-Shafer theory, enabling visual adaptation to be conditioned on textual confidence and suppressing conflicting or uncertain cross-modal updates. We conduct a comprehensive evaluation on 15 biomedical imaging datasets spanning 8 organs and 8 imaging modalities under few-shot learning and domain generalization settings. Evi-Steer consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods under few-shot learning and domain shift settings, demonstrating a practical and robust pathway for deploying vision-language models in real-world clinical settings. Code is available at https://github.com/HealthX-Lab/Evi-Steer.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言基础模型的参数高效适配对于生物医学图像的精准多模态理解至关重要，然而现有方法仍保持确定性，且常难以应对域偏移或模糊的图像-文本对齐。这一限制在临床场景中尤为关键，因为模型应在低数据场景和域偏移中保持鲁棒。我们提出Evi-Steer，一种用于BiomedCLIP的证据驱动的跨模态低维引导框架，能够在仅更新总模型参数0.11%的情况下实现不确定性感知的参数高效微调。我们的方法在视觉和文本编码器中同时执行轻量级低维token更新，并同步估计认知不确定性。这些不确定性估计用于更新门控残差，使模型在证据薄弱时能够进行保守地适应。此外，我们引入了基于Dempster-Shafer理论的跨模态置信度融合，使视觉适应能够以文本置信度为条件，并抑制冲突或不确定的跨模态更新。我们在涵盖8个器官和8种成像模态的15个生物医学成像数据集上，在少样本学习和域泛化设置下进行了全面评估。Evi-Steer在少样本学习和域偏移设置下一致优于现有方法，展示了在真实临床环境中部署视觉-语言模型的实用且鲁棒的路径。代码开源在https://github.com/HealthX-Lab/Evi-Steer。

</details>


### [12] [E$^3$C: Video Generation with 3D Environmental Memory and Ego-Exo Human Pose Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26316)
*Qiao Gu,Lingni Ma,Adam W Harley,Richard Newcombe,Florian Shkurti,Julian Straub*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: E$^3$C是一个可控的自我中心视频扩散框架，通过构建半稠密点云3D记忆解耦场景结构与人类动态，实现物理合理的自我中心视频生成，在Nymeria数据集上显著优于基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自我中心视频生成面临独特挑战：相机与演员紧密耦合导致视角快速变化和频繁自我遮挡；底层动作微妙、关节化且仅部分可见；人物和场景状态需随指定控制信号保持一致演化。现有视频生成方法难以同时满足这些物理合理性要求。

Method: 提出E$^3$C框架：1) 从上下文帧构建半稠密点云3D记忆，并用Video-VAE特征增强每个点；2) 将记忆渲染到目标视角产生对齐条件；3) 通过骨架渲染控制场景中其他人（外部人体控制），通过3D身体关节和6DoF手腕运动控制相机佩戴者（自我人体控制）；4) 引入自我运动编码器，在佩戴者身体部位不可见时生成持久交叉注意力token。

Result: 在Nymeria数据集上，E$^3$C在视觉保真度、相机运动精度、物体一致性以及自我与外部人体控制方面均显著优于强基线方法，同时支持直观的场景编辑。

Conclusion: E$^3$C通过解耦场景结构与人类动态的条件设计，有效解决了自我中心视频生成中的关键挑战，为具身智能体提供了一种物理合理且可控的自我中心视频生成框架。

Original Abstract: Controllable and physically grounded egocentric video generation is essential for embodied agents to reason about how their own and others' actions manifest and change the world. Compared to generic video synthesis, egocentric generation is especially challenging: the camera is tightly coupled to the actor, leading to rapid viewpoint changes and frequent self-occlusions; the underlying actions are subtle, articulated, and often only partially visible; and both the people and the scene state must evolve consistently with the specified controls. We present E$^3$C, a controllable video diffusion framework for egocentric generation that builds structured and compact conditions disentangling persistent scene structure from human-driven dynamics. From context frames, E$^3$C constructs a semi-dense point cloud-based 3D memory and augments each point with appearance descriptors from video-VAE features. Rendering this memory into target viewpoints produces conditioning aligned with the target frames. Human dynamics are modeled separately. The observed people in the scene are controlled by skeleton renderings (exo human control), while the camera wearer is specified by their 3D body joints and 6DoF wrist motion (ego human control). To preserve ego human control when the wearer's body parts are invisible, we introduce an ego motion encoder that produces persistent cross-attention tokens. Experiments on Nymeria show that E$^3$C improves visual fidelity, camera-motion accuracy, object consistency, and ego & exo human control over strong baselines, while also enabling intuitive scene editing.

中文摘要翻译: 可控且物理合理的自我中心视频生成对于具身智能体推理自身及他人动作如何表现和改变世界至关重要。与通用视频合成相比，自我中心生成尤其具有挑战性：相机与演员紧密耦合，导致视角快速变化和频繁的自我遮挡；底层动作微妙、关节化且通常仅部分可见；人物和场景状态必须与指定的控制信号一致演化。我们提出E$^3$C，一种用于自我中心生成的可控视频扩散框架，该框架构建了结构化且紧凑的条件，将持久场景结构与人类驱动的动态解耦。从上下文帧出发，E$^3$C构建了一个基于半稠密点云的3D记忆，并用Video-VAE特征增强每个点的外观描述子。将该记忆渲染到目标视角，产生与目标帧对齐的条件信号。人类动态则被单独建模。场景中观察到的人物通过骨架渲染进行控制（外部人体控制），而相机佩戴者则由其3D身体关节和六自由度手腕运动指定（自我人体控制）。为了在佩戴者身体部位不可见时保持自我人体控制，我们引入了一个自我运动编码器，生成持久的交叉注意力token。在Nymeria上的实验表明，E$^3$C在视觉保真度、相机运动精度、物体一致性以及自我与外部人体控制方面均优于强基线方法，同时支持直观的场景编辑。

</details>


### [13] [RadarSim: Simulating Single-Chip Radar via Multimodal Neural Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26328)
*Chuhan Chen,Tianshu Huang,Akarsh Prabhakara,Chaithanya Kumar Mummadi,Zhongxiao Cong,Anthony Rowe,Matthew O'Toole,Deva Ramanan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一可微分渲染器RadarSim，利用RGB相机的高角分辨率从相机初始化的神经场生成多普勒雷达距离图像，解决雷达空间分辨率低的问题，并展示更清晰的几何和多普勒帧。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 雷达数据难以解释且不同传感器间差异大，现有基于新视角合成的雷达重建方法受限于雷达本身低空间分辨率，需要利用相机高角分辨率来提升雷达重建质量。

Method: 提出RadarSim——一个统一的可微分渲染器，利用RGB相机的高角分辨率，从相机初始化的神经场中生成多普勒雷达距离图像。

Result: 在自建标定雷达-相机数据集上，RadarSim生成的几何和多普勒距离帧比纯雷达重建更清晰。

Conclusion: 利用相机高角分辨率可有效弥补雷达空间分辨率不足，实现更优的雷达数据仿真与重建。

Original Abstract: Radars are an ideal complement to cameras: both are inexpensive, solid-state sensors, with cameras offering fine angular resolution, while radars provide metric depth and robustness under adverse weather. However, radar data is more difficult to interpret than camera images and varies significantly between sensors, necessitating increased reliance on simulation for prototyping sensors and processing pipelines. Recent work treating radar reconstruction as a novel view synthesis problem has shown great promise in reconstructing radar-relevant geometry and simulating low-level radar data. However, such methods are constrained by the low spatial resolution of the underlying radar. To address this, we propose a unified differentiable renderer, RadarSim, which leverages the high angular resolution of RGB cameras to generate Doppler radar range images from a camera-initialized neural field. Using a novel data set of calibrated radar camera recordings from a custom hand-held rig, we demonstrate that RadarSim produces sharper geometry and Doppler range frames than radar-only reconstructions.

中文摘要翻译: 雷达是摄像头的理想补充：两者都是廉价、固态传感器，摄像头提供精细的角分辨率，而雷达提供公尺度深度并在恶劣天气下保持鲁棒性。然而，雷达数据比摄像头图像更难以解释，且不同传感器间差异显著，这增加了对仿真技术的依赖以进行传感器及处理管线的原型设计。近期将雷达重建视为新视图合成问题的工作展示了重建雷达相关几何和仿真低层雷达数据的巨大潜力。然而，这些方法受限于雷达本身低空间分辨率。为解决此问题，我们提出统一可微分渲染器RadarSim，利用RGB相机的高角分辨率，从相机初始化的神经场生成多普勒雷达距离图像。利用自建标定雷达-摄像头手持采集数据集，我们证明RadarSim生成的几何和多普勒距离帧比纯雷达重建更清晰。

</details>


### [14] [Erased but Exploitable: Black-box Embedding-Aware Prompting Against Unlearned Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26332)
*Arian Komaei Koma,Seyed Amir Kasaei,AmirMahdi Sadeghzadeh,Mohammad Hossein Rohban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BEAP，一种黑盒、嵌入感知的对抗性提示攻击方法，利用大语言模型（LLM）迭代生成对抗性提示，以恢复已从文本到图像扩散模型中擦除的概念。BEAP在文本空间中进行嵌入感知搜索，结合多个奖励信号（未学习概念出现、文本-图像对齐、图像质量），在保持提示不被安全过滤器检测的同时生成高质量图像。实验表明，BEAP的攻击成功率（ASR）比先前方法提升超过60%，每次成功攻击平均仅需15个提示。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有针对机器擦除模型的攻击方法威胁模型不现实：白盒攻击需要访问模型权重，黑盒攻击产生的提示杂乱无意义，易被基于规则的简单安全机制检测。本文旨在弥补这一差距，提出更隐蔽、更有效的黑盒攻击方法。

Method: BEAP（Black-box, Embedding-Aware adversarial Prompting）利用LLM迭代生成对抗性提示，执行嵌入感知的文本空间搜索，联合优化三个奖励信号：未学习概念的生成出现率、文本-图像对齐度和生成图像质量，同时确保提示不被安全过滤器检测。

Result: BEAP的攻击成功率（ASR）相比先前方法提升超过60%，每次成功攻击平均仅需15个提示，且生成的提示能够逃脱安全过滤器的检测。

Conclusion: BEAP证明了对机器擦除模型存在更隐蔽、更实用的黑盒攻击路径，突出现有擦除方法在面对嵌入感知的对抗性提示时仍存在安全隐患，需要更强的鲁棒性防御机制。

Original Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove specific concepts from pretrained text-to-image diffusion models, yet several white- and black-box attacks have been introduced to make the model generate such unlearned concepts. These attacks, nevertheless, do not assume a realistic threat model, i.e. they either assume access to the model weights, or result in gibberish adversarial prompts that could be easily detected even through naive rule-based safeguarding. We aim to address this gap in this paper.
  We introduce BEAP, a black-box, embedding-aware adversarial prompting attack that leverages a large language model (LLM) to iteratively generate effective adversarial prompts and exploit such hidden vulnerabilities.
  BEAP performs an embedding-aware search in text space, combining multiple reward signals: unlearned concept presence, text-image alignment, and image quality, to refine generated prompts.
  Unlike previous attack methods, BEAP keeps its prompts undetectable to safety filters while producing high-quality images.
  Extensive experiments show that BEAP improves the Attack Success Rate (ASR) by more than 60% over prior methods, while requiring only an average of fifteen prompts per successful attack. Warning: This paper contains model outputs that may be offensive or upsetting in nature.

中文摘要翻译: 机器擦除（machine unlearning）旨在从预训练的文本到图像扩散模型中移除特定概念，然而已有多种白盒和黑盒攻击被提出，使模型重新生成这些被擦除的概念。然而，这些攻击并未假设现实的威胁模型——它们要么假设能够访问模型权重，要么生成的对抗性提示杂乱无章，即使通过简单的基于规则的防护措施也容易被检测。本文旨在弥补这一差距。我们提出BEAP，一种黑盒、嵌入感知的对抗性提示攻击方法，利用大语言模型（LLM）迭代生成有效的对抗性提示，以利用此类隐藏的脆弱性。BEAP在文本空间中执行嵌入感知搜索，结合多个奖励信号——未学习概念出现率、文本-图像对齐度和图像质量——来优化生成的提示。与以往的攻擊方法不同，BEAP在生成高质量图像的同时，其提示能够保持不被安全过滤器检测。大量实验表明，BEAP的攻击成功率（ASR）相比先前方法提升超过60%，且每次成功攻击平均仅需15个提示。警告：本文包含可能具有冒犯性或引发不适的模型输出。

</details>


### [15] [Personalized Generative Models for Contextual Debiasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26353)
*Xinran Liang,Esin Tureci,Prachi Sinha,Ye Zhu,Vikram V. Ramaswamy,Olga Russakovsky*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DecoupleGen方法，利用文本到图像扩散模型生成包含罕见上下文的图像，作为训练数据增强手段，以缓解模型在常见场景中表现良好但罕见场景中识别能力不足的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现实世界中不同视觉模式出现频率不均，导致训练模型在常见场景中识别效果好，但在罕见场景（如沙滩球出现在路上）中识别能力不足。由于收集真实世界中的罕见图像困难，作者探索是否可以用生成模型生成包含不常见上下文的图像作为训练增强。

Method: 提出DecoupleGen（Decoupling Contextual Patterns with Generations），通过个性化文本到图像扩散模型，在保持与原始数据集分布接近的同时，生成具有罕见上下文的连贯图像。该方法解耦上下文模式，保留原始视觉细节，并应用验证约束确保增强数据的相关性。

Result: 在复杂场景数据集上的目标分类和识别任务中，DecoupleGen相比之前的方法取得了一致的性能提升，分析实验进一步揭示了这些提升背后的关键因素。

Conclusion: 生成具有罕见上下文的图像作为训练增强可以有效缓解模型在常见与罕见场景间的识别能力差异，DecoupleGen方法在保持数据分布一致性的同时成功生成了语义丰富且视觉对齐的图像。

Original Abstract: Different visual patterns appear with different frequencies in the world: e.g., beach balls appear on sand more often than they do on a road. These statistics are reflected in vision datasets, and as a result trained models more easily recognize objects in common scenarios. However, recognizing a beach ball on a road may arguably be even more important than recognizing it on sand. We study how to mitigate this discrepancy. Since collecting uncommon images in the real world may be difficult, we explore whether generating images with less frequent contexts can serve as effective training augmentation. A key challenge is guiding generations to remain close to the original dataset distribution while creating diverse images with uncommon contexts. We introduce Decoupling Contextual Patterns with Generations (DecoupleGen), a method that personalizes text-to-image diffusion models to facilitate coherent synthesis of images with rare contexts while preserving original visual details. The generated images contain semantically meaningful content and remain visually aligned with the original datasets. We further apply verification constraints to ensure relevance of the augmented data. We evaluate our approach on object classification and recognition tasks on complex scene datasets. Our experiments demonstrate consistent improvements over previous approaches, and our analyses identify factors underlying these improvements.

中文摘要翻译: 世界上不同的视觉模式出现的频率各不相同：例如，沙滩球出现在沙滩上的频率远高于出现在道路上。这些统计规律反映在视觉数据集中，导致训练后的模型更容易识别常见场景中的物体。然而，识别道路上的沙滩球可能比识别沙滩上的沙滩球更为重要。本文研究如何缓解这种差异。由于在现实世界中收集不常见的图像可能很困难，我们探索生成具有较少频繁上下文的图像是否能作为有效的训练增强。一个关键挑战是如何在生成具有不常见上下文的多样化图像的同时，使其保持与原始数据集分布接近。我们提出了DecoupleGen（去耦上下文模式的生成方法），该方法个性化文本到图像扩散模型，以促进在保留原始视觉细节的同时合成为具有罕见上下文的连贯图像。生成的图像包含语义上有意义的内容，并与原始数据集在视觉上保持一致。我们进一步应用验证约束以确保增强数据的相关性。我们在复杂场景数据集上的目标分类和识别任务中评估了我们的方法。实验表明，我们的方法相比之前的方法取得了一致的改进，并且我们的分析确定了这些改进背后的因素。

</details>


### [16] [Unified Panoramic Geometry Estimation via Multi-View Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26368)
*Vukasin Bozic,Isidora Slavkovic,Dominik Narnhofer,Nando Metzger,Denis Rozumny,Konrad Schindler,Nikolai Kalischek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PaGeR框架，将预训练的3D透视基础模型扩展到全景域，实现从单张全景图恢复360度场景的深度、法线和天空掩码，达到最先进性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的3D重建基础模型主要针对透视图像设计，无法直接处理全景图像。作者希望将这些强大的透视3D先验知识迁移到全景域，实现从单张全景图进行完整的360度场景几何重建。

Method: 基于预训练的3D重建Transformer，通过最小化架构改动，在训练时混合透视图像和全景图像进行联合训练，使模型保留原有3D先验的同时学会从单张全景图中估计几何一致的360度场景（包括尺度不变深度、度量深度、表面法线和天空掩码）。

Result: 在室内和室外场景的广泛测试中，PaGeR达到了最先进的性能，并且在多种场景下展现出优异的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: PaGeR成功将透视3D基础模型的能力扩展到全景域，能够仅通过单张全景图进行高质量的360度场景几何重建，且无需大规模架构改动。

Original Abstract: Geometry estimation from perspective images has greatly advanced, maturing to the point where off-the-shelf foundation models are able to reconstruct 3D scene structure not only from multi-view imagery, but even from a single view. A natural extension is 3D reconstruction from panoramas, with the exciting prospect of recovering a full 360-degree scene from a single panoramic image. In this work, we introduce PaGeR (Panoramic Geometry Reconstruction), a framework to lift powerful 3D foundation models designed for perspective imagery to the panorama domain. Our strategy is to start from a pre-trained transformer for 3D reconstruction and turn it into a unified high-performance model that predicts scale-invariant depth, metric depth, surface normals, and sky masks from both perspective and omnidirectional images, in a single forward pass. By keeping architectural changes to a minimum and mixing perspective and panoramic images during training, PaGeR retains the rich 3D prior of the underlying foundation model while learning to also estimate geometrically consistent 360-degree scenes from single panoramas. We extensively test our method in both indoor and outdoor environments and find that it delivers state-of-the-art performance and excellent zero-shot performance across a wide range of scenes.

中文摘要翻译: 从透视图像进行几何估计已取得巨大进步，已成熟到现成的基础模型不仅能够从多视图图像、甚至能从单视图重建3D场景结构。一个自然的扩展是全向图的3D重建，其令人兴奋的前景在于从单张全景图像中恢复完整的360度场景。在这项工作中，我们提出了PaGeR（全景几何重建），一个将针对透视图像设计的强大3D基础模型提升到全景域的统一框架。我们的策略是从一个预训练的3D重建Transformer出发，将其转变为一个统一的高性能模型，能够通过单次前向传播从透视图像和全向图像中预测尺度不变深度、度量深度、表面法线和天空掩码。通过将架构改动最小化并在训练时混合透视图像和全景图像，PaGeR在保持底层基础模型丰富3D先验的同时，学会了从单张全景图中估计几何一致的360度场景。我们在室内和室外环境中广泛测试了我们的方法，发现它在各种场景下都达到了最先进的性能和优异的零样本表现。

</details>


### [17] [BioFact-MoE: Biologically Factorized Mixture of Experts for Vision-Language Prognostic Modeling in Hepatocellular Carcinoma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26376)
*Junlin Yang,Tian Yu,Nicha C. Dvornek,Yuexi Du,Peiyu Duan,Annabella Shewarega,Lawrence H. Staib,James S. Duncan,Julius Chapiro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BioFact-MoE框架，通过生物因子化的专家混合模型将肝脏和肿瘤因素显式解耦，在肝癌生存预测中优于所有基线方法，并实现表型感知的风险分层。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有预后视觉-语言模型（VLM）将肝脏和肿瘤相关因素编码到单一纠缠的隐空间中，限制了预测精度和生物学可解释性。

Method: 提出BioFact-MoE，一个生物因子化的混合专家（MoE）框架，通过生物监督的专家模块在残差MoE生存架构中显式分解肝脏和肿瘤因素。

Result: 在N=588的HCC队列（预训练于4,582个3D MRI图像-报告对）上，12、18、24个月AUC分别达75.33%、75.85%、73.96%，均优于所有基线。专家门控权重实现表型感知的风险分层，通路信息门控揭示治疗相关生存异质性。肝脏和肿瘤嵌入分别与肝功能及肿瘤负荷标志物选择性关联（p<0.05）。

Conclusion: 生物因子化的MoE框架能有效提升肝癌生存预测精度与生物学可解释性，为多模态预后建模提供新范式。

Original Abstract: Hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) is biologically heterogeneous, shaped by the interplay between hepatic functional reserve and tumor-related oncologic factors; thus, similar survival outcomes may reflect fundamentally different underlying biological processes. Prognostic modeling in HCC is informed by rich multimodal information from multiparametric MRI and radiology reports from routine clinical practice. Existing prognostic vision-language models (VLMs) learn a single entangled latent representation that blends hepatic and tumor-related factors, limiting both accuracy and biological interpretability. We present BioFact-MoE, a biologically factorized Mixture of Experts (MoE) framework that explicitly decomposes liver and tumor factors via biologically supervised experts within a residual MoE survival architecture. On a HCC cohort of N=588 patients (pretrained on 4,582 3D MRI image-report pairs), BioFact-MoE consistently improves survival prediction over all baselines across time horizons, achieving 12-, 18-, and 24-month AUCs of 75.33%, 75.85%, and 73.96%. Beyond scalar risk prediction, gated expert weights enable phenotype-aware risk stratification. Pathway-informed gating uncovers clinically meaningful treatment-associated survival heterogeneity. In held-out validation, hepatic and tumor embeddings show selective associations with liver function and tumor burden markers, respectively (p<0.05), without supervision. The code is available at https://github.com/jy-639/BioFact-MoE.

中文摘要翻译: 肝细胞癌（HCC）具有生物学异质性，受到肝功能储备和肿瘤相关因素之间相互作用的影响；因此，相似的生存结局可能反映根本不同的潜在生物学过程。HCC的预后建模得益于临床常规实践中多参数MRI和放射学报告提供的丰富多模态信息。现有的预后视觉-语言模型（VLM）学习单个纠缠的隐表示，将肝脏因素与肿瘤相关因素混合在一起，限制了预测精度和生物学可解释性。我们提出BioFact-MoE，一个生物因子化的混合专家（MoE）框架，通过残差MoE生存架构中的生物监督专家模块显式分解肝脏和肿瘤因素。在N=588名患者的HCC队列（预训练于4,582个3D MRI图像-报告对）上，BioFact-MoE在所有时间窗口上均持续改善生存预测，12、18和24个月AUC分别达到75.33%、75.85%和73.96%。除标量风险预测外，门控专家权重实现了表型感知的风险分层。通路信息门控揭示了具有临床意义的治疗相关生存异质性。在留出验证中，肝脏和肿瘤嵌入分别与肝功能标志物和肿瘤负荷标志物选择性关联（p<0.05），无需监督。代码可在https://github.com/jy-639/BioFact-MoE获取。

</details>


### [18] [VisualNeedle: Benchmarking Active Visual Search in Information-Dense Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26380)
*Jingru Chen,Yiming Liu,Mingtao Chen,Sijie Chen,Richeng Xuan,Liang Yang,Zhichao Hu,Fanyang Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出多模态大语言模型在细粒度感知基准上虽取得高分，但并未真正依赖视觉证据，而是利用了语言先验、全局语义和工具捷径。作者提出VisualNeedle基准测试和反事实裁剪-黑屏设置，证明模型在需要定位极小关键区域的细粒度视觉搜索任务中表现极差（最佳仅56.01%），远低于人类准确率（63.00%），揭示了当前MLLMs在主动视觉搜索方面的根本局限。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有细粒度感知基准测试分数虚高，模型通过三种捷径（语言先验、全局语义、工具返回图像的中间视觉证据被破坏后答案几乎不变）来“作弊”而非真正依赖视觉证据。因此需要构建一个能真正测试模型主动视觉搜索能力、避免上述捷径的挑战性基准。

Method: 1) 构建VisualNeedle基准：场景中的关键证据被空间限制在微小区域，无法一眼识别，信息密集且高度细粒度；2) 提出反事实裁剪-黑屏（crop-black）设置：将工具返回的裁剪区域替换为同尺寸黑色图像，检验模型是否真正依赖中间视觉证据；3) 在无工具、标准工具启用、裁剪-黑屏三种设置下评估9个主流MLLMs。

Result: 无工具设置下准确率低于20%；最佳工具启用模型仅56.01%，远低于人类多数投票准确率63.00%；裁剪-黑屏消融实验确认成功依赖于真实的中间视觉证据，而非捷径。

Conclusion: 当前MLLMs在需要主动搜索极小关键区域的细粒度视觉任务中存在根本性局限，仅靠提高输入分辨率或扩大问题池无法引发真正的主动视觉搜索。VisualNeedle基准和裁剪-黑屏设置可为未来研究提供有效评估手段。

Original Abstract: Frontier multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have been reported to achieve over 90% accuracy on fine-grained perception benchmarks. However, such scores do not necessarily imply faithful use of visual evidence. Prior studies have identified three shortcuts that inflate benchmark performance. First, linguistic priors and lexical cues in questions often enable models to infer plausible answers without seeing the image. Second, coarse global semantics from the visual encoder can bypass fine-grained local details. Third, in some ``think-with-images'' benchmarks, corrupting the intermediate images returned by visual tools barely affects the final answer. These findings suggest that higher input resolution or larger question pools alone do not elicit genuine active visual search. To address this, we introduce VisualNeedle, a challenging, information-dense, and fine-grained benchmark for scenes where critical evidence is spatially constrained to minute regions and not discernible at a glance. We further propose a counterfactual crop-black setting, which replaces crops returned by tools with black images of the same size, to test whether tool-enabled performance truly relies on intermediate visual evidence. We evaluate 9 promninent MLLMs across three settings: no-tool, standard tool-enabled, and crop-black. No-tool accuracy stays below 20\%, and the best tool-enabled model reaches only 56.01\%, still trailing the 63.00% human majority-vote accuracy. These results reveal persistent limitations in fine-grained visual search, while the crop-black ablation confirms that success on VisualNeedle hinges on genuine intermediate visual evidence.

中文摘要翻译: 前沿多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在细粒度感知基准测试上已被报道达到超过90%的准确率。然而，这样的分数并不一定意味着对视觉证据的忠实使用。先前的研究已识别出三种夸大基准性能的捷径。第一，问题中的语言先验和词汇线索使模型无需看到图像即可推断出合理的答案。第二，来自视觉编码器的粗粒度全局语义可以绕过细粒度的局部细节。第三，在某些"用图像思考"的基准测试中，破坏视觉工具返回的中间图像几乎不影响最终答案。这些发现表明，仅靠更高的输入分辨率或更大的问题集并不能引发真正的主动视觉搜索。为解决这一问题，我们提出了VisualNeedle——一个具有挑战性、信息密集且细粒度的基准，专门针对关键证据在空间上局限于极小区域且无法一目了然的场景。我们进一步提出了一种反事实裁剪-黑屏设置，将工具返回的裁剪区域替换为同尺寸的黑色图像，以测试基于工具的性能是否真正依赖于中间视觉证据。我们在三种设置下评估了9个主流MLLMs：无工具、标准工具启用和裁剪-黑屏。无工具准确率低于20%，最佳工具启用模型仅达到56.01%，仍落后于63.00%的人类多数投票准确率。这些结果揭示了细粒度视觉搜索中持续存在的局限性，而裁剪-黑屏消融实验证实，在VisualNeedle上的成功确实依赖于真实的中间视觉证据。

</details>


### [19] [Detail Consistent Stage-Wise Distillation for Efficient 3D MRI Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26382)
*Mengchen Fan,Baocheng Geng,Xi Xiao,Tianyang Wang,Siyuan Mei,Pulin Che,Xiaoqian Jiang,Qizhen Lan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为细节一致蒸馏（DCD）的逐阶段知识蒸馏框架，通过在小波域中对齐教师-学生模型的细节分量来保留3D医学图像分割中的细粒度结构特征，在压缩模型的同时不引入推理开销。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 高性能3D医学图像分割器（如nnU-Net）受限于内存占用和推理延迟，需要模型压缩；但压缩后的3D编码器在跨多分辨率阶段反复下采样时会丢失细小病灶和锐利边界等精细结构信息。

Method: 提出DCD（Detail Consistent Distillation）框架，采用逐阶段蒸馏策略，在编码器每个阶段将教师和学生特征变换到小波域，对方向性细节分量进行对齐蒸馏，同时保留粗尺度近似分量不受过多约束，避免对全局语义的过度正则化。

Result: 在BraTS 2024和ISLES 2022基准测试上，使用3D多模态MRI数据取得了优于对比方法的分割性能。

Conclusion: DCD作为一种仅在训练阶段使用的蒸馏方法，能在不增加推理开销的前提下有效保持压缩模型的结构细节表达能力，提升3D医学图像分割性能。

Original Abstract: Deploying high-performing 3D medical image segmenters (e.g., nnU-Net) is often limited by memory footprint and inference latency. Compression is therefore necessary, but compact 3D encoders tend to lose fine structural cues (small lesions and sharp boundaries) as downsampling repeats across multi-resolution stages. We propose Detail Consistent Distillation (DCD), a stage-wise distillation framework that preserves structural detail across scales by aligning teacher-student features in a wavelet-decomposed representation. At each encoder stage, DCD distills directional detail components in the wavelet domain while leaving the coarse approximation comparatively unconstrained, avoiding over-regularization of global semantics. DCD is used only during training and introduces no inference-time overhead. Experiments on the BraTS 2024 and ISLES 2022 benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves superior performance in MRI segmentation using 3D multi-modal data. Code and implementation details for DCD are publicly available at https://github.com/ClinicaAlpha/DCD-3D-MedSeg.

中文摘要翻译: 部署高性能3D医学图像分割器（如nnU-Net）常常受限于内存占用和推理延迟。因此，模型压缩是必要的，但紧凑的3D编码器会随多分辨率阶段的反复下采样而丢失精细的结构线索（细小病灶和锐利边界）。我们提出了细节一致蒸馏（DCD），一种逐阶段蒸馏框架，通过在小波分解表示中对齐教师-学生特征来跨尺度保持结构细节。在每个编码器阶段，DCD在小波域中对方向性细节分量进行蒸馏，同时对粗尺度近似分量保持较少约束，从而避免对全局语义的过度正则化。DCD仅在训练阶段使用，不引入任何推理开销。在BraTS 2024和ISLES 2022基准上的实验表明，我们的方法在使用3D多模态数据的MRI分割中取得了优越的性能。DCD的代码和实现细节公开于https://github.com/ClinicaAlpha/DCD-3D-MedSeg。

</details>


### [20] [Zero-Shot Object Re-Identification in Egocentric Kitchen Videos via Multi-Stage SAM3 Feature Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26383)
*Dmytro Klepachevskyi,Alexander Wong,Sirisha Rambhatla,Yuhao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对自我中心厨房视频中目标重识别（ReID）因视角快速变化、频繁遮挡、场景杂乱及类内外观差异大而导致的困难，研究了零样本方法。评估了五种预训练特征提取器后，发现最佳基线仅达45.3% mAP，进而提出了以SAM3分割为核心的增强型SAM3 ReID流水线（多阶段零样本方法），通过背景抑制、多模型嵌入融合、掩码形状IoU几何一致性和k-倒数重排序，将mAP提升至52.8%（+7.5%）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自我中心厨房视频中物体ReID任务面临视角剧变、频繁遮挡、场景杂乱和类内外观差异大等挑战；物体可能离场再入场，且实例多样性大、标注有限，使得有监督ReID难以扩展，因此亟需可扩展的零样本方法。

Method: 提出增强型SAM3 ReID流水线，一种零样本多阶段方法：(1) 阶段1使用SAM3分割抑制背景杂乱；(2) 阶段2融合SAM3、DINOv2和CLIP的嵌入为单一L2归一化描述子；(3) 阶段3用掩码形状IoU增强余弦相似度以实现几何一致性；(4) 阶段4应用k-倒数重排序。

Result: 五种特征提取器（CLIP、DINOv2、DreamSim、I-JEPA、SAM3）中最佳基线仅达45.3% mAP；所提完整流水线将性能提升至52.8% mAP，提高了7.5%。

Conclusion: 所提出的零样本多阶段流水线能有效提升自我中心厨房视频中目标ReID的性能，但仍表明现有零样本方法在该极具挑战性的任务上表现有限。

Original Abstract: Object re-identification (ReID) in egocentric kitchen videos is challenging due to rapid viewpoint changes, frequent occlusions, cluttered scenes, and large intra-class appearance variations. Objects may leave and re-enter the field of view, and the large diversity of instances with limited annotations makes supervised ReID difficult to scale, motivating zero-shot approaches. We study zero-shot object ReID on the EPIC-Kitchens benchmark, where the goal is to match active food and kitchen-tool instances across frames using only pre-trained visual features. We first evaluate five state-of-the-art feature extractors, including Vision-Language Models (VLMs) - CLIP, DINOv2, DreamSim, I-JEPA, and SAM3 - and show that zero-shot methods fail, with the best baseline achieving only 45.3% mAP. We then propose an Enhanced SAM3 ReID Pipeline, a zero-shot multi-stage method built around SAM3 segmentation as the core component. Stage 1 uses SAM3 to suppress background clutter. Stage 2 fuses embeddings from SAM3, DINOv2, and CLIP into a single L2-normalized descriptor. Stage 3 augments cosine similarity with mask-shape IoU for geometric consistency, and Stage 4 applies k-reciprocal re-ranking. The full pipeline improves performance by 7.5% mAP to 52.8%.

中文摘要翻译: 由于视角快速变化、频繁遮挡、场景杂乱以及较大的类内外观差异，自我中心厨房视频中的目标重识别（ReID）具有挑战性。物体可能离开并重新进入视野，且实例的多样性大、标注有限，使得有监督的ReID难以扩展，这激发了零样本方法的研究。我们在EPIC-Kitchens基准上研究零样本目标ReID，其目标是仅使用预训练的视觉特征来匹配跨帧的活动食物和厨房工具实例。我们首先评估了五种最先进的特征提取器，包括视觉语言模型（VLM）——CLIP、DINOv2、DreamSim、I-JEPA和SAM3，并表明零样本方法失效，最佳基线仅达到45.3% mAP。然后，我们提出了一种增强型SAM3 ReID流水线，这是一种以SAM3分割为核心组件的零样本多阶段方法。阶段1使用SAM3抑制背景杂乱。阶段2将来自SAM3、DINOv2和CLIP的嵌入融合为单个L2归一化描述子。阶段3用掩码形状IoU增强余弦相似度以实现几何一致性，阶段4应用k-倒数重排序。完整流水线将性能提升7.5% mAP，达到52.8%。

</details>


### [21] [OmniGF: A Dual-Branch Vision-Language Framework for Unified Gaze Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26399)
*Qiaomu Miao,Haoyu Wu,Jingyi Xu,Minh Hoai,Dimitris Samaras*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniGF是一个统一视觉-语言框架，采用双分支解码策略（语言分支+连续空间分支）将基础VLM适配到多人注视推理任务中，在空间注视估计、语义注视预测和复杂社会注视推理上均取得SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统注视跟踪模型局限于纯空间定位，缺乏语义推理能力，且多人场景需冗余计算；而VLM虽具备语义推理能力，但离散文本生成在连续空间定位任务上精度有限。现有工作无法同时兼顾高精度空间定位和丰富的语义推理。

Method: 提出OmniGF框架，采用双分支解码策略：(1) 语言分支生成离散推理状态；(2) 连续空间分支利用VLM密集隐藏状态，通过高分辨率注视目标热图监督来突破纯文本坐标生成的空间瓶颈。同时引入头部嵌入（从裁剪头部图像编码）为所有个体提供细粒度外观和朝向线索，实现多人场景的显式建模。

Result: 在多个标准基准数据集上取得了新的最优（SOTA）性能，证明了框架在空间注视估计、语义注视预测和复杂社会注视推理任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: OmniGF通过统一视觉-语言框架有效弥合了VLM在连续空间任务上的精度不足问题，同时保留了强大的语义推理能力，为多人注视推理提供了一种高度可扩展的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Understanding human gaze behavior is essential for complex scene comprehension and human-computer interaction. Traditional gaze following models are typically restricted to pure spatial localization, lacking the high-level capacity to reason about semantic targets or complex social contexts. Furthermore, these models often process individuals sequentially, requiring redundant computations over the same scene image for multi-person inference. While recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) offer the exceptional semantic reasoning needed to address gaze-related semantic tasks, their reliance on discrete text generation inherently limits precision in continuous spatial tasks like gaze localization. To bridge this gap, we propose OmniGF, a unified vision-language framework that adapts foundational VLMs for highly scalable multi-person gaze reasoning. The model adopts a dual-branch decoding strategy: a structured language branch generates discrete reasoning states, while a continuous spatial branch directly taps into the VLM's dense hidden states. Supervising these extracted representations with high-resolution gaze target heatmaps effectively overcomes the spatial bottleneck of text-only coordinate generation. Furthermore, to explicitly ground the model in multi-person scenes, we augment the input with head embeddings encoded from cropped head images, providing fine-grained appearance and orientation cues for all individuals simultaneously. By modeling all individuals and leveraging the strong semantic capability of VLMs, OmniGF seamlessly integrates precise spatial gaze target estimation, semantic gaze prediction, and complex social gaze reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework establishes new state-of-the-art performance across multiple standard benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/cvlab-stonybrook/omnigf.

中文摘要翻译: 理解人类注视行为对于复杂场景理解和人机交互至关重要。传统的注视跟踪模型通常局限于纯空间定位，缺乏对语义目标或复杂社会上下文进行推理的高级能力。此外，这些模型通常逐个体处理，对于多人推理需要在同一场景图像上进行冗余计算。尽管近年来的视觉-语言模型（VLM）提供了解决注视相关语义任务所需的卓越语义推理能力，但它们依赖离散文本生成的方式从根本上限制了在注视定位等连续空间任务中的精度。为弥合这一差距，我们提出OmniGF，一个统一的视觉-语言框架，将基础VLM适配到高度可扩展的多人注视推理任务中。该模型采用双分支解码策略：结构化的语言分支生成离散推理状态，而连续空间分支直接利用VLM的密集隐藏状态。用高分辨率注视目标热图监督这些提取的表示，有效克服了纯文本坐标生成的空间瓶颈。此外，为将模型显式锚定在多人场景中，我们使用从裁剪头部图像编码的头部嵌入来增强输入，同时为所有个体提供细粒度外观和朝向线索。通过对所有个体进行建模并利用VLM的强大语义能力，OmniGF无缝集成了精确的空间注视目标估计、语义注视预测和复杂社会注视推理。大量实验表明，我们的框架在多个标准基准上建立了新的最优性能。代码可在 https://github.com/cvlab-stonybrook/omnigf 获取。

</details>


### [22] [The Rescue Effect: Spatio-Semantic Early Exit Bypasses Quantization Collapse in CLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26415)
*Kahyeon Nam,Hyesong Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对视觉-语言模型在INT8量化下出现的量化诱导表示坍缩（QIRC）问题——即Transformer块中累积的激活噪声扭曲多模态嵌入方向、破坏零样本检索的余弦对齐——本文提出LRA-EE（层级感知表示引导的早退机制），通过空间-语义聚合、多特征门控和层级自适应置信度阈值，在ImageNet-1K零样本分类上相比INT8基线减少13.4% FLOPs并提升+2.44% Top-1准确率，揭示了浅层早退可挽救噪声淹没的正确样本的“救援效应”。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在资源受限硬件上部署视觉-语言模型（如CLIP）需使用INT8量化，但联合嵌入架构在量化后会出现不同于CNN分类器的独特失效模式：跨Transformer块累积的激活噪声扭曲多模态嵌入方向，破坏零样本检索依赖的余弦对齐。作者将其定义为量化诱导表示坍缩（QIRC），并量化发现INT8 CLIP ViT-B/32中逐层噪声信号比从浅层低于10%升至第11层的52%，亟需针对性解决方案。

Method: 提出LRA-EE（Layer-wise Representation-Aware Early Exit）方法，包含三个核心组件：(1) 空间-语义聚合（Spatio-Semantic Aggregation），用全局patch token平均替代浅层不成熟的[CLS] token；(2) 学习型多特征门控，融合置信度、top-2边际和空间激活方差三种特征；(3) 层级自适应置信度阈值，根据每层的信息噪声比（INR）校准退出阈值，在噪声饱和的深层之前提前退出。

Result: 在ImageNet-1K零样本分类上，LRA-EE相比INT8基线降低13.4% FLOPs，Top-1准确率从58.72%提升至61.16%（+2.44%）。四象限分解揭示了“救援效应”：9.5%的样本在浅层早退时分类正确但在全深度被噪声淹没，而仅有7.1%出现相反情况，证明早退机制有效挽救了被深层噪声破坏的正确预测。

Conclusion: 本文系统揭示了量化视觉-语言模型中的表示坍缩问题，并提出LRA-EE早退策略有效缓解了深层噪声对多模态嵌入的破坏。该方法不仅降低了计算开销，还通过避开噪声饱和层提升了零样本分类精度，为在资源受限设备上高效部署量化视觉-语言模型提供了新思路。

Original Abstract: Deploying Vision-Language Models on resource-constrained hardware typically requires INT8 quantization, but in joint-embedding architectures such as CLIP this introduces a failure mode distinct from quantized CNN classifiers: activation noise accumulated across transformer blocks perturbs the direction of the multimodal embedding, eroding the cosine alignment on which zero-shot retrieval depends. We characterize this as Quantization-Induced Representation Collapse (QIRC) and quantify it on INT8 CLIP ViT-B/32, where the layer-wise noise-to-signal ratio grows from below 10% in shallow blocks to 52% at Layer 11. We propose LRA-EE (Layer-wise Representation-Aware Early Exit), which bypasses noise-saturated deep layers via Spatio-Semantic Aggregation (replacing the immature shallow [CLS] with a global patch-token average), a learned multi-feature gate (confidence, top-2 margin, spatial-activation variance), and Layer-adaptive Confidence Thresholding calibrated to each layer's Information-to-Noise Ratio. On ImageNet-1K zero-shot classification, LRA-EE reduces FLOPs by 13.4% and improves Top-1 accuracy by +2.44%p (58.72% -> 61.16%) over the INT8 baseline. A four-quadrant decomposition isolates the Rescue Effect: 9.5% of samples are correctly classified at shallow exits but lost to noise at full depth, against only 7.1% suffering the inverse.

中文摘要翻译: 在资源受限的硬件上部署视觉-语言模型通常需要INT8量化，但在CLIP这类联合嵌入架构中，量化会引入一种不同于量化CNN分类器的独特失效模式：跨Transformer块累积的激活噪声会扰动多模态嵌入的方向，侵蚀零样本检索所依赖的余弦对齐。我们将此现象定义为量化诱导表示坍缩（QIRC），并在INT8 CLIP ViT-B/32上对其进行量化分析，发现逐层噪声信号比从浅层块的低于10%增长至第11层的52%。我们提出LRA-EE（层级感知表示引导的早退机制），通过空间-语义聚合（用全局patch token平均替代不成熟的浅层[CLS]）、学习型多特征门控（置信度、top-2边际、空间激活方差）以及根据每层信息噪声比校准的层级自适应置信度阈值，绕过噪声饱和的深层实现早退。在ImageNet-1K零样本分类任务上，LRA-EE相比INT8基线降低了13.4%的FLOPs，并将Top-1准确率从58.72%提升至61.16%（提升+2.44%）。四象限分解揭示了"救援效应"：9.5%的样本在浅层早退时被正确分类但在全深度被噪声淹没，而仅有7.1%出现相反情况。

</details>


### [23] [HydraPrompt: An Adaptive and Asymmetric Framework of Vision-Language Models for Synthetic Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26421)
*Senyuan Shi,Hao Tan,Zichang Tan,Shuhan Feng,Ajian Liu,Sergio Escalera,Jun Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HydraPrompt非对称提示框架，通过自适应调整类别中心来检测合成图像，在SID基准上达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于CLIP的合成图像检测方法使用静态提示作为真假图像的固定边界，无法适应推理时出现的各类伪造变体。

Method: 提出非对称提示适配器(APA)：真实类别使用单组统一提示锚点，伪造类别使用样本自适应提示捕捉多样线索；并引入条件监督对比学习(CSC)目标，压缩真实表征同时捕获细粒度伪造线索。

Result: 在多个主流SID基准上达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: HydraPrompt通过动态调整类别中心对齐细粒度图像线索，有效提升了合成图像检测的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: The rapid evolution of generative models has precipitated a proliferation of fabricated content, posing significant challenges to existing Synthetic Image Detection (SID) methods. Capitalizing on advancements in vision-language models (e.g., CLIP), recent attempts have leveraged learnable textual prompts to identify synthetic images. However, they still leverage static prompt as a fixed boundary for real and fake images, failing to adapt to the varying types of forgery that emerge during inference. To overcome this issue, we propose **HydraPrompt**, an asymmetric prompting framework that dynamically adjusts the category centers by aligning with fine-grained image cues. Specifically, we propose an Asymmetric Prompt Adapter (**APA**): (1) for authentic category, we introduce a single set of prompts to capture the consistent representative patterns, which serves as a unified anchor for real content. While (2) for fake category, we construct sample-adaptive prompts that specialize in capturing diverse cues from different samples, enabling adaptive modeling of forgery image variations. To increase pronounced discriminability within different synthetic images, we further introduce a Conditional Supervised Contrastive (**CSC**) objective, which compacts the authentic representations while capturing fine-grained forgery clues. Extensive experiments on popular SID benchmarks demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance of our framework.

中文摘要翻译: 生成模型的快速发展导致了伪造内容的激增，对现有的合成图像检测(SID)方法构成了重大挑战。借助视觉-语言模型（如CLIP）的进步，近期研究尝试利用可学习的文本提示来识别合成图像。然而，这些方法仍然使用静态提示作为真实与伪造图像的固定边界，无法适应推理过程中出现的各类伪造变体。为解决这一问题，我们提出了**HydraPrompt**，一种非对称提示框架，通过对齐细粒度图像线索动态调整类别中心。具体而言，我们提出了一种非对称提示适配器(**APA**)：(1) 对于真实类别，我们引入单组提示来捕获一致的典型模式，作为真实内容的统一锚点；而(2) 对于伪造类别，我们构建样本自适应提示，专门捕获不同样本中的多样线索，从而实现对伪造图像变体的自适应建模。为增强不同合成图像间的显著可区分性，我们进一步引入了条件监督对比学习(**CSC**)目标，在压缩真实表征的同时捕获细粒度的伪造线索。在主流SID基准上的大量实验表明，我们的框架达到了最先进的性能。

</details>


### [24] [Rethinking Weakly-supervised Video Temporal Grounding From a Game Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26441)
*Xiang Fang,Zeyu Xiong,Wanlong Fang,Xiaoye Qu,Chen Chen,Jianfeng Dong,Keke Tang,Pan Zhou,Yu Cheng,Daizong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于博弈论视角的弱监督视频时序定位方法，通过多元合作博弈理论建模视频帧与查询词之间的细粒度交互，替代了传统基于候选片段的框架，在Charades-STA和ActivityNet Caption数据集上取得了优异性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有弱监督视频时序定位方法存在两个关键问题：一是粗粒度的跨模态学习仅捕捉全局视频级对齐，忽略了帧与词之间的细粒度一致性；二是复杂候选片段的生成与选择既耗时又严重依赖候选质量。

Method: 首次从博弈论视角处理该任务，将每个视频帧和查询词建模为博弈玩家，利用多元合作博弈理论学习它们对跨模态相似度得分的贡献；通过量化帧-词在联盟内合作的趋势，评估所有不确定但可能的对应关系；最终使用学习到的查询引导的帧级得分进行定位，无需候选片段。该方法实现了多粒度、灵活组合的多级跨模态交互。 

Result: 在Charades-STA和ActivityNet Caption两个数据集上取得了优于现有方法的性能。

Conclusion: 通过博弈论视角有效建模帧-词间细粒度对应关系，替代了传统的候选片段框架，为弱监督视频时序定位提供了新的解决思路。

Original Abstract: This paper addresses the challenging task of weakly-supervised video temporal grounding. Existing approaches are generally based on the moment proposal selection framework that utilizes contrastive learning and reconstruction paradigm for scoring the pre-defined moment proposals. Although they have achieved significant progress, we argue that their current frameworks have overlooked two indispensable issues: 1) Coarse-grained cross-modal learning: previous methods solely capture the global video-level alignment with the query, failing to model the detailed consistency between video frames and query words for accurately grounding the moment boundaries. 2) Complex moment proposals: their performance severely relies on the quality of proposals, which are also time-consuming and complicated for selection. To this end, in this paper, we make the first attempt to tackle this task from a novel game perspective, which effectively learns the uncertain relationship between each vision-language pair with diverse granularity and flexible combination for multi-level cross-modal interaction.Specifically, we creatively model each video frame and query word as game players with multivariate cooperative game theory to learn their contribution to the cross-modal similarity score. By quantifying the trend of frame-word cooperation within a coalition via the game-theoretic interaction, we are able to value all uncertain but possible correspondence between frames and words. Finally, instead of using moment proposals, we utilize the learned query-guided frame-wise scores for better moment localization.Experiments show that our method achieves superior performance on both Charades-STA and ActivityNet Caption datasets.

中文摘要翻译: 本文针对弱监督视频时序定位这一具有挑战性的任务。现有方法通常基于候选片段选择框架，利用对比学习和重建范式对预定义的候选片段进行评分。尽管这些方法取得了显著进展，但我们认为其现有框架忽视了两个关键问题：1）粗粒度的跨模态学习：先前方法仅捕获查询与视频的全局级对齐，未能建模视频帧与查询词之间的细粒度一致性，从而无法准确定位时间边界。2）复杂的候选片段：其性能严重依赖于候选片段的质量，而候选片段的生成与选择既耗时又复杂。为此，本文首次尝试从新颖的博弈论视角处理该任务，通过多粒度与灵活组合的多级跨模态交互，有效学习每个视觉-语言对之间的不确定关系。具体而言，我们创造性地将每个视频帧和查询词建模为博弈玩家，利用多元合作博弈理论学习它们对跨模态相似度得分的贡献。通过博弈论交互量化联盟内帧-词合作的趋势，我们能够评估帧与词之间所有不确定但可能的对应关系。最终，我们不再使用候选片段，而是利用学习到的查询引导的帧级得分进行更精准的时刻定位。实验表明，我们的方法在Charades-STA和ActivityNet Caption数据集上均取得了优越性能。

</details>


### [25] [Underwater360: Reconstructing Underwater Scenes from Panoramic Images with Omnidirectional Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26447)
*Jiangbei Hu,Weichao Song,Shibo Yu,Mohan Wang,Zihan Yi,Rui Wu,Mingkang Xiang,Na Lei,Shengfa Wang,Zhongxuan Luo,Ying He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Underwater360，一个物理信息引导的全景3D高斯泼溅框架，通过球面相机空间的光线投射和基于物理的外观-介质建模，解决水下全景场景重建中的几何畸变和介质退化问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 水下场景重建受吸收、散射等介质效应和相机视野限制的双重挑战，传统3DGS无法处理球面投影畸变和水下介质退化，现有方法缺乏针对全景水下场景的专用解决方案。

Method: 1) 全向高斯泼溅模块：直接在球面相机空间进行光线投射，避免2D投影近似带来的几何畸变；2) 基于物理的外观-介质建模架构：使用姿态条件外观嵌入，显式解耦内在场景辐射与深度依赖的后向散射和衰减；3) 构建包含合成和真实场景的全景水下基准数据集。

Result: 在水下新视角合成和场景外观恢复任务上达到优越性能，在复杂水下环境中实现了更优的渲染质量和跨视角一致性。

Conclusion: Underwater360有效解决了全景水下场景重建中的几何畸变和介质退化问题，为水下沉浸式探索提供了可行的解决方案，代码和数据集已开源。

Original Abstract: Underwater scene reconstruction is essential for immersive exploration of aquatic environments, yet remains challenging due to complex participating-media effects such as absorption and scattering, as well as the limited field of view (FoV) of conventional cameras. Although combining panoramic imaging with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) offers a promising direction for photorealistic underwater rendering, traditional 3DGS struggles with both spherical projection distortion and underwater medium degradation. In this paper, we propose \textbf{Underwater360}, a physics-informed omnidirectional 3DGS framework for underwater panoramic scene reconstruction. First, we introduce an Omnidirectional Gaussian Splatting module that performs ray casting directly in spherical camera space instead of relying on 2D projection approximations, thereby reducing geometric distortions under 360$^\circ$ FoV. Second, we design a physics-based appearance-medium modeling architecture with pose-conditioned appearance embeddings to explicitly decouple intrinsic scene radiance from depth-dependent backscatter and attenuation, enabling physically grounded scene appearance restoration. Finally, we establish a new panoramic underwater benchmark dataset containing both synthetic and real-world scenes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Underwater360 achieves superior performance in underwater novel view synthesis and scene appearance restoration, delivering improved rendering quality and cross-view consistency in complex underwater environments. The code and datasets are released at https://github.com/SwcK423/Underwater360

中文摘要翻译: 水下场景重建对于沉浸式探索水生环境至关重要，但由于复杂的参与性介质效应（如吸收和散射）以及传统相机的有限视野（FoV）而仍然具有挑战性。尽管将全景成像与三维高斯泼溅（3DGS）相结合为逼真的水下渲染提供了一条有前景的方向，但传统的3DGS难以处理球面投影畸变和水下介质退化。在本文中，我们提出了Underwater360，一个物理信息引导的全向3DGS框架，用于水下全景场景重建。首先，我们引入了一个全向高斯泼溅模块，该模块直接在球面相机空间中进行光线投射，而非依赖于二维投影近似，从而减少了360°视野下的几何畸变。其次，我们设计了一种基于物理的外观-介质建模架构，结合姿态条件外观嵌入，显式地将内在场景辐射与深度依赖的后向散射和衰减解耦，从而实现物理可解释的场景外观恢复。最后，我们建立了一个包含合成场景和真实场景的全景水下基准数据集。大量实验表明，Underwater360在水下新视角合成和场景外观恢复方面取得了优越性能，在复杂水下环境中实现了更优的渲染质量和跨视角一致性。代码和数据集已发布在https://github.com/SwcK423/Underwater360。

</details>


### [26] [Cross-scale Aligned Supervision for Training GANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26449)
*Sangeek Hyun,MinKyu Lee,Jae-Pil Heo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出多尺度GAN中标准尺度级对抗监督存在跨尺度轨迹未对齐问题，即各阶段输出可能代表不同样本而非渐进细化。提出CAT（跨尺度对齐Transformer），通过保持判别器尺度级并增加生成器侧的 consistency regularization，使中间输出与最终输出对齐。在ImageNet-256上以60轮训练达到FID-50K为1.56，超越同期GAN和扩散/流模型基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法将多尺度GAN的中间输出解释为从粗到细的层级生成，但作者认为标准尺度级对抗监督并未构建真正的粗细层级：每个中间图像被独立推向对应分辨率的真实分布，但尺度级真实性不能保证各阶段输出代表同一生成样本；且前一阶段输出并未作为后一阶段的显式细化目标，导致后阶段可能转向不同样本而非细化前一输出，即跨尺度轨迹未对齐问题。

Method: 提出CAT（Cross-scale Aligned Transformer），保持判别器尺度级（每个中间输出在各自分辨率下被评估），同时在生成器侧增加简单的 consistency regularization，强制中间输出与最终输出对齐，从而解决跨尺度轨迹未对齐问题。

Result: 在类条件ImageNet-256上，CAT-H/2经过仅60轮训练，单步推理即达到FID-50K为1.56，优于强一步GAN和扩散/流模型基线。

Conclusion: 跨尺度轨迹未对齐是多尺度GAN性能受限的关键问题，所提出的CAT通过生成器侧一致性正则化有效地解决了该问题，取得了显著的生成质量提升。

Original Abstract: Modern GANs often introduce adversarial supervision on intermediate generator outputs and interpret the resulting multi-stage synthesis as coarse-to-fine hierarchical generation. In this work, we challenge this interpretation. We argue that standard scale-wise adversarial supervision does not construct a proper coarse-to-fine hierarchy: each intermediate image is independently pushed toward the real distribution at its own resolution, but this scale-wise realism does not ensure that outputs across stages represent the identical generated sample. Moreover, the scale-specific image produced at each stage is not used as an explicit refinement target for the subsequent stage. Therefore, its adversarial loss can improve a scale-specific output without constraining later stages to preserve the same sample trajectory, allowing them to move toward a different sample rather than refine the previous output. We refer to this problem as a cross-scale trajectory misalignment problem. To resolve it, we propose CAT, a Cross-scale Aligned Transformer for multi-scale adversarial generation. CAT keeps the discriminator scale-wise, so each intermediate output is evaluated at its own resolution, while adding a simple generator-side consistency regularization that aligns intermediate outputs with the final output. On class-conditional ImageNet-256, CAT-H/2 achieves an FID-50K of 1.56 with one-step inference after only 60 training epochs, outperforming strong one-step GAN and diffusion/flow baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 现代GAN通常对生成器的中间输出施加对抗性监督，并将由此产生的多阶段合成解释为从粗到细的层级生成。在这项工作中，我们挑战了这一解释。我们认为标准的尺度级对抗监督并未构建出恰当的从粗到细的层级结构：每个中间图像被独立地推向其自身分辨率下的真实分布，但这种尺度级的真实性并不能确保各阶段的输出代表相同的生成样本。此外，每个阶段产生的特定尺度图像并未作为后续阶段的显式细化目标。因此，其对抗损失可以改善某个特定尺度的输出，但并未约束后续阶段保留相同的样本轨迹，使得它们可能转向不同的样本而非细化前一输出。我们将这一问题称为跨尺度轨迹未对齐问题。为解决该问题，我们提出了CAT——一种用于多尺度对抗生成的跨尺度对齐Transformer。CAT保持判别器的尺度级结构，因此每个中间输出在其各自分辨率下被评估，同时增加了一个简单的生成器侧一致性正则化，将中间输出与最终输出对齐。在类条件ImageNet-256上，CAT-H/2在仅60轮训练后，以单步推理达到了1.56的FID-50K，超越了强一步GAN和扩散/流模型基线。

</details>


### [27] [Sparse-LiDAR Prompting of Monocular Geometry Foundations: An Empirical Study Toward Long-Range Driving Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26456)
*Kai Zheng,Qiang Feng,Xingjian Liu,Wenquan Tan,Yuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SLIM，首次将MoGe-2适配为接受真正稀疏LiDAR输入的深度基础模型，通过部分卷积稀疏编码器和多尺度融合颈，在长距离驾驶场景（100-150米）将绝对相对误差降低约39-51%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有稀疏LiDAR提示的深度基础模型（如PromptDA等）存在两个局限：一是缺乏长距离（50-150米）的系统性分层评估；二是基于视差基础的方法依赖预插值密集先验，尚未探索在点图基础（如MoGe-2）上进行真正稀疏LiDAR注入。

Method: SLIM将MoGe-2的point-map解码器改造为接受真正稀疏LiDAR输入。方法包括：(1) 部分卷积稀疏编码器处理稀疏LiDAR点云；(2) 多尺度融合颈在五个尺度上将LiDAR特征融合到点图解码器中；(3) 密度无关训练（随机注入比例0.005-0.30），使单一模型适应不同输入密度。

Result: 在Virtual KITTI和CARLA上，SLIM在100-150米范围内将MoGe-2基线的绝对相对误差降低约39-51%。六种注入比例的消融实验中，部分卷积注入在所有六种设置下均改善AbsRel和RMSE（Virtual KITTI）；在CARLA上，五种设置改善AbsRel（一种近持平），RMSE在三种设置中提升（最多0.31单位），其余三种最多仅损失0.11单位。

Conclusion: SLIM成功验证了在点图基础模型上进行真正稀疏LiDAR注入的有效性，在长距离驾驶深度估计任务上显著优于基线，且单一模型可适应多种稀疏程度。

Original Abstract: Sparse-LiDAR-prompted depth foundation models (PromptDA, Prior Depth Anything, DMD3C) have shown strong results on indoor scenes or within KITTI's standard 80-meter evaluation cap. However, two limitations remain: (i) systematic distance-stratified evaluation in long-range driving regimes (50-150 m) is largely absent; (ii) prior approaches built on disparity-based foundations rely on pre-interpolated dense priors, leaving truly sparse LiDAR injection on point-map foundations (e.g., MoGe-2, NeurIPS 2025) unexplored. We present SLIM (Sparse-LiDAR Injected Monocular geometry), the first adaptation of MoGe-2 to accept truly sparse LiDAR input. SLIM integrates a partial-convolution sparse encoder with a multi-scale fusion neck that fuses LiDAR features into the point-map decoder at five scales. We adopt density-agnostic training (random injection ratio in [0.005, 0.30]) so a single model serves diverse input densities. On Virtual KITTI and CARLA, SLIM reduces the absolute relative error of the MoGe-2 baseline by approximately 39-51% at 100-150 m. Ablation across six injection ratios shows partial-convolution injection improves both AbsRel and RMSE on Virtual KITTI in all six settings; on CARLA, AbsRel improves in five of six settings (one near-tie at 0.015 differs by 0.0013), and RMSE is comparable across encoders, with partial-convolution improving in three settings (by up to 0.31 unit) and losing by at most 0.11 unit in the other three.

中文摘要翻译: 基于稀疏LiDAR提示的深度基础模型（PromptDA、Prior Depth Anything、DMD3C）在室内场景或KITTI标准80米评估上限内表现优异。然而，存在两个局限：(i) 在长距离驾驶场景（50-150米）中缺乏系统性的分层距离评估；(ii) 基于视差基础的方法依赖于预插值的密集先验，尚未探索在点图基础模型（例如MoGe-2，NeurIPS 2025）上进行真正稀疏LiDAR注入。本文提出SLIM（稀疏LiDAR注入的单目几何），这是首个将MoGe-2适配为接受真正稀疏LiDAR输入的工作。SLIM集成了部分卷积稀疏编码器与多尺度融合颈，在五个尺度上将LiDAR特征融合到点图解码器中。我们采用密度无关训练（随机注入比例在[0.005, 0.30]范围内），使得单一模型能够适应不同的输入密度。在Virtual KITTI和CARLA上，SLIM在100-150米范围内将MoGe-2基线的绝对相对误差降低了约39-51%。跨六种注入比例的消融实验表明，部分卷积注入在Virtual KITTI的所有六种设置下均改善了AbsRel和RMSE；在CARLA上，AbsRel在六种设置中的五种上得到改善（在0.015处为近持平，差异为0.0013），RMSE在不同编码器间具有可比性，部分卷积在三种设置下有所提升（最多提升0.31单位），在其余三种设置中最多损失0.11单位。

</details>


### [28] [AnchorDiff: Training-Free Concept Grounding for MM-DiTs via Anchor-Based Graph Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26460)
*Jian Zhang,Zhijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对多模态扩散Transformer（MM-DiT）在无训练概念定位中存在的"概念泄露"问题（目标响应溢出到非目标对象），提出AnchorDiff方法，通过锚点选择与混合图传播解耦语义定位与结构精炼，有效减少视觉易混淆概念上的重叠激活。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于注意力的无训练定位方法在处理视觉上易混淆的概念时，会产生重叠激活，导致目标响应溢出到非目标对象（即概念泄露），影响定位精度。

Method: 从概念到图像的注意力图中选取高置信度的锚点，将其作为独热种子在基于图像自注意力推导的混合图上传播。混合图利用输出空间相似性实现密集的类内传播，并采用行级注意力门控抑制跨对象连接。

Result: 在ImageNet-Segmentation和PascalVOC上取得强定位性能；在提出的多概念混淆数据集（Multi-Concept Confusion Dataset）上显著减少了概念泄露。

Conclusion: AnchorDiff通过解耦语义定位与结构精炼，有效解决了多模态扩散Transformer中视觉易混淆概念间的泄露问题，是一种有效的无训练定位方法。

Original Abstract: Multi-Modal Diffusion Transformers (MM-DiTs) encode rich representations for training-free concept grounding, but existing attention-based methods often produce overlapping activations on visually confusable concepts, a failure mode we call concept leakage, where target responses spill over to non-target objects. To address this issue, we propose AnchorDiff, a training-free grounding method that decouples semantic localization from structural refinement. AnchorDiff selects a high-confidence anchor from concept-to-image attention map and propagates it as a one-hot seed over a hybrid graph derived from image-to-image self-attention. The graph uses output-space similarity for dense within-object propagation and a row-wise attention gate to suppress cross-object connections. Additionally, we introduce the Multi-Concept Confusion Dataset, which contains images with multiple visually similar concepts and separate masks, enabling explicit evaluation of concept leakage. Experiments show that AnchorDiff achieves strong grounding performance on ImageNet-Segmentation and PascalVOC, while substantially reducing concept leakage on our Multi-Concept Confusion Dataset.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态扩散Transformer（MM-DiT）为无训练的概念定位编码了丰富的表示，但现有的基于注意力的方法在视觉上易混淆的概念上常常产生重叠激活，我们将这种失败模式称为"概念泄露"——目标响应溢出到非目标对象。为解决此问题，我们提出AnchorDiff，一种将语义定位与结构精炼解耦的无训练定位方法。AnchorDiff从概念到图像的注意力图中选取高置信度的锚点，并将其作为独热种子在基于图像自注意力推导的混合图上传播。该混合图利用输出空间相似性实现密集的类内传播，并通过行级注意力门控抑制跨对象连接。此外，我们引入了多概念混淆数据集，该数据集包含多张视觉上相似概念及独立掩码的图像，能够显式评估概念泄露。实验表明，AnchorDiff在ImageNet-Segmentation和PascalVOC上取得了强劲的定位性能，同时在我们提出的多概念混淆数据集上显著减少了概念泄露。

</details>


### [29] [Triadic Dynamics Aware Diffusion Posterior Sampling for Inverse Problems: Optimizing Guidance and Stochasticity Schedules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26470)
*Junseo Bang,Dong Ju Mun,Hoigi Seo,Seongmin Hong,Se Young Chun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了 TriPS 方法，通过优化扩散模型中数据一致性引导、无分类器引导和随机性三个组件的调度策略，显著提升逆问题求解性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法多关注扩散模型逆问题求解的三个组件（DC引导、CFG、随机性）的单独或联合开发，但忽略了三者之间的调度交互，导致启发式固定或部分调整的次优调度方案。

Method: 将后验采样重新表述为时变控制问题，提出 TriPS 方法，采用两种策略优化调度：基于模板的函数先验搜索以获取可靠基线调度，以及基于 GRPO 的强化学习以实现更灵活的时序曲线。调度遵循三元趋势：DC 和随机性尺度递减，CFG 尺度递增。

Result: 实验表明 TriPS 在数据保真度和感知真实性方面均优于现有最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: 三个组件的交互调度对于显著提升逆问题求解性能至关重要，早期激进 CFG 与 DC 引导冲突，而随机性可将轨迹拉回高概率区域；所提 TriPS 方法有效优化了这一调度策略。

Original Abstract: Generative posterior sampling using diffusion models has emerged as a dominant paradigm for solving inverse problems in imaging, which usually consists of three main components: data consistency (DC) guidance, classifier-free guidance (CFG) and stochasticity. While prior arts have focused on how to develop each or all components, less attention has given to how to schedule them, leading to heuristically fixed or partially adjusted suboptimal schedules. In this work, we argue that the interactions among all three components in terms of scheduling are crucial for significantly improved performance in solving inverse problems in imaging. Our analysis shows that aggressive CFG early in sampling conflict with DC guidance, while stochasticity brings the trajectory back to higher-probability regions. Based on these findings, we propose Triadic Dynamics Aware Posterior Sampling (TriPS), which reformulates posterior sampling as a time-varying control problem and optimizes schedules following a triadic trend of decreasing DC and stochasticity scales alongside increasing CFG scale. TriPS achieves this through two strategies: template-based search over functional priors for reliable baseline schedules, and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)-based reinforcement learning for more flexible temporal curves. Experiments demonstrate TriPS outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in data fidelity and perceptual realism.

中文摘要翻译: 使用扩散模型的生成式后验采样已成为解决成像逆问题的主流范式，通常包含三个主要组件：数据一致性（DC）引导、无分类器引导（CFG）和随机性。现有工作侧重于如何开发每个或所有组件，但较少关注如何调度它们，导致采用启发式固定或部分调整的次优调度方案。本文论证了所有三个组件在调度方面的交互对于显著提升成像逆问题求解性能至关重要。我们的分析表明，采样早期激进的 CFG 与 DC 引导存在冲突，而随机性则将轨迹拉回高概率区域。基于这些发现，我们提出三元动态感知后验采样（TriPS），将后验采样重新表述为时变控制问题，并按照 DC 和随机性尺度递减、CFG 尺度递增的三元趋势优化调度。TriPS 通过两种策略实现这一目标：基于模板的函数先验搜索以获得可靠的基线调度，以及基于分组相对策略优化（GRPO）的强化学习以实现更灵活的时序曲线。实验表明，TriPS 在数据保真度和感知真实性方面均优于最先进的基线方法。

</details>


### [30] [Comparative Study of Vision-Based Metric Measurement for Large-Scale Planar Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26475)
*ZhiXin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在真实的库区监控场景下，对比了三种基于视觉的平面度量方法（单目测距、图像拼接鸟瞰变换、双目测距），分析了各自在不同条件下的精度与适用性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在大尺度室外环境中，基于视觉的度量距离和面积测量因远距离感知、相机变焦和不稳定成像条件而面临挑战，亟需系统性地评估不同方法的优劣。

Method: 1) 基于几何的单目测距：从相机几何推导平面定位模型，分析俯仰角影响；2) 图像拼接与鸟瞰变换：用于大区域地图构建；3) 双目测距：使用两个联合标定的单目相机实现远距离测量，无需专用立体硬件。

Result: 单目测距在足够大俯仰角下达到米级精度；双目测距达到分米级精度且对俯仰角变化不敏感；图像拼接在小尺度场景有效，但随场景增大稳定性和可扩展性下降。

Conclusion: 三种方法存在明确的权衡取舍，需根据实际场景大小、精度要求和相机配置选择合适方案。
