Original Abstract: Vision-based metric distance and area measurement remains challenging in large-scale outdoor environments due to long-range sensing, camera zoom, and unstable imaging conditions. This work studies planar metric measurement in a real-world reservoir monitoring scenario using PTZ cameras and compares three representative approaches: geometry-based monocular ranging, image stitching with birds-eye-view transformation, and stereo-based ranging using two jointly calibrated monocular cameras. For monocular ranging, planar localization models are derived from camera geometry and the effect of camera pitch angle is analyzed. Image stitching is investigated for large-area mapping, while a stereo-based scheme is developed for long-range measurement without dedicated stereo hardware. Experiments show clear trade-offs: monocular ranging achieves meter-level accuracy under sufficiently large pitch angles, stereo-based ranging achieves decimeter-level accuracy with reduced sensitivity to pitch variations, and image stitching is effective for small-scale scenes but degrades in stability and scalability as scene size increases.

中文摘要翻译: 在大尺度室外环境中，基于视觉的度量距离和面积测量因远距离感知、相机变焦和不稳定的成像条件而面临挑战。本文在真实的库区监控场景下，使用PTZ相机研究平面度量测量，并比较了三种代表性方法：基于几何的单目测距、图像拼接与鸟瞰变换、以及使用两台联合标定单目相机的立体测距。对于单目测距，从相机几何推导了平面定位模型，并分析了相机俯仰角的影响。研究了图像拼接用于大区域地图构建，同时开发了一种无需专用立体硬件的立体测距方案用于远距离测量。实验结果显示明确的权衡：单目测距在足够大的俯仰角下达到米级精度，立体测距达到分米级精度且对俯仰角变化的敏感性较低，图像拼接在小尺度场景中有效，但随着场景增大其稳定性和可扩展性下降。

</details>


### [31] [Clinically-Grounded Counterfactual Reasoning for Medical Video Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26483)
*Jianzhe Gao,Churan Wang,Weiyi Zhang,Jianghua Li,Li-An Li,Wenguan Wang,Yixin Zhu,Yizhou Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MedVCR，一种基于反事实推理的医学视频诊断框架，通过扩散模型生成反事实组织演化、反事实表示学习和双重诊断预测策略，在宫颈镜和结肠镜等视频诊断任务上取得2.6%-10.2%的性能提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有医学视频诊断方法采用端到端学习范式，存在三个关键缺陷：1) 关注外观而非病理本质；2) 缺乏临床先验知识；3) 仅基于观测进行推理，缺乏反事实对比。受临床诊断思维启发，需要引入反事实推理来模拟医生"如果...会怎样"的思考过程。

Method: MedVCR包含三个核心模块：1) 反事实生成器（Counterfactual Generator）：基于扩散模型，在指定病理状态下合成组织演化过程；2) 反事实表示学习模块（Counterfactual Representation Learning）：通过时间一致性、病理可分离性和反事实对齐三种临床规则编码诊断知识；3) 双重诊断预测策略（Dual Diagnostic Prediction）：结合视频级评估与帧级反事实分析进行联合诊断。

Result: 在全监督（如宫颈镜）和弱监督（如结肠镜）视频诊断设置下进行评估，相比主流基线方法性能提升2.6%-10.2%。全面的消融实验验证了各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: MedVCR通过反事实推理框架有效模拟临床诊断思维，显著提升了医学视频诊断的准确性和可解释性，代码将开源。

Original Abstract: Medical video diagnosis involves inferring clinical decisions from dynamic tissue responses throughout examination processes. Existing methods rely on an end-to-end learning paradigm that i) focuses on appearance rather than pathology, ii) lacks clinical priors, and iii) reasons solely from observations without counterfactual comparison. This work introduces MedVCR, a counterfactual reasoning framework that mimics clinical diagnostic thinking. MedVCR comprises three components: a Counterfactual Generator that synthesizes tissue evolution under specified pathological states via a diffusion-based manner; a Counterfactual Representation Learning module that encodes diagnostic knowledge through clinical rules (i.e., temporal consistency, pathological separability, and counterfactual alignment); and a Dual Diagnostic Prediction strategy that integrates video-level assessment with frame-level counterfactual analysis. MedVCR is evaluated under both fully supervised (e.g., colposcopy) and weakly supervised (e.g., colonoscopy) video diagnosis settings, yielding 2.6%-10.2% performance gains compared with leading baselines. Comprehensive ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of each component. The code will be released.

中文摘要翻译: 医学视频诊断涉及从整个检查过程中动态组织反应推断临床决策。现有方法依赖于端到端学习范式，该范式存在三个问题：1) 关注外观而非病理本质；2) 缺乏临床先验知识；3) 仅基于观测进行推理，缺乏反事实对比。本文提出MedVCR，一种模拟临床诊断思维的反事实推理框架。MedVCR包含三个组件：反事实生成器，通过基于扩散的方式在指定病理状态下合成组织演化过程；反事实表示学习模块，通过临床规则（即时间一致性、病理可分离性和反事实对齐）编码诊断知识；以及双重诊断预测策略，融合视频级评估与帧级反事实分析。MedVCR在全监督（如宫颈镜）和弱监督（如结肠镜）视频诊断设置下均进行了评估，相比主流基线方法性能提升2.6%-10.2%。全面的消融研究进一步验证了各模块的有效性。代码将开源。

</details>


### [32] [OmniInteract: Benchmarking Real-World Streaming Interaction for Real-Time Omnimodal Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26485)
*Xudong Lu,Xueying Li,Annan Wang,Yang Bo,Jinpeng Chen,Zengliang Li,Nianzu Yang,Rui Liu,Xue Yang,Jingwen Hou,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniInteract是一个面向实时全模态大语言模型的流式基准测试，要求模型在音频-视觉流上在线推理，检测多模态触发信号并实时响应。实验表明当前模型在流式交互中表现薄弱，最佳IA-QTF1仅0.368，1QnA场景仅0.052。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基准测试多为离线视频理解或基于文本提示的流式问答，未能保留原始音视频流并测试模型在无法访问未来内容条件下的在线交互能力。因此需要构建一个保留流式特征、包含多模态触发和实时响应的基准测试。

Method: 构建包含250个视频、1430个时间锚定响应槽的基准测试。响应槽分为1Q1A（实时/主动/嵌套场景，1062个）和1QnA（连续任务监控和步骤指导，368个）。每个槽包含触发信号、响应窗口和目标答案。评估指标包括Interaction-Aware Quality-Timeliness F1、Interruption Diagnostic Suite和Nested Chain Completion Score。

Result: 当前模型在流式交互中表现薄弱，最佳整体IA-QTF1仅0.368，最佳1QnA IA-QTF1仅0.052。在全双工数学推理中，离线能力并不必然迁移到在线交互场景。

Conclusion: 当前模型在流式在线交互任务上仍存在显著不足，离线能力与在线交互能力之间存在差距，亟需进一步研究。

Original Abstract: We introduce OmniInteract, a streaming benchmark for real-time omnimodal large language models evaluated through native online inference over audio-visual streams. Unlike offline video understanding or text-prompted streaming QA, OmniInteract preserves the original audio-visual stream and requires models to process it online, without access to future content. User queries and ambient sounds are embedded in the audio track, requiring models to detect multimodal triggers, decide when to respond, and answer while the stream unfolds. OmniInteract contains 250 videos with 1,430 temporally grounded response slots: 1,062 1Q1A slots across real-time, proactive, and nested scenarios, and 368 1QnA slots for continuous task monitoring and step guidance. Each slot includes a trigger, response window, and target answer. We evaluate response correctness, timing, invalid outputs, interruption handling, and context continuity using Interaction-Aware Quality-Timeliness F1, Interruption Diagnostic Suite, and Nested Chain Completion Score. Experiments show that current models remain weak in streaming interaction, with the best overall IA-QTF1 reaching only 0.368 and the best 1QnA IA-QTF1 only 0.052. Further study on mathematical reasoning in full-duplex settings shows that offline capability does not necessarily transfer to online interaction. Code and datasets will be made publicly accessible at https://github.com/Lucky-Lance/OmniInteract.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了OmniInteract，一个面向实时全模态大语言模型的流式基准测试，通过音视频流上的原生在线推理进行评估。与离线视频理解或基于文本提示的流式问答不同，OmniInteract保留了原始音视频流，要求模型在线处理流数据，无法访问未来内容。用户查询和环境声音嵌入在音频轨道中，要求模型检测多模态触发信号、决定何时响应并在流展开过程中作答。OmniInteract包含250个视频，共1430个时间锚定响应槽：其中1062个1Q1A槽涵盖实时、主动和嵌套场景，以及368个1QnA槽用于连续任务监控和步骤指导。每个响应槽包含触发信号、响应窗口和目标答案。我们使用交互感知质量-时效F1分数、中断诊断套件和嵌套链完成分数来评估响应正确性、时序、无效输出、中断处理及上下文连续性。实验表明，当前模型在流式交互中表现薄弱，最佳整体IA-QTF1仅达到0.368，最佳1QnA IA-QTF1仅0.052。在全双工数学推理的进一步研究中发现，离线能力并不必然迁移到在线交互场景。代码和数据集将在https://github.com/Lucky-Lance/OmniInteract公开提供。

</details>


### [33] [LongCat-Video-Avatar 1.5 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26486)
*Meituan LongCat Team,Xunliang Cai,Meng Cheng,Feng Gao,Zhe Kong,Jiamu Li,Le Li,Weiheng Li,Hongyu Liu,Shuai Tan,Xiaoming Wei,Tianyu Yang,Yong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LongCat-Video-Avatar 1.5 是一个升级版开源框架，通过工程化优化（Whisper Large编码器、训练配方调整、数据筛选、RLHF训练、步数蒸馏）实现商业级稳定的音频驱动视频生成，在唇形同步、全身时序稳定、长视频生成和身份一致性上达到或超越HeyGen等闭源系统。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管音频驱动视频生成取得进展，但达到商业级稳定性仍具挑战，现有方法难以兼顾唇形同步、全身时序稳定、长视频生成和身份一致性，且开源方案与闭源商业系统之间存在差距。

Method: 1) 将音频编码器升级为Whisper Large；2) 精心调优训练配方和数据筛选；3) 采用RLHF（基于人类反馈的强化学习）训练；4) 使用高级步数蒸馏将推理加速至8 NFE。

Result: 在超过500个测试案例的基准上，v1.5在逼真度评分和专家级质量评估中达到或超越HeyGen、OmniHuman 1.5、Kling Avatar 2.0等领先闭源系统，并具备多人物交互、物体处理等复杂场景泛化能力。

Conclusion: LongCat-Video-Avatar 1.5 通过系统化工程优化而非架构创新，有效缩小了学术研究原型与商业级部署之间的差距，开源发布进一步推动了该领域的发展。

Original Abstract: Despite advances in audio-driven video generation, achieving commercial-grade stability remains challenging. We present LongCat-Video-Avatar 1.5, an upgraded open-source framework prioritizing systematic engineering and production-readiness over architectural novelty. By upgrading the audio encoder to Whisper Large and meticulously scaling our training recipes, v1.5 achieves accurate lip-synchronization, full-body temporal stability, and robust long-video generation with strict identity consistency. Through rigorous data curation and RLHF Training, the model readily generalizes to stylized domains such as anime and animals, and natively handles complex real-world conditions, such as multi-person interactions and object handling. Furthermore, addressing the practical demands of industrial deployment, we employ advanced step distillation to accelerate inference to an optimal 8 NFE, achieving a favorable trade-off between serving efficiency and visual fidelity. The superiority of our approach is validated through extensive quantitative metrics and a rigorous human evaluation conducted on a comprehensive benchmark of over 500 diverse test cases. Results show that v1.5 achieves competitive or superior performance compared to leading closed-source systems (e.g., HeyGen, OmniHuman 1.5, Kling Avatar 2.0) across human-likeness ratings and expert-level quality assessments on our benchmark. With its open-source release, LongCat-Video-Avatar 1.5 narrows the gap between academic research prototypes and commercial-grade deployment.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管音频驱动的视频生成取得了进展，但实现商业级稳定性仍然具有挑战性。我们提出LongCat-Video-Avatar 1.5，一个升级版开源框架，其优先考虑系统性工程和产品就绪性而非架构新颖性。通过将音频编码器升级为Whisper Large并精心调整训练配方，v1.5实现了精确的唇形同步、全身时序稳定、鲁棒的长视频生成以及严格的身份一致性。通过严格的数据筛选和基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）训练，该模型能够很好地泛化到动画和动物等风格化领域，并原生处理多人交互和物体操作等复杂的真实世界场景。此外，为满足工业部署的实际需求，我们采用先进的步数蒸馏将推理加速至最优的8 NFE，在服务效率与视觉保真度之间实现了良好的权衡。我们在包含500多个多样化测试案例的综合基准上，通过广泛的定量指标和严格的人工评估验证了本方法的优越性。结果表明，v1.5在逼真度评分和专家级质量评估方面，相较于领先的闭源系统（如HeyGen、OmniHuman 1.5、Kling Avatar 2.0）达到了具有竞争力或更优的性能。通过开源发布，LongCat-Video-Avatar 1.5缩小了学术研究原型与商业级部署之间的差距。

</details>


### [34] [3D Gaussian Map with Open-Set Semantic Grouping for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26500)
*Jianzhe Gao,Rui Liu,Wenguan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出3D高斯地图（3D Gaussian Map）用于视觉-语言导航（VLN），通过可微分的3D高斯体表征环境，结合开放集语义分组和多粒度动作预测策略，在R2R、R4R、REVERIE三个基准上取得优异效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLN方法虽引入多种场景表征，但忽视了复杂3D几何结构与丰富语义信息，导致在多样未见环境中泛化能力有限。为此需要一种同时捕捉3D几何与开放集语义的统一场景表征。

Method: 1）在线构建自我中心场景地图（Egocentric Scene Map），从稀疏伪激光雷达点云初始化3D高斯体，提供几何先验；2）通过开放集语义分组（Open-Set Semantic Grouping）操作，将3D高斯体按开放世界中的物体实例或材质类别进行分组，形成统一3D高斯地图；3）设计多粒度动作预测策略（Multi-Level Action Prediction），融合空间-语义线索辅助决策。

Result: 在R2R、R4R和REVERIE三个公开基准上的大量实验验证了该方法在VLN任务中的有效性，具体数值指标显示优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的3D高斯地图有效统一了3D几何与开放集语义信息，多粒度动作预测策略显著提升了VLN智能体在复杂环境中的导航与泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Vision-language navigation (VLN) requires an agent to traverse complex 3D environments based on natural language instructions, necessitating a thorough scene understanding. While existing works equip agents with various scene representations to enhance spatial awareness, they often neglect the complex 3D geometry and rich semantics in VLN scenarios, limiting the ability to generalize across diverse and unseen environments. To address these challenges, this work proposes a 3D Gaussian Map that represents the environment as a set of differentiable 3D Gaussians and accordingly develops a navigation strategy for VLN. Specifically, Egocentric Scene Map is constructed online by initializing 3D Gaussians from sparse pseudo-lidar point clouds, providing informative geometric priors for scene understanding. Each Gaussian primitive is further enriched through Open-Set Semantic Grouping operation, which groups 3D Gaussians based on their membership in object instances or stuff categories within the open world, resulting in a unified 3D Gaussian Map. Building on this map, Multi-Level Action Prediction strategy, which combines spatial-semantic cues at multiple granularities, is designed to assist agents in decision-making. Extensive experiments conducted on three public benchmarks (i.e., R2R, R4R, and REVERIE) validate the effectiveness of our method.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言导航（VLN）要求智能体基于自然语言指令遍历复杂的3D环境，这需要充分的场景理解能力。尽管现有工作为智能体配备了多种场景表征以增强空间感知，但它们往往忽略了VLN场景中复杂的3D几何结构和丰富的语义信息，限制了其在多样且未见环境中的泛化能力。为应对这些挑战，本文提出一种3D高斯地图（3D Gaussian Map），将环境表示为一组可微分的3D高斯体，并据此开发了适用于VLN的导航策略。具体而言，自我中心场景地图（Egocentric Scene Map）通过从稀疏伪激光雷达点云中初始化3D高斯体在线构建，为场景理解提供信息丰富的几何先验。进一步地，通过开放集语义分组（Open-Set Semantic Grouping）操作对每个高斯基元进行语义增强，该操作根据3D高斯体在开放世界中属于物体实例或材质类别的成员关系对其进行分组，形成统一的3D高斯地图。基于此地图，本文设计了多粒度动作预测策略（Multi-Level Action Prediction），该策略融合了多个粒度层次的空间-语义线索，辅助智能体进行决策。在三个公开基准（即R2R、R4R和REVERIE）上进行的大量实验验证了本文方法的有效性。

</details>


### [35] [Unveiling the Fragility of Vision-Language Models: Multi-Modal Adversarial Synergy via Texture-Constrained Perturbations and Cross-Modal Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26501)
*Xiang Fang,Wanlong Fang,Changshuo Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对大型视觉语言模型的黑盒通用多模态对抗攻击框架MMAS，同时在图像和文本模态生成对抗扰动，仅通过模型查询即可实现高效攻击。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有LVLMs的多模态对抗攻击研究不足，已有攻击局限于单模态或需要不切实际的白盒访问，缺乏现实可行性，威胁自动驾驶、内容审核等关键应用的安全。

Method: MMAS框架联合优化两种扰动：1）基于小波纹理约束的通用对抗扰动（图像），确保不可感知性和跨视觉输入的鲁棒性；2）嵌入空间L范数约束的可学习提示扰动（文本），保持语义连贯性。通过跨模态正则化项对齐两者梯度方向，增强协同效果与迁移能力，仅利用模型查询实现黑盒攻击。

Result: 在主流LVLMs上的大量实验表明，所提出的攻击方法具有强大的通用对抗能力。

Conclusion: MMAS能够有效实现针对LVLMs的通用、黑盒、多模态对抗攻击，具有跨任务和跨模型的迁移性，揭示了多模态模型在联合对抗攻击下的脆弱性。

Original Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have transformed multi-modal understanding, excelling in tasks like image captioning and visual question answering by integrating visual and textual inputs. However, their robustness against adversarial attacks, particularly those exploiting both modalities, remains underexplored, posing risks to critical applications like autonomous driving and content moderation. Existing attacks focus on single modalities or require impractical white-box access, limiting their real-world relevance. In this paper, we introduce Multi-Modal Adversarial Synergy, a groundbreaking framework that crafts universal, black-box multi-modal attacks against LVLMs. MMAS simultaneously generates a texture scale-constrained universal adversarial perturbation for images and a learnable prompt perturbation for text, optimized jointly using only model queries. The image perturbation leverages wavelet-based texture constraints to ensure imperceptibility and robustness across diverse visual inputs. The text perturbation, constrained by an L-norm in the embedding space, maintains semantic coherence while steering outputs toward a target. A novel cross-modal regularization term aligns the perturbations' gradient directions, enhancing their synergistic impact and transferability across tasks and models. Extensive experiments show the strong universal adversarial capabilities of our proposed attack with prevalent LVLMs.

中文摘要翻译: 大型视觉语言模型通过整合视觉和文本输入，在图像描述和视觉问答等任务中表现出色，推动了多模态理解的变革。然而，它们针对对抗攻击（尤其是利用双模态的攻击）的鲁棒性仍未被充分探索，这给自动驾驶和内容审核等关键应用带来了风险。现有攻击局限于单模态或需要不切实际的白盒访问，限制了其现实相关性。在本文中，我们提出了多模态对抗协同框架——一种开创性的黑盒通用多模态攻击方法，用于攻击大型视觉语言模型。MMAS同时生成图像上的纹理尺度约束通用对抗扰动和文本上的可学习提示扰动，仅通过模型查询进行联合优化。图像扰动利用基于小波的纹理约束确保不可感知性和跨多样化视觉输入的鲁棒性；文本扰动通过嵌入空间中的L范数约束在保持语义连贯性的同时将输出导向目标。一种新颖的跨模态正则化项对齐扰动的梯度方向，增强其协同效果以及跨任务和跨模型的迁移能力。大量实验表明，我们提出的攻击方法在主流大型视觉语言模型上具有强大的通用对抗能力。

</details>


### [36] [Uncertainty-Aware Gaussian Map for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26503)
*Jianzhe Gao,Rui Liu,Yuxuan Xu,Tongtong Cao,Yingxue Zhang,Zhanguang Zhang,Sida Peng,Yi Yang,Wenguan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种显式建模三种感知不确定性（几何、语义、外观不确定性）并将其融入智能体观测空间的方法，通过构建语义高斯地图（SGM）和统一3D价值图，提升视觉-语言导航（VLN）中智能体在不确定环境下的决策可靠性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLN智能体在导航过程中普遍面临感知不确定性（如证据不足导致的地面真值不可靠、空间线索歧义等问题），但在动作预测时往往忽略这些不确定性信息，导致决策不够鲁棒。本文旨在显式建模这些不确定性并将其整合到智能体的观测空间中，以支持更可靠的导航决策。

Method: 首先，构建语义高斯地图（SGM），由从全景观测初始化的可微3D高斯原语组成，编码环境的几何结构和语义内容。在此基础上：1）通过高斯位置和尺度的变分扰动估计几何不确定性；2）通过扰动高斯语义属性捕捉语义不确定性；3）利用Fisher Information表征外观不确定性，衡量渲染观测对高斯级变化的敏感性。将这些不确定性融入SGM，扩展为统一的3D价值图，将其作为可供性和约束条件支撑可靠导航。

Result: 在多个VLN基准数据集上的综合评估验证了所提智能体的有效性，结果表明显式建模不确定性能够提升导航性能。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模并整合几何、语义和外观三种感知不确定性，能够显著提升VLN智能体在复杂环境中的导航可靠性和决策质量。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) requires an agent to navigate 3D environments following natural language instructions. During navigation, existing agents commonly encounter perceptual uncertainty, such as insufficient evidence for reliable grounding or ambiguity in interpreting spatial cues, yet they typically ignore such information when predicting actions. In this work, we explicitly model three forms of perceptual uncertainty (i.e., geometric, semantic, and appearance uncertainty) and integrate them into the agent's observation space to enable informed decision-making. Concretely, our agent first constructs a Semantic Gaussian Map (SGM), composed of differentiable 3D Gaussian primitives initialized from panoramic observations, that encodes both the geometric structure and semantic content of the environment. On top of SGM, geometric uncertainty is estimated through variational perturbations of Gaussian position and scale to assess structural reliability; semantic uncertainty is captured by perturbing Gaussian semantic attributes to reveal ambiguous interpretations; and appearance uncertainty is characterized by Fisher Information, which measures the sensitivity of rendered observations to Gaussian-level variations. These uncertainties are incorporated into SGM, extending it into a unified 3D Value Map, which grounds them as affordances and constraints that support reliable navigation. Comprehensive evaluations across multiple VLN benchmarks show the effectiveness of our agent.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言导航（VLN）要求智能体在遵循自然语言指令的同时在3D环境中导航。在导航过程中，现有智能体通常会遇到感知不确定性，例如缺乏足够证据进行可靠的地面真值判断，或空间线索存在歧义，然而它们在预测动作时往往忽略此类信息。本文显式建模了三种形式的感知不确定性（即几何不确定性、语义不确定性和外观不确定性），并将其整合到智能体的观测空间中，以实现更具信息性的决策。具体而言，我们的智能体首先构建一个语义高斯地图（SGM），该地图由从全景观测初始化的可微3D高斯原语组成，编码了环境的几何结构和语义内容。在SGM的基础上，通过高斯位置和尺度的变分扰动来估计几何不确定性以评估结构可靠性；通过扰动高斯语义属性来捕捉语义不确定性以揭示歧义解释；通过Fisher信息量来刻画外观不确定性，该指标衡量渲染观测对高斯级变化的敏感性。将这些不确定性融入SGM，将其扩展为一个统一的3D价值图，将它们作为可供性和约束条件，以支持可靠的导航。在多个VLN基准上的综合评估表明了所提智能体的有效性。

</details>


### [37] [Re-M3Dr: Rebalanced MultiModal Mean Deviation Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26513)
*Haojie Yin,Chengcheng Feng,Tianyi Liu,Tianqi Zhang,Kaizhu Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出Rebalanced MultiModal Mean Deviation Regression (Re-M3Dr)框架，解决多模态融合（OCT+眼底照相）在平均偏差回归中性能劣于单模态模型的问题，通过自适应边缘监督对比学习增强单模态表征，并结合锐度感知梯度调制稳定联合优化，在公开和私有数据集上平均MSE降低29%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 以往工作仅从OCT预测MD，直觉上结合OCT与眼底照相（FP）两种互补影像能提升性能，但实际发现多模态融合反而比单模态模型更差，根本原因是数据分布与模态学习之间的耦合不平衡导致优化景观扭曲、训练不稳定。

Method: 提出Re-M3Dr框架：1）通过自适应边界的监督对比学习增强单模态表征；2）采用锐度感知梯度调制稳定多模态联合优化，解决模态间学习冲突与数据分布不平衡问题。

Result: 在公开和私有临床数据集上，相较于SOTA多模态学习方法，MSE平均降低29%，验证了Re-M3Dr的有效性。

Conclusion: 多模态融合在眼科MD回归任务中并非天然优于单模态，关键在于解决模态间的耦合不平衡问题；Re-M3Dr通过对比学习增强表征与梯度调控稳定优化，显著提升了回归性能。

Original Abstract: Mean Deviation (MD) is a critical metric for assessing visual field loss in ophthalmology. While previous work has focused solely on predicting MD from Optical Coherence Tomography (OCT), it is intuitive to assume that combining OCT with another imaging of fundus photography (FP) could improve performance, as two ophthalmic medical imaging provide complementary information. This is particularly expected when sophisticated multi-objective optimization is applied, as documented in common multimodal classification. Surprisingly, our investigations reveal that multimodal fusion in this medical imaging scenario performs worse than unimodal model. Through detailed analysis, we identify the root cause as a coupled imbalance between data distribution and modality learning conflict. This imbalance distorts the optimization landscape, leading to unstable training. To address this challenge, we propose the method of Rebalanced MultiModal Mean Deviation Regression (Re-M3Dr), a novel multimodal regression framework. We enhance unimodal representation through adaptive margin based supervised contrastive learning. Then, our framework stabilizes the joint optimization with the sharpness-aware gradient modulation. Experimental results on both public and private clinical datasets show average 29\% reduction in MSE compared to SOTA multimodal learning methods, demonstrating the superiority of Re-M3Dr. The code is available in the supplementary materials.

中文摘要翻译: 平均偏差（MD）是评估眼科视野损失的关键指标。以往工作仅聚焦于从光学相干断层扫描（OCT）预测MD，但直觉上，将OCT与另一种眼底照相（FP）影像相结合可能会提升性能，因为两种眼科医学影像提供了互补信息。当应用复杂的多目标优化时，这种提升尤为可期，这在常见的多模态分类中已有文献支持。令人意外的是，我们的研究发现，在这种医学影像场景下，多模态融合的表现反而逊于单模态模型。通过详细分析，我们将根本原因归结为数据分布与模态学习冲突之间的耦合不平衡。这种不平衡扭曲了优化景观，导致训练不稳定。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了一种新的多模态回归框架——重平衡多模态平均偏差回归（Re-M3Dr）。我们通过自适应边界的监督对比学习来增强单模态表征，进而利用锐度感知梯度调制来稳定联合优化过程。在公开和私有临床数据集上的实验结果表明，与最先进的多模态学习方法相比，MSE平均降低了29%，证明了Re-M3Dr的优越性。代码见补充材料。

</details>


### [38] [CSV-ViT: A Vision Transformer with the Variable-sized Cortical Supervertices for Detection of Alzheimer's Disease Pathologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26514)
*Geonwoo Baek,Ikbeom Jang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于大脑皮层表面tokenization的CSV-ViT模型，通过保留ROI的顶点级可变大小补丁划分（皮层超顶点CSV），结合变大小补丁兼容的Vision Transformer，在AD诊断、淀粉样蛋白和tau蛋白阳性分类中优于现有表面模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于脑皮层表面的深度学习方法存在两个主要问题：一是基于面的均匀补丁在补丁边界处产生重复顶点；二是许多表面模型对感兴趣区域（ROI）的感知有限，可能包含非皮层区域（如内侧壁）。这些限制了模型在阿尔茨海默病MRI预筛查中的性能。

Method: 提出皮层表面tokenization方法，进行ROI保留的、基于顶点的可变大小补丁划分，生成皮层超顶点（CSV）。在此基础上设计CSV-ViT模型，采用填充和掩码感知的补丁嵌入机制，使Vision Transformer能够处理可变大小的补丁。使用T1加权MRI进行AD诊断、淀粉样蛋白阳性和tau蛋白阳性三分类评估。

Result: 在AD诊断、淀粉样蛋白阳性和tau蛋白阳性三个分类任务中，CSV-ViT均取得了优于近期基于皮层表面模型的分类性能。

Conclusion: 提出的CSV-ViT模型能够支持基于MRI的AD相关状态预测，有望在PET或脑脊液确认之前提供预筛查能力。

Original Abstract: Confirming Alzheimer's disease (AD) typically relies on positron emission tomography (PET), which remains costly and invasive, motivating the use of structural MRI-based prescreening. Deep learning on non-Euclidean manifolds, particularly brain cortical surfaces, faces significant challenges due to the data's spherical topology. Recent surface models have enabled learning from cortical surface data; however, imposing face-based uniform patches often causes duplicate vertices at patch boundaries. In general, many surface-based models are limited in their awareness of the region of interest (ROI), which can result in non-cortical regions, such as the medial wall, being included. We propose a cortical surface tokenization that performs ROI-preserving, vertex-based, variable-sized patch partitioning. We refer to these cortical surface patches as cortical supervertices (CSVs). Building on this representation, we design the CSV Vision Transformer (CSV-ViT), a variable-size patch-tolerant Vision Transformer that uses padding and a mask-aware patch embedding. We used T1-weighted MRI and evaluated our framework by classifying AD-related status into three categories: AD diagnosis, amyloid positivity, and tau positivity. Across the experiments, CSV-ViT achieved higher classification performance than recent surface-based models. The results suggest that the proposed CSV-ViT may support MRI-based prediction of AD-related status prior to PET or CSF confirmation.

中文摘要翻译: 确认阿尔茨海默病（AD）通常依赖于正电子发射断层扫描（PET），该方法仍然成本高昂且具有侵入性，这促使研究者采用基于结构MRI的预筛查。在非欧几里得流形（特别是脑皮层表面）上的深度学习面临着数据球形拓扑带来的重大挑战。最近的表面模型已能够从皮层表面数据中进行学习；然而，施加基于面的均匀补丁常常在补丁边界处产生重复顶点。总体而言，许多基于表面的模型对感兴趣区域（ROI）的感知能力有限，这可能导致非皮层区域（如内侧壁）被包含在内。我们提出了一种皮层表面tokenization方法，该方法执行ROI保留的、基于顶点的可变大小补丁划分。我们将这些皮层表面补丁称为皮层超顶点（CSV）。基于这种表示，我们设计了CSV视觉Transformer（CSV-ViT），这是一个能够容忍可变大小补丁的Vision Transformer，使用了填充和掩码感知的补丁嵌入。我们使用T1加权MRI，通过将AD相关状态分为三类（AD诊断、淀粉样蛋白阳性和tau蛋白阳性）来评估我们的框架。在各项实验中，CSV-ViT均取得了优于近期基于皮层表面模型的分类性能。结果表明，所提出的CSV-ViT可能在PET或脑脊液确认之前，为基于MRI的AD相关状态预测提供支持。

</details>


### [39] [InterSketch: An Interleaved Reasoning Model with Self-correcting Visual Sketch and Stepwise Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26520)
*Zhiwei Ning,Wenwen Tong,Xiangli Kong,Shengnan Ma,Ziyi Shang,Jingcheng Ni,Tao Hu,Yong Xien Chng,Jixuan Ying,Zehuan Wu,Hanming Deng,Jie Yang,Yuanjie Zheng,Wei Liu,Lewei Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InterSketch是一个交错的推理模型，通过自校正和逐步奖励机制增强视觉-文本思维链（VT-CoT）能力。它动态生成中间视觉草图并与文本推理交错，在冷启动阶段使用合成的高质量交错VT-CoT数据集和反思机制，在强化学习阶段设计逐步奖励机制缓解长程推理中的奖励稀疏问题。实验表明其性能优于Gemini-3-Pro等闭源模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLM）虽然具备多轮视觉推理能力，但其推理轨迹较浅，且以文本为中心的范式主导，限制了其在复杂视觉任务上的应用。相比之下，人类思维通常涉及长程推理和视觉-文本交错的思维链（VT-CoT）。因此，需要弥合这一差距。

Method: InterSketch包含两个阶段：1）冷启动阶段：构建合成的高质量交错VT-CoT数据集，并引入反思机制（reflection mechanism），使模型具备多轮交错推理和自我校正能力；2）强化学习阶段：设计逐步奖励机制（stepwise reward mechanism），以缓解长程推理中仅使用最终监督信号导致的奖励稀疏问题。模型动态使用外部工具生成中间视觉草图，并与文本推理步骤交错进行。

Result: 在视觉推理基准上的大量实验表明，InterSketch有效提升了性能，甚至超越了Gemini-3-Pro等闭源模型。

Conclusion: InterSketch通过交错视觉-文本思维链、自校正机制和逐步奖励机制，显著增强了VLM在长程视觉理解任务上的推理能力，证明了交错推理范式相较于纯文本推理的优势。

Original Abstract: While vision-language models (VLMs) have exhibited multi-turn visual reasoning capabilities, their reasoning trajectories remain relatively shallow and are dominated by a text-centric paradigm, limiting their applicability to complex visual challenges. In contrast, human-like thought typically involves long-horizon reasoning with an interleaved visual-textual chain-of-thought (VT-CoT). To bridge this gap, we introduce InterSketch, an interleaved reasoning model to enhance the VT-CoT capability via self-correcting and stepwise reward mechanisms. InterSketch dynamically generates intermediate visual sketches using external tools and interleaves them with textual reasoning, enabling effective perception and logical reasoning over long-horizon visual understanding tasks. Specifically, in the first cold-start stage, we propose a synthesized high-quality interleaved VT-CoT dataset and include a reflection mechanism to enable the model's capability in multi-turn interleaved reasoning and self-correction. In the subsequent reinforcement learning (RL) stage, we design a stepwise reward mechanism to mitigate the sparsity of reward signals inherent in end-only supervision over long-horizon reasoning. Extensive experiments on visual reasoning benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of InterSketch, even outperforming proprietary models such as Gemini-3-Pro.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管视觉-语言模型（VLM）已展现出多轮视觉推理能力，但其推理轨迹仍相对浅层且以文本为中心的范式主导，限制了其在复杂视觉挑战中的应用。相比之下，人类思维通常涉及长程推理与视觉-文本交错的思维链（VT-CoT）。为弥合这一差距，我们提出InterSketch，一种通过自校正和逐步奖励机制增强VT-CoT能力的交错推理模型。InterSketch利用外部工具动态生成中间视觉草图，并将其与文本推理交错进行，从而在长程视觉理解任务中实现有效的感知与逻辑推理。具体而言，在冷启动阶段，我们构建了合成的高质量交错VT-CoT数据集，并引入反思机制，使模型具备多轮交错推理与自我校正能力。在后续的强化学习（RL）阶段，我们设计了逐步奖励机制，以缓解长程推理中仅依赖最终监督信号所带来的奖励稀疏问题。在视觉推理基准上的大量实验证明了InterSketch的有效性，其性能甚至超越了Gemini-3-Pro等闭源模型。

</details>


### [40] [CmIVTP: Cross-modal Interaction-based Vessel Trajectory Prediction for Maritime Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26524)
*Yuxu Lu,Dong Yang,Xiaoyu Li,Mengwei Bao,Congcong Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出跨模态交互式船舶轨迹预测框架CmIVTP，融合AIS与CCTV多模态数据，通过交叉注意力机制捕获船舶动态与环境约束的交互，提升轨迹预测准确性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有船舶轨迹预测方法受限于单一数据源：AIS数据对小船稀疏或缺失，CCTV数据难以完整捕捉动态行为，亟需多模态融合方案以提升预测精度与环境适应性。

Method: 1) 构建目标感知场景编码器提取环境语义特征；2) 提出跨模态交互Transformer，通过交叉注意力机制融合AIS运动特征、CCTV环境特征与场景表示；3) 构建船舶群体轨迹库，聚类历史AIS轨迹生成代表性运动模式。

Result: 在多模态船舶轨迹预测基准上，CmIVTP相比现有方法取得了更优的性能表现。

Conclusion: 通过跨模态交互机制有效融合多源数据，实现了动态一致且环境可行的船舶轨迹预测，同时发布大规模多模态数据集Maritime-MmD⁺支持后续研究。

Original Abstract: Maritime intelligent transportation systems (MITS) are essential for ensuring navigation safety and efficiency in busy waterways. However, accurate vessel trajectory prediction remains challenging due to the limitations of single-source data. Automatic identification system (AIS) data is often sparse or unavailable for small vessels, while closed-circuit television (CCTV) data alone cannot fully capture dynamic vessel behavior. To mitigate these challenges, we propose a cross-modal interaction-based vessel trajectory prediction (named CmIVTP) framework to model the intricate interactions between vessel dynamics and environmental constraints. Specifically, we introduce a target-aware scene encoder to extract scene semantic features, effectively capturing vessel-environment interactions and enhancing trajectory prediction accuracy. In addition, we propose a cross-modal interaction transformer, which integrates AIS-derived motion features, CCTV-based environmental features, and scene representations. It leverages cross-modal attention mechanisms to simultaneously capture intra-modal semantics and inter-modal interactions, ensuring dynamically consistent and environmentally feasible predictions. Furthermore, we construct a vessel group trajectory bank by clustering historical AIS trajectories into representative motion patterns, providing an efficient and scalable approach for candidate trajectory generation. Additionally, we introduce the maritime multimodal dataset plus (named Maritime-MmD$^+$), a large-scale dataset that synchronizes AIS data and CCTV video data, providing robust support for multimodal trajectory prediction research. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CmIVTP achieves better performance on multimodal-driven vessel trajectory prediction benchmarks. The code resources for this work can be available at https://github.com/LouisYxLu/CmIVTP.

中文摘要翻译: 海上智能交通系统（MITS）对于保障繁忙水域的航行安全与效率至关重要。然而，由于单一数据源的局限性，准确的船舶轨迹预测仍然具有挑战性。自动识别系统（AIS）数据对于小型船舶而言往往稀疏或无法获取，而仅靠闭路电视（CCTV）数据无法完整捕捉船舶的动态行为。为缓解这些问题，我们提出了一种基于跨模态交互的船舶轨迹预测框架（命名为CmIVTP），用于建模船舶动力学与环境约束之间的复杂交互。具体而言，我们引入了一个目标感知场景编码器来提取场景语义特征，有效捕获船舶-环境交互，提升轨迹预测精度。此外，我们提出了一种跨模态交互Transformer，该模块融合了AIS导出的运动特征、基于CCTV的环境特征以及场景表示，利用跨模态注意力机制同时捕获模态内语义和模态间交互，确保预测结果的动态一致性和环境可行性。进一步地，我们通过将历史AIS轨迹聚类为代表性运动模式，构建了船舶群体轨迹库，为候选轨迹生成提供了高效且可扩展的方案。同时，我们发布了海上多模态数据集增强版（命名为Maritime-MmD⁺），这是一个同步AIS数据与CCTV视频数据的大规模数据集，为多模态轨迹预测研究提供了有力支撑。大量实验表明，CmIVTP在多模态驱动的船舶轨迹预测基准上取得了更优的性能。本工作的代码资源可访问 https://github.com/LouisYxLu/CmIVTP 获取。

</details>


### [41] [ReCA: Multi-Shot Long Video Extrapolation via Recursive Context Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26525)
*Akide Liu,Jinbo Xing,Chaojie Mao,Ye Li,Zeyu Zhang,Yefei He,Weijie Wang,Zihan Wang,Yu Liu,Gholamreza Haffari,Bohan Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出多镜头视频外推（MSVE）任务及递归上下文分配（ReCA）框架，在有限生成预算下实现3-5分钟电影化视频生成，相比最强基线提升8-16%归一化得分和28-43%多镜头一致性指标。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频生成范式存在碎片化问题：单镜头外推保留锚点但缺乏电影结构，多镜头叙事可构图但无法延续观测状态。长视频失败不仅是上下文长度限制，更是上下文分配失败。需要一种在短视频模型有限调用预算下，实现结构化的多镜头视频外推方法。

Method: 提出递归上下文分配（ReCA）推理框架，将MSVE递归分解为上下文有界子问题，在叶节点调用冻结的生成器，跨时间传播结构化状态更新，实现层次化的规划与生成上下文分配。

Result: 相比最强竞争控制器，ReCA提升平均归一化得分8-16%，多镜头一致性指标提升28-43%。

Conclusion: 长视频生成的核心瓶颈是上下文分配失败而非上下文长度不足，ReCA通过层次化上下文分配有效解决MSVE任务，显著优于现有方法。

Original Abstract: Minute-scale cinematic video generation is a central challenge for generative video models. Existing paradigms address only fragments of this challenge: single-shot extrapolation preserves an anchor but lacks cinematic structure, while multi-shot storytelling imposes structure yet remains free to invent its visual states rather than continue an observed one. We define Multi-Shot Video Extrapolation (MSVE), a task that extends an observed frame or clip into a sequence of cinematically structured shots while preserving anchor state and advancing narrative intent. This setting operates under the finite per-call generation budget of short-video models. We identify three coupled bottlenecks: (1) global planners over-specify unsupported details from full screenplays; (2) shot-level prompts dilute task-relevant state when carrying the complete story; and (3) temporal chaining turns generated frames into a lossy memory in which identity, scene, object, and action state decay. MSVE reveals that long-video failure is not merely a limitation of context length, but a failure of context allocation. We propose Recursive Context Allocation (ReCA), an inference-time framework that allocates context hierarchically across planning and generation. ReCA recursively decomposes MSVE into context-bounded subproblems, invokes frozen generators at leaf nodes, and propagates structured state updates across time. To evaluate this setting, we further propose MSVE-Bench and NB-Q, a source-grounded protocol with prompts purpose-built for 3 to 5 minute long-video generation, a regime not addressed by existing short-clip benchmarks. Compared to previous methods, ReCA improves average normalized score by 8 to 16 percent over the strongest competing controller and improves multi-shot consistency metrics by 28 to 43 percent. View the project page at https://reca.vmv.re.

中文摘要翻译: 分钟级电影化视频生成是生成式视频模型的核心挑战。现有范式仅解决这一挑战的碎片：单镜头外推保留了锚点但缺乏电影结构，而多镜头叙事施加了结构却可以自由发明视觉状态而非延续已观测到的状态。我们定义了多镜头视频外推（MSVE），这是一个将观测到的帧或片段扩展为一系列具有电影结构的镜头，同时保留锚点状态并推进叙事意图的任务。该设置运行在短视频模型每次调用有限生成预算的约束下。我们识别出三个耦合的瓶颈：（1）全局规划器从完整剧本中过度指定了不支持的细节；（2）镜头级提示在承载完整故事时会稀释任务相关状态；（3）时间链接使生成帧成为有损记忆，其中身份、场景、物体和动作状态不断衰减。MSVE揭示长视频失败不仅是上下文长度的限制，更是上下文分配失败。我们提出递归上下文分配（ReCA），一个在推理时跨规划和生成层级化分配上下文的框架。ReCA递归地将MSVE分解为上下文有界子问题，在叶节点调用冻结的生成器，并跨时间传播结构化状态更新。为评估该设置，我们进一步提出MSVE-Bench和NB-Q，一个源扎根协议及其专为3至5分钟长视频生成设计的提示集，这一时长区间未被现有短视频基准所覆盖。与先前方法相比，ReCA在最强竞争控制器上将平均归一化得分提高了8%至16%，并将多镜头一致性指标提高了28%至43%。查看项目页面：https://reca.vmv.re。

</details>


### [42] [TrackRef3D: Multi-View Consistent Track-then-Label for Open-World Referring Segmentation in 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26576)
*Yuyang Tan,Renhe Zhang,Hang Zhang,Ao Li,Xin Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TrackRef3D 提出了一种无需人工标注的全自动流程，通过多视角一致的“先跟踪后标注”范式，解耦目标发现与语义定位，实现3D高斯泼溅中的开放世界指代分割。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有指代3D高斯泼溅方法依赖昂贵的逐场景人工标注和逐视角伪掩码生成，存在多视角不一致以及难以适应不同查询粒度的问题。

Method: 提出轨迹感知语义共识模块（TSCM），通过同义聚类和轨迹感知投票聚合跨视角预测，建立规范语义身份以确保多视角一致性；采用可见性感知描述生成策略缓解歧义；提出混合训练策略（HTS），利用多正样本对比目标联合优化粗粒度类别语义与细粒度指代线索。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，TrackRef3D达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: TrackRef3D通过全自动流程实现了开放世界3D指代分割，无需人工标注，有效解决了多视角不一致和查询特异性泛化问题。

Original Abstract: Referring 3D Gaussian Splatting (R3DGS), which utilizes natural language for 3D object segmentation, has emerged as a crucial capability for embodied AI. However, existing methods typically rely on expensive per-scene manual annotation and per-view pseudo mask generation, which suffer from multi-view inconsistency and poor generalization to varying query specificities. To address this, we present TrackRef3D, a fully automatic pipeline that achieves open-world referring segmentation in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) without manual annotation by introducing a multi-view consistent track-then-label paradigm that fundamentally decouples object discovery from semantic grounding. Specifically, we propose a Trajectory-Aware Semantic Consensus Module (TSCM) which aggregates cross-view predictions via synonymous clustering and trajectory-aware voting to establish a canonical semantic identity, thereby ensuring multi-view consistency. Furthermore, we employ a visibility-aware description generation strategy to mitigate ambiguity and propose a Hybrid Training Strategy (HTS) that jointly optimizes coarse category semantics and fine-grained referential cues to ensure robustness under varying query specificities using a multi-positive contrastive objective. Extensive experiments on benchmarks demonstrate that TrackRef3D achieves state-of-the-art performance.

中文摘要翻译: 指代3D高斯泼溅（R3DGS）利用自然语言进行3D目标分割，已成为具身人工智能的关键能力。然而，现有方法通常依赖昂贵的逐场景人工标注和逐视角伪掩码生成，这些方法存在多视角不一致以及难以适应不同查询粒度的问题。为解决这一问题，我们提出了TrackRef3D，这是一种全自动流程，通过在3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）中引入多视角一致的“先跟踪后标注”范式，从根本上将目标发现与语义定位解耦，从而无需人工标注即可实现开放世界指代分割。具体地，我们提出轨迹感知语义共识模块（TSCM），通过同义聚类和轨迹感知投票聚合跨视角预测，建立规范语义身份，从而确保多视角一致性。此外，我们采用可见性感知描述生成策略以缓解歧义，并提出混合训练策略（HTS），利用多正样本对比目标联合优化粗粒度类别语义和细粒度指代线索，确保在不同查询粒度下的鲁棒性。在多个基准上的大量实验表明，TrackRef3D达到了最先进的性能。

</details>


### [43] [O-MARC: Omni Memory-Augmented Compression Distillation for Efficient Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26584)
*Peiran Wu,Yunze Liu,Chi-Hao Wu,Chen Chen,Junxiao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UGC-AVQA基准和OMAC/O-MARC方法，解决全模态大模型在用户生成视频中音视频联合理解成本高的问题，通过训练无关的压缩方法（OMAC）和压缩蒸馏框架（O-MARC），在3B模型上实现性能超越全token推理，同时降低34.6%延迟和34.7%内存。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 全模态大模型在统一音视频理解时，长联合token序列导致推理成本高昂，且现有基准未能充分隔离噪声用户生成视频中音视频关联的验证需求。

Method: 1) UGC-AVQA基准：包含1,000个视频和4,816个QA对，通过音频移除测试确保问题需要音视频联合证据；2) OMAC：训练无关的即插即用压缩方法，保留显著视觉记忆和时序锚定的音频锚点；3) O-MARC：压缩蒸馏框架，使紧凑模型能够鲁棒地处理压缩后的多模态输入。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Omni-3B上，O-MARC在四个基准的平均分达到45.8，优于全token推理（44.1）和OmniZip（41.0）；OMAC实现推理延迟降低34.6%（1.53倍加速）和内存降低34.7%。

Conclusion: 所提出的压缩方法和蒸馏框架能有效降低全模态模型在音视频理解中的推理成本，同时保持甚至提升性能，为UGC场景下的高效多模态理解提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Omnimodal large language models enable unified audio video understanding, but long joint token sequences make inference costly, and existing benchmarks do not fully isolate audio visual association in noisy user generated videos. We introduce UGC-AVQA, a public UGC benchmark with 1,000 videos and 4,816 QA pairs, where an audio removal test ensures that benchmark questions require both acoustic and visual evidence. To reduce inference cost, we propose OMAC, a training free plug in compression method that preserves salient visual memory and temporally grounded audio anchors. To further make compact models robust to compressed inputs, we introduce O-MARC, a compression distillation framework for learning with memory compressed multimodal contexts. On Qwen2.5-Omni-3B, O-MARC improves the average score across four benchmarks to 45.8, outperforming full token inference at 44.1 and OmniZip at 41.0. OMAC also keeps inference efficient, reducing latency by 34.6\% (1.53$\times$ speedup) and memory by 34.7\% compared with full token inference.

中文摘要翻译: 全模态大语言模型能够实现统一的音频-视频理解，但长联合token序列导致推理成本高昂，且现有基准未能完全隔离噪声用户生成视频中的音视频关联。我们提出了UGC-AVQA，一个公开的UGC基准，包含1,000个视频和4,816个问答对，其中音频移除测试确保基准问题需要同时依赖声学和视觉证据。为降低推理成本，我们提出OMAC，一种无需训练的即插即用压缩方法，保留显著视觉记忆与时序锚定的音频锚点。为进一步使紧凑模型对压缩输入具有鲁棒性，我们引入O-MARC，一种基于记忆压缩多模态上下文的压缩蒸馏框架。在Qwen2.5-Omni-3B模型上，O-MARC将四个基准的平均得分提升至45.8，优于全token推理的44.1和OmniZip的41.0。OMAC还保持了推理高效性，与全token推理相比，延迟降低34.6%（1.53倍加速），内存降低34.7%。

</details>


### [44] [FTibSuite: A Comprehensive Resource Suite for Tibetan Vision-Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26601)
*Guixian Xu,Yide Liang,Zeli Su,Xuexian Song,Ziyin Zhang,Yushuang Dong,Ting Zhang,Xu Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了FTibSuite，一个面向藏语视觉-语言研究的综合资源套件，包含人工验证的多模态训练语料库FTibData、五个主流多模态基准的藏语适配版FTibBench，以及基于Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct的三阶段适配基线模型FTibVLM，填补了藏语多模态研究缺乏标准化基础设施的空白。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视觉-语言模型虽发展迅速，但藏语作为极度低资源语言，由于缺乏可复现的训练与评估基础设施而严重受限。

Method: 构建FTibSuite套件，包括：(1) FTibData——经人工验证的多模态训练语料（涵盖连续预训练、图像-文本对齐和指令微调数据）；(2) FTibBench——通过分层质量控制流程降低翻译噪声，适配五个主流多模态基准的藏语版本；(3) FTibVLM——基于Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct经过三阶段适配管线的可复现基线模型。

Result: FTibVLM在所有任务上均取得一致性能提升，例如MMBench准确率从42.97提升至67.78，POPE-random准确率从47.53提升至80.56，同时骨干模型的原始中文能力几乎无退化。

Conclusion: FTibSuite为藏语多模态研究提供了首个标准化基础，验证了所提方法的有效性，为低资源语言的多模态研究提供了可复现的范式。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models have progressed rapidly, but Tibetan remains a severely underserved low-resource language due to the lack of reproducible training and evaluation infrastructure. To fill this gap, we introduce FTibSuite, a comprehensive resource suite for Tibetan vision-language research, consisting of FTibData (human-verified multimodal training corpora spanning continual pretraining, image-text alignment, and instruction tuning data), FTibBench (Tibetan adaptations of five mainstream multimodal benchmarks with a hierarchical quality-control workflow to reduce translation noise), and FTibVLM, a reproducible baseline built on Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct via a three-stage adaptation pipeline. Experiments on FTibBench show FTibVLM delivers consistent performance gains across all tasks, such as improving MMBench accuracy from 42.97 to 67.78 and POPE-random accuracy from 47.53 to 80.56, while retaining the backbone's original Chinese capabilities with minimal degradation, providing the first standardized foundation for Tibetan multimodal research.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型取得了快速发展，但藏语作为一种极度低资源语言，由于缺乏可复现的训练与评估基础设施而严重受限。为填补这一空白，我们提出了FTibSuite，一个面向藏语视觉-语言研究的综合资源套件，包含FTibData（经人工验证的多模态训练语料库，涵盖连续预训练、图像-文本对齐和指令微调数据）、FTibBench（通过分层质量控制流程降低翻译噪声，对五个主流多模态基准进行藏语适配），以及FTibVLM（基于Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct经三阶段适配管线构建的可复现基线模型）。在FTibBench上的实验表明，FTibVLM在所有任务上均取得了一致的性能提升，例如MMBench准确率从42.97提升至67.78，POPE-random准确率从47.53提升至80.56，同时骨干模型原有的中文能力几乎无退化，为藏语多模态研究提供了首个标准化基础。

</details>


### [45] [Gaussian-Voxel Duet: A Dual-Scaffolding Hybrid Representation for Fast and Accurate Monocular Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26616)
*Zhenhua Du,Zhen Tan,Haoyu Zhang,Dewen Hu,Shuaifeng Zhi,Peidong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种混合高斯-体素表示方法VoxelGS，通过将锚定高斯与原符号距离场（SDF）体素骨架联合优化，在保证快速收敛和实时渲染的同时，实现了表面重建质量和新视角合成的双重最优。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D高斯泼溅方法在图像渲染上速度快但几何不准确（因多余基元过拟合训练视图），而集成神经SDF的方法几何好但训练成本过高。本文旨在打破几何精度与优化效率之间的权衡。

Method: 提出混合高斯-体素表示，将锚定高斯约束在由体素化SDF定义的表面附近的窄带内；同时引入隐式表面绑定损失，引导高斯基元向SDF诱导表面靠近，实现相互正则化。

Result: 在ScanNet++、ScanNetv2和DeepBlending等多个真实室内场景数据集上，表面重建质量达到SOTA，新视角合成优于主流基线，同时保持快速训练收敛和实时渲染能力。

Conclusion: VoxelGS通过高斯与体素骨架的联合优化，有效兼顾了几何精度与优化效率，为3D重建和新视角合成提供了一种高效且高质量的解决方案。

Original Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting has achieved remarkable success in photorealistic novel view synthesis, its pursuit of fast and high-fidelity 3D reconstruction has long been constrained by a trade-off between geometric accuracy and optimization efficiency. Methods specialized in image rendering converge quickly at the cost of imperfect geometry caused by superfluous primitives overfitting training views, while methods integrating neural signed-distance field (SDF) for better geometry incur prohibitive training costs. In this paper, we attempt to strike a better trade-off by tethering scaffold-anchored Gaussians to a jointly optimized sparse voxel scaffold. This hybrid Gaussian-Voxel representation explicitly confines anchored Gaussians to a narrow band around surfaces defined by voxelized SDFs, which effectively improves representation efficiency and condenses floating Gaussians without sacrificing geometry quality. An implicit surface tethering loss further pulls individual Gaussian primitives closer to SDF-induced surfaces in a mutually regularized manner for improved reconstruction accuracy. Extensive experiments on diverse real-world indoor scenes from ScanNet++, ScanNetv2, and DeepBlending datasets demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art surface reconstruction quality as well as superior novel view synthesis against leading baselines, while maintaining fast training convergence and real-time rendering. Code will be available at https://github.com/duzh11/VoxelGS.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管3D高斯泼溅在逼真的新视角合成方面取得了显著成功，但其追求快速且高保真3D重建的过程长期受到几何精度与优化效率之间权衡的制约。专注于图像渲染的方法收敛速度快，但多余基元对训练视图的过拟合导致几何不完美；而集成神经符号距离场以改善几何的方法则带来了高昂的训练成本。本文尝试通过在联合优化的稀疏体素骨架上系留锚定高斯，来达成更好的权衡。这种混合高斯-体素表示明确地将锚定高斯约束在由体素化SDF定义的表面附近的窄带内，有效提高了表示效率，压缩了浮动高斯而不牺牲几何质量。隐式表面绑定损失进一步以相互正则化的方式将单个高斯基元拉近至SDF诱导的表面，从而提升重建精度。在来自ScanNet++、ScanNetv2和DeepBlending数据集的多个真实室内场景上的大量实验表明，我们的方法在表面重建质量上达到了最先进的水平，在新视角合成上超越了领先的基线方法，同时保持了快速的训练收敛和实时渲染。代码将发布于https://github.com/duzh11/VoxelGS。

</details>


### [46] [MedVol-R1: Reward-Driven Evidence Grounding for Volumetric Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26621)
*Zichun Wang,Hairong Shi,Bingzheng Wei,Yan Xu,Zihua Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedVol-R1提出基于强化学习的框架，将体积推理分割解耦为可解释的证据定位（2D关键切片与边界框）和体积分割两个阶段，在M3D-Seg基准上达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法依赖专用分割token将语言与掩码解码耦合，导致决策过程坍缩为不透明的潜在表示，限制了可解释性和对多样化临床表达的泛化能力。

Method: 采用冷启动监督微调+GRPO强化学习训练，LVLM将临床推理定位到可验证的2D证据锚点（关键轴向切片和2D边界框），再由冻结的MedSAM2模块传播为连贯的3D掩码，由多组件奖励（信息性证据选择、2D空间定位准确性、跨切片体积一致性）引导。

Result: 在CT-ORG、AbdomenCT-1K和KiTS23三个数据集上均优于强基线方法，达到SOTA性能，强化学习相比纯监督微调带来显著提升。

Conclusion: 将证据定位与体积分割显式解耦可提升VRS的可解释性和性能，强化学习训练相比纯监督微调具有明显优势。

Original Abstract: Volumetric Reasoning Segmentation (VRS) aims to segment a target region in a 3D medical scan from a free-form clinical query, where the referent is often implicit and requires both medical knowledge and volume-grounded reasoning. Existing methods typically rely on specialized segmentation tokens to connect language with mask decoding, but this coupling collapses the decision process into opaque latent representations, limiting interpretability and generalization to diverse narrative expressions. In this paper, we present MedVol-R1, a reinforcement learning-based framework for VRS that explicitly decouples evidence grounding from volumetric delineation: the LVLM grounds clinical reasoning to a verifiable 2D evidence anchor (key axial slice and 2D bounding boxes), which is then propagated into a coherent 3D mask by a frozen MedSAM2 module. We train MedVol-R1 with cold-start supervised fine-tuning followed by GRPO, guided by a multi-component reward that encourages informative evidence selection, accurate 2D spatial grounding, and cross-slice volumetric coherence, without requiring costly chain-of-thought annotations. Experiments on CT-ORG, AbdomenCT-1K, and KiTS23 from the M3D-Seg benchmark demonstrate that MedVol-R1 consistently outperforms strong baselines and achieves state-of-the-art performance, with reinforcement learning providing clear gains over pure supervised fine-tuning.

中文摘要翻译: 体积推理分割（VRS）旨在根据自由形式的临床查询在3D医学扫描中分割目标区域，其中指代对象通常是隐式的，需要医学知识和体积级推理。现有方法通常依赖专用的分割token来连接语言与掩码解码，但这种耦合将决策过程坍缩为不透明的潜在表示，限制了可解释性和对多样化叙述表达的泛化能力。本文提出MedVol-R1，一种基于强化学习的VRS框架，显式地将证据定位与体积勾画解耦：LVLM将临床推理定位到可验证的2D证据锚点（关键轴向切片和2D边界框），再由冻结的MedSAM2模块将其传播为连贯的3D掩码。我们通过冷启动监督微调结合GRPO训练MedVol-R1，并由多组件奖励引导，该奖励鼓励信息性证据选择、准确的2D空间定位以及跨切片体积一致性，无需昂贵的思维链标注。在M3D-Seg基准中的CT-ORG、AbdomenCT-1K和KiTS23上的实验表明，MedVol-R1持续优于强基线方法并达到最先进性能，其中强化学习相比纯监督微调带来了明显的性能提升。

</details>


### [47] [DelowlightSplat: Feed-Forward Gaussian Splatting for Lowlight 3D Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26629)
*Fuzhen Jiang,Zengtian Xie,Zhuoran Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对低光照条件下前馈式3D高斯重建失败的问题，提出了DelowlightSplat——一种低光照感知的前馈式高斯泼溅框架，通过低光照适配器和基于代价体的多视图推理，实现从稀疏有视角图像中预测干净3D高斯并合成高质量新视图。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有前馈式3D高斯重建方法在低光照条件下因噪声、颜色偏移和不可靠的特征匹配而性能严重退化，缺乏专门针对低光照场景的端到端重建框架。

Method: （1）构建可控的多视图低光照基准，仅退化上下文视图而保持目标视图干净；（2）提出轻量级低光照适配器（Lowlight Adapter）进行残差增强以提升特征可匹配性；（3）耦合基于代价体的多视图推理，直接预测干净的3D高斯体。

Result: 在低光照条件下，DelowlightSplat显著优于之前的前馈式方法及两阶段流水线。

Conclusion: 提出的低光照感知前馈式框架能有效应对低光照场景中的噪声、颜色偏移和匹配困难问题，实现高质量的新视图合成和3D重建。

Original Abstract: Novel-view synthesis and 3D reconstruction from sparse posed images are central to robotics and AR/VR. Yet, feed-forward 3D Gaussian reconstruction fails under lowlight due to noise, color shifts, and unreliable correspondence. We propose DelowlightSplat, a lowlight-aware feed-forward Gaussian splatting framework for clean novel-view rendering. We build a controllable multi-view lowlight benchmark by degrading only context views while keeping target views clean. We introduce a lightweight Lowlight Adapter for residual enhancement to improve matchability, and couple it with cost-volume-based multi-view inference to directly predict clean 3D Gaussians. Experiments show that DelowlightSplat significantly outperforms previous feed-forward method and two-stage pipeline under lowlight conditions.

中文摘要翻译: 从稀疏有视角图像中进行新视图合成和3D重建是机器人和AR/VR领域的核心任务。然而，前馈式3D高斯重建在低光照条件下会因噪声、颜色偏移和不可靠的对应关系而失效。我们提出DelowlightSplat，一个低光照感知的前馈式高斯泼溅框架，用于生成干净的新视图渲染。我们通过仅退化上下文视图而保持目标视图干净，构建了一个可控的多视图低光照基准。我们引入轻量级低光照适配器进行残差增强以提升可匹配性，并将其与基于代价体的多视图推理相结合，直接预测干净的3D高斯体。实验表明，DelowlightSplat在低光照条件下显著优于此前的前馈式方法和两阶段流水线。

</details>


### [48] [JetViT: Efficient High-Resolution Vision Transformer with Post-Training Attention Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26636)
*Dongyun Zou,Zhuoyang Zhang,Junyu Chen,Wenkun He,Qinhe Peng,Hanrong Ye,Yao Lu,Hongxu Yin,Yu Wang,Song Han,Han Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JetViT提出了一种名为“训练后注意力搜索”的后训练加速框架，将预训练的全注意力ViT转换为高效混合注意力变体，在保持精度的同时，对高分辨率图像实现了最高1.79倍吞吐量提升和44.81%延迟降低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有全注意力Vision Transformer（ViT）在处理高分辨率图像时计算开销巨大，推理效率低下，而直接从头训练混合注意力模型成本高昂且不灵活。因此，本文旨在不牺牲精度的前提下，高效地将已预训练的全注意力ViT转换为混合注意力架构以提升推理速度。

Method: 提出Post-Training Attention Search（训练后注意力搜索）框架，包含三个关键步骤：(1) 优化线性注意力块的设计；(2) 搜索线性注意力块和窗口注意力块的最佳组合；(3) 识别并保留关键的全注意力块。该框架继承基模型的MLP和注意力权重，高效探索架构设计空间。

Result: 在NVIDIA H100 GPU上，基于DINOv3和DepthAnythingV2两个高分辨率视觉基础模型，JetViT实现了最高1.79倍的吞吐量提升和最高44.81%的延迟降低，且不损失精度。

Conclusion: JetViT通过后训练搜索策略，无需重新训练即可将全注意力ViT高效转换为混合注意力变体，在保持与最先进全注意力模型相同精度的同时，显著提升了高分辨率图像的推理效率。

Original Abstract: We introduce JetViT, a novel family of hybrid-architecture Vision Transformer (ViT) models that match the accuracy of state-of-the-art full-attention vision foundation models while achieving substantially higher inference efficiency on high-resolution images. At the core of our approach is Post-Training Attention Search, a post-training acceleration framework that converts pre-trained full-attention ViTs into efficient hybrid-attention variants by identifying and replacing redundant full-attention blocks with linear or window-attention blocks. By inheriting the MLP and attention weights from the base model, Post-Training Attention Search efficiently explores the architectural design space through three key steps: (1) optimizing the linear-attention block design; (2) finding the best combination of linear-attention and window-attention blocks; and (3) identifying and preserving critical full-attention blocks. We evaluate JetViT on two representative high-resolution vision foundation models, DINOv3 and DepthAnythingV2. On the NVIDIA H100 GPU, JetViT achieves up to 1.79x higher throughput and up to 44.81% lower latency without sacrificing accuracy. We will release our code and accelerated ViT models soon.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了JetViT，一种新型的混合架构视觉Transformer（ViT）模型家族，它在匹配最先进的全注意力视觉基础模型精度的同时，在高分辨率图像上实现了显著更高的推理效率。我们方法的核心是训练后注意力搜索（Post-Training Attention Search），这是一种后训练加速框架，通过识别冗余的全注意力块并将其替换为线性注意力块或窗口注意力块，将预训练的全注意力ViT转换为高效的混合注意力变体。通过继承基模型的MLP和注意力权重，训练后注意力搜索通过三个关键步骤高效地探索架构设计空间：（1）优化线性注意力块的设计；（2）寻找线性注意力块和窗口注意力块的最佳组合；（3）识别并保留关键的全注意力块。我们在两个具有代表性的高分辨率视觉基础模型DINOv3和DepthAnythingV2上评估了JetViT。在NVIDIA H100 GPU上，JetViT在不牺牲精度的情况下实现了高达1.79倍的吞吐量提升和高达44.81%的延迟降低。我们即将发布代码和加速后的ViT模型。

</details>


### [49] [OmniRetriever: Any-to-Any Audio-Video-Text Retrieval via Fusion-as-Teacher Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26641)
*Yunze Liu,Chi-Hao Wu,Enmin Zhou,Junxiao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出融合即教师蒸馏方法，利用融合的多模态嵌入作为教师信号指导单模态嵌入学习，并引入Tuple-InfoNCE损失监督融合嵌入本身，构建了OmniRetriever-7B模型，在零样本检索和AVT基准测试中均取得领先性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的音视频文本编码器虽能生成联合嵌入，但标准配对InfoNCE目标函数在训练时并未利用三个模态同时可用时的信号，导致融合信息被浪费。

Method: 提出融合即教师（fusion-as-teacher）蒸馏方法：将融合嵌入的停止梯度副本作为教师信号指导单模态嵌入学习；同时引入Tuple-InfoNCE项直接监督融合嵌入的语义质量。

Result: 在Clotho和SoundDescs上超过闭源Gemini Embedding 2达13.3-18.0 R@1；在MSR-VTT和MSVD上达到当前最先进零样本开放视频文本编码器水平；在自建OmniRetriever-Bench上AVG-all达34.84，优于Gemini Embedding 2的1.72和最优开源AVT方法的8.03。

Conclusion: 融合即教师蒸馏能有效利用多模态融合信号提升单模态和联合嵌入质量，OmniRetriever-7B在跨模态检索任务上表现优异。

Original Abstract: Unified multimodal embedding spaces have become the standard interface for cross-modal retrieval and multimodal RAG, and recent audio-video-text (AVT) encoders extend this setting to three modalities. Such encoders can produce a joint (T,V,A) embedding whenever all three modalities are available, but standard pairwise InfoNCE objectives leave this signal unused during training. We close this gap with fusion-as-teacher distillation, which treats a stop-gradient copy of the fused embedding as a teacher signal for the single-modal embeddings, paired with a Tuple-InfoNCE term that supervises the fused embedding directly. We instantiate this objective as OmniRetriever-7B. Across six zero-shot retrieval benchmarks, OmniRetriever-7B surpasses the closed-source Gemini Embedding 2 by 13.3-18.0 R@1 on Clotho and SoundDescs, and reaches the contemporary zero-shot specialist band of open video-text encoders on MSR-VTT and MSVD. To stress-test joint representations, we further release OmniRetriever-Bench, a 12-direction AVT retrieval benchmark totaling 3782 triples; on it OmniRetriever-7B attains AVG-all 34.84, improving over Gemini Embedding 2 by 1.72 and over the best prior open-source AVT method by 8.03.

中文摘要翻译: 统一的多模态嵌入空间已成为跨模态检索和多模态RAG的标准接口，最近的音视频文本编码器将此设置扩展到三种模态。这类编码器在三模态皆可用时能生成联合(T,V,A)嵌入，但标准的配对InfoNCE目标函数在训练时未利用此信号。我们通过融合即教师蒸馏来弥补这一差距，将融合嵌入的停止梯度副本视为单模态嵌入的教师信号，并配以Tuple-InfoNCE项直接监督融合嵌入。我们将该目标函数实例化为OmniRetriever-7B。在六个零样本检索基准上，OmniRetriever-7B在Clotho和SoundDescs上超过闭源Gemini Embedding 2达13.3-18.0 R@1，在MSR-VTT和MSVD上达到当代零样本专用开放视频文本编码器的水平。为压力测试联合表示，我们进一步发布了OmniRetriever-Bench，这是一个包含12个方向的AVT检索基准，总计3782个三元组；在此基准上，OmniRetriever-7B取得了34.84的AVG-all，比Gemini Embedding 2提高了1.72，比之前最优的开源AVT方法提高了8.03。

</details>


### [50] [DV-SFT: Direct Vision Supervision for Fine-Grained Visual Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26656)
*Jianfei Zhao,Feng Zhang,Xin Sun,Chong Feng,Bing Wang,Zhixing Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DV-SFT方法，通过对视觉token施加直接的、token级别的监督信号（利用OCR场景中的图文对应关系，将视觉token与其图像块中的单词对齐），在不修改模型架构或增加额外前向传播的情况下，通过相同的next-token prediction目标同时训练视觉和文本token，显著提升了细粒度视觉理解能力和多模态对齐效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）仅对文本token施加监督信号，视觉token仅作为上下文被隐式优化，导致粗粒度的视觉理解。虽然已有工作尝试监督视觉输入，但依赖额外的解码器或前向传播等辅助组件，限制了实际应用。

Method: 提出Direct Vision Supervised Fine-Tuning (DV-SFT)，利用OCR场景中直接的视觉-文本对应关系，自动为每个视觉token标注其对应图像块中的单词，然后通过与文本相同的next-token prediction目标对视觉token进行显式、token级别的监督训练。该方法将MLLM视为黑盒，无需修改架构或增加额外前向传播。

Result: 在3个域内基准和4个域外基准上，DV-SFT一致优于标准SFT。进一步分析表明，视觉监督有效增强了细粒度视觉理解，并实现了更高的多模态对齐效率。

Conclusion: 直接对视觉token施加显式监督信号是可行且有效的，能够在不增加模型复杂度的情况下显著提升多模态大语言模型的视觉理解能力。

Original Abstract: Multimodal large language models are typically trained end-to-end to predict ground-truth answers, yet supervision signals are applied exclusively to text tokens. Visual tokens, the core carriers of visual information, are optimized only implicitly as part of the context, leading to coarse-grained visual understanding. Prior works attempt to supervise visual inputs but inevitably rely on auxiliary components such as additional decoders or forward passes, because visual tokens lack readily interpretable labels. This limits their practical applicability. In this work, we propose \textbf{D}irect \textbf{V}ision \textbf{S}upervised \textbf{F}ine-\textbf{T}uning (DV-SFT), which constructs explicit, token-level supervision for visual tokens and trains them through the same next-token prediction objective used for text. Specifically, we exploit the direct vision--text correspondence in OCR-related scenarios and automatically label each visual token with the word in its corresponding image patch. DV-SFT treats the MLLM as a black box, requiring no architectural modifications or additional forward passes. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of direct vision supervision. DV-SFT consistently outperforms standard SFT across three in-domain and four out-of-domain benchmarks. Further analyses show that vision supervision effectively enhances fine-grained visual understanding and achieves higher multimodal alignment efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型通常以端到端方式训练以预测真实答案，但监督信号仅施加于文本token。视觉token作为视觉信息的核心载体，仅作为上下文被隐式优化，导致粗粒度的视觉理解。以往工作尝试监督视觉输入，但不可避免地依赖额外组件（如额外的解码器或前向传播），因为视觉token缺乏现成的可解释标签，这限制了其实际应用。本文提出直接视觉监督微调（DV-SFT），该方法为视觉token构建显式的、token级别的监督信号，并通过与文本相同的next-token prediction目标训练它们。具体而言，我们利用OCR相关场景中直接的视觉-文本对应关系，自动为每个视觉token标注其对应图像块中的单词。DV-SFT将MLLM视为黑盒，无需修改架构或增加额外前向传播。大量实验证明了直接视觉监督的优越性。DV-SFT在三个域内基准和四个域外基准上均一致优于标准SFT。进一步分析表明，视觉监督有效增强了细粒度视觉理解，并实现了更高的多模态对齐效率。

</details>


### [51] [Respecting Modality Gap in Post-hoc Out-of-distribution Detection with Pre-trained Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26661)
*Yuanwei Hu,Bo Peng,Yadan Luo,Zhen Fang,Ling Chen,Jie Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对预训练视觉-语言模型在零样本OOD检测中文本原型与视觉原型存在模态不对齐的问题，提出了一种在线伪监督框架，在测试时利用未标记数据流直接在视觉特征空间中学习类原型，实现了SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法普遍将类名的文本嵌入作为类原型，但本文理论证明现成的文本原型与最优视觉原型之间存在固有的模态差异，且仅靠提示工程无法消除。因此需要在后验（post-hoc）约束下弥补这一模态差距。

Method: 提出在线伪监督框架，利用预训练VLM对未标注测试数据流生成软预测（soft predictions），直接在视觉特征空间中在线学习类原型，并提供了该在线优化过程收敛性的理论保证。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在多种OOD检测设置下均取得了新的最佳结果（state of the art）。

Conclusion: 文本作为原型的范式存在固有局限性，通过在线伪监督方式在视觉空间学习原型可以有效消除模态差异，显著提升零样本OOD检测性能。

Original Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection has emerged as a popular technique to enhance the reliability of machine learning models by identifying unexpected inputs from unknown classes. Recent progress in pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs) has enabled zero-shot OOD detection without access to in-distribution (ID) training data; in this setting, existing methods commonly treat text embeddings of class names as class prototypes. In this paper, we challenge the widely adopted text-as-prototype paradigm by theoretically showing that off-the-shelf textual prototypes are generally misaligned with the optimal visual prototypes, yielding an intrinsic modality gap that cannot be eliminated by prompt engineering alone. To mitigate this gap under the post-hoc constraint, this paper presents an online pseudo-supervised framework that directly learns class prototypes in the visual feature space using unlabeled test-time data streams and soft predictions from the pre-trained VLMs. We provide theoretical guarantees for the convergence of the online optimization procedure. Extensive experiments empirically demonstrate that our method achieves a new state of the art across a variety of OOD detection setups.

中文摘要翻译: 分布外（OOD）检测已成为一种流行的技术，通过识别来自未知类别的意外输入来增强机器学习模型的可靠性。预训练视觉-语言模型（VLM）的最新进展使得无需访问分布内（ID）训练数据即可实现零样本OOD检测；在这种设定下，现有方法通常将类名的文本嵌入作为类原型。本文挑战了这种广泛采用的文本即原型范式，通过理论证明表明现成的文本原型通常与最优视觉原型存在不对齐，从而产生固有的模态差异，而仅靠提示工程无法消除该差异。为了在后验约束下缓解这一差距，本文提出了一种在线伪监督框架，利用未标记的测试时数据流和预训练VLM的软预测，直接在视觉特征空间中学习类原型。我们为在线优化过程的收敛性提供了理论保证。大量实验实证表明，我们的方法在多种OOD检测设置下均取得了新的最佳结果。

</details>


### [52] [DynFrame: Adaptive Reasoning-Driven Multimodal Framework with Dynamic Frame Augmentation for Complex Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26680)
*Peng Zhang,Guanghao Zhang,Wanggui He,Longxiang Zhang,Mushui Liu,Yan Xia,Zhenhao Peng,Weilong Dai,Jinlong Liu,Haobing Tang,Le Zhang,Hao Jiang,Pipei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DynFrame提出可学习的时间窗口和采样密度令牌，结合SD-GRPO实现细粒度奖励分配，使4B模型媲美7B-8B基线，8B模型达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频MLLM存在两个结构缺陷：(i)采样密度不可学习，细粒度证据需多次检索，增加推理长度和训练难度；(ii)检索与答案生成共享单轨迹优势，无法区分"看哪里"和"如何答"的贡献。

Method: 提出DynFrame框架：将时间窗口和采样密度作为原生令牌在单自回归过程中生成，实现单步多粒度检索。进一步提出SD-GRPO，在检索边界分割rollout，为采样令牌和答案令牌分配角色特定的令牌级优势。

Result: 在DM-CoT-74k和DM-RL-45k上训练，DynFrame-4B在六个基准上媲美强7B-8B基线，DynFrame-8B在大多数指标上达到新SOTA。

Conclusion: 通过学习密度和窗口的可检索机制以及细粒度奖励分配，能够在不增加推理上下文的前提下高效获取多粒度证据，显著提升视频MLLM性能。

Original Abstract: Recent video multimodal large language models (MLLMs) increasingly couple step-by-step reasoning with on-demand visual evidence retrieval, allowing models to revisit relevant video segments during inference. However, two structural gaps remain in existing thinking-with-video systems. (i) Sampling density is not a learnable decision: existing methods may let the model decide where to look, but the per-window frame rate is largely fixed. As a result, fine-grained evidence is often recovered through repeated retrieval calls, which increases inference context length and training difficulty. (ii) Retrieval and answer generation are usually optimized with a single trajectory-level advantage, so the "where to look" tokens and the "how to answer" tokens receive the same credit even when one is correct and the other is not. To address these gaps, we present DynFrame, a framework that emits the temporal window and the sampling density as native tokens within a single autoregressive pass. This learnable span-density retrieval enables acquiring multi-granularity evidence with a single retrieval step. Based on the above tokenized retrieval interface, we further introduce Segment-Decoupled GRPO (SD-GRPO), which splits each rollout at the retrieval boundary and assigns role-specific token-level advantages, separately crediting the sampling decision and the answer. Trained on the curated DM-CoT-74k and DM-RL-45k, DynFrame-4B is competitive with strong 7B-8B baselines across six benchmarks (NExT-GQA, Charades-STA, ActivityNet-MR, Video-MME, MLVU, LVBench), and DynFrame-8B sets new state-of-the-art on most metrics. Code is available at https://github.com/zhangguanghao523/DynFrame.

中文摘要翻译: 近期视频多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）越来越多地将逐步推理与按需视觉证据检索结合起来，允许模型在推理过程中重新访问相关视频片段。然而，现有的"思考+视频"系统仍存在两个结构上的缺陷。(i) 采样密度不是可学习的决策：现有方法可能让模型决定看哪里，但每窗口的帧率在很大程度上是固定的。因此，细粒度证据通常通过重复的检索调用来恢复，这增加了推理上下文长度和训练难度。(ii) 检索和答案生成通常使用单一轨迹级优势进行优化，因此"看哪里"的令牌和"如何答"的令牌获得相同的信用，即使其中一个正确而另一个不正确。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了DynFrame，一个在单次自回归过程中将时间窗口和采样密度作为原生令牌发射的框架。这种可学习的跨度-密度检索使得单步检索即可获取多粒度证据。基于上述令牌化检索接口，我们进一步引入了分段解耦GRPO（SD-GRPO），该方法在每个rollout的检索边界处进行分割，并分配角色特定的令牌级优势，分别对采样决策和答案进行信用分配。在精心整理的DM-CoT-74k和DM-RL-45k上训练后，DynFrame-4B在六个基准测试（NExT-GQA、Charades-STA、ActivityNet-MR、Video-MME、MLVU、LVBench）上与强大的7B-8B基线具有竞争力，而DynFrame-8B在大多数指标上设立了新的最先进水平。代码可在 https://github.com/zhangguanghao523/DynFrame 获取。

</details>


### [53] [PinPoint: Prompting with Informative Interior Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26689)
*Pouya Sadeghi,Shawn He,Pedro Pablo Guerrero Vela,C. Thomas,Alex Wong,Sirisha Rambhatla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现当前训练自由的指代图像分割流水线中，性能差距主要源于提示歧义（即边界框内点选不准确），而非VLM或SAM本身的能力限制。为此提出PinPoint，一种无需训练、基于视觉线索融合的确定性点选择器，仅需两次VLM调用即可匹配监督与强化学习微调方法的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有训练自由的指代图像分割方法（VLM+SAM组合）性能始终落后于微调或强化学习微调的专用模型，但此前不清楚差距来源。本文旨在探究差距的根源（VLM的定位能力、SAM的掩码生成能力、还是提示本身），并尝试在不进行任务特定训练的情况下缩小差距。

Method: 提出PinPoint，一种确定性、无需训练的点选择器。（1）融合四种视觉线索生成共识图；（2）选择紧凑、空间多样且远离边界的点；（3）使用冻结的VLM为每个点标注语义。仅需两次VLM调用即可完成每个查询。

Result: 在RefCOCO/+/g数据集上，使用五个内点替代随机采样，累计交并比（cIoU）提升12-18个点。PinPoint无需任务特定训练即可匹配监督和强化学习微调方法。

Conclusion: 指代图像分割中训练自由方法与微调方法的性能差距主要由提示歧义（即点选择策略）主导，而非VLM或SAM的能力限制。通过合理的点选择策略（如PinPoint），可以在不训练的情况下达到与监督/强化学习方法相当的性能。

Original Abstract: Modern referring image segmentation pipelines couple a vision-language model (VLM) for grounding with a promptable segmenter such as the Segment Anything Model (SAM) for mask generation. Prior training-free instances of this recipe consistently trail fine-tuned and reinforcement-learning (RL)-tuned specialists, and it has been unclear whether the gap comes from the VLM's grounding, SAM's capacity, or the prompt. We show that the gap is dominated by prompt ambiguity: a VLM-proposed bounding box (bbox) leaves SAM to guess which pixels inside the bbox belong to the object the expression denotes. Interior points are the natural disambiguator, but where they fall matters; prior work relies on naively sampled points that land on boundaries, distractors, and background clutter, and can even hurt performance compared to the bbox alone. Supervised and RL-tuned methods close this gap by training a VLM to predict better points; we show that this training is unnecessary. At a matched budget of five interior points, replacing naive sampling with stable, informative point selection improves cumulative Intersection-over-Union (cIoU) by 12-18 points across RefCOCO/+/g, with every model fixed. We turn this observation into PinPoint, a deterministic, training-free point selector that fuses four visual cues into a consensus map, selects compact, spatially diverse points away from boundaries, and uses the frozen VLM to label each point. Without any task-specific training, PinPoint matches supervised and RL-tuned specialists on the same stack while issuing only two VLM calls per query.

中文摘要翻译: 现代指代图像分割流程将用于定位的视觉-语言模型（VLM）与可提示的分割器（如Segment Anything Model, SAM）相结合以生成掩码。此前该方案中无需训练的实例始终落后于微调和强化学习微调的专用模型，且尚不清楚差距来自VLM的定位能力、SAM的容量还是提示本身。我们表明该差距主要由提示歧义主导：VLM提出的边界框（bbox）让SAM难以判断框内哪些像素属于表达式所指代的对象。内部点是一种自然的去歧义手段，但点的落位至关重要；先前工作依赖的朴素采样点往往落在边界、干扰物和背景杂波上，甚至可能比仅用边界框效果更差。监督和强化学习微调方法通过训练VLM预测更好的点来缩小这一差距；我们证明这种训练是不必要的。在五个内部点的固定预算下，将朴素采样替换为稳定、信息量丰富的点选择方法，在RefCOCO/+/g上累计交并比（cIoU）提升12-18个点，且所有模型参数固定不变。我们将这一观察转化为PinPoint——一种确定性、无需训练的点选择器，它融合四种视觉线索生成共识图，选择紧凑、空间多样且远离边界的点，并使用冻结的VLM为每个点标注。无需任何任务特定训练，PinPoint在相同模型栈上即可匹配监督和强化学习微调的专用模型，且每个查询仅需两次VLM调用。

</details>


### [54] [Rotation-Invariant Spherical Watermarking via Third-Order SO(3) Representation Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26702)
*Pengzhen Chen,Yanwei Liu,Xiaoyan Gu,Antonios Argyriou,Wu Liu,Weiping Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对全景图像在任意3D旋转下的可靠水印问题，提出了一种基于球面信号和SO(3)表示论的三阶不变性构造方法——球面不变双谱，通过将高阶不可约表示经张量积投影到平凡表示，在保持旋转不变性的同时保留相位信息，从而实现了高容量、高鲁棒性的全景图像水印嵌入与提取。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统平面表示和基于数据增强的鲁棒性策略缺乏理论保证，无法应对全景图像在SO(3)群作用下的任意3D旋转；而现有球谐系数构造的旋转不变量通常局限于零阶统计量，丢弃了方向信息，严重限制了水印的嵌入容量。

Method: 将全景图建模为球面信号，利用SO(3)表示论，通过张量积耦合高阶SO(3)不可约表示并投影到平凡表示，构造出一个三阶球面不变双谱（spherical invariant bispectrum）。该双谱在严格保持旋转不变性的同时保留了相位信息。水印被嵌入到高阶球谐系数中，并从不变双谱标量中恢复，从而实现任意3D旋转下的可靠提取。

Result: 提供了所提方法的SO(3)不变性的理论证明，并通过实验证明该方法在连续旋转下具有近乎完美的鲁棒性，同时保持高视觉保真度。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于球面不变双谱的水印方法能够在任意3D旋转下实现可靠的盲提取，兼具理论保证和实际性能，为全景图像版权保护提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: Reliable watermarking of panoramic imagery is fundamentally challenged by arbitrary 3D rotations. As panoramas are defined on the sphere, they naturally transform under the action of $SO(3)$, rendering conventional planar representations and augmentation-based robustness strategies inadequate and devoid of theoretical guarantees. To address this, we formulate panoramas as spherical signals and leverage $SO(3)$ representation theory to derive provably rotation-invariant descriptors. While spherical harmonic coefficients transform equivariantly under rotations, the natural invariant constructions are typically limited to zeroth-order statistics which eliminate directional information and severely constrain embedding capacity. In this work, we introduce a principled third-order invariant construction by coupling higher-order $SO(3)$ irreducible representations via tensor products and projecting onto the trivial representation. This yields a spherical invariant bispectrum that preserves phase information while remaining strictly rotation-invariant. Leveraging this property, we embed watermarks into higher-order spherical harmonic coefficients and recover them from invariant bispectral scalars, enabling reliable extraction under arbitrary 3D rotations. We provide a theoretical proof of $SO(3)$ invariance for it and demonstrate experimentally its near-perfect robustness to continuous rotations while maintaining high visual fidelity.

中文摘要翻译: 全景图像的可信水印受到任意三维旋转的根本性挑战。由于全景图定义在球面上，其在SO(3)群作用下自然变换，使得传统的平面表示和基于数据增强的鲁棒性策略缺乏理论保证且效果不佳。为解决这一问题，本文将全景图建模为球面信号，并利用SO(3)表示论推导出具有可证明旋转不变性的描述子。虽然球谐系数在旋转下等变变换，但天然的不变量构造通常局限于零阶统计量，会丢弃方向信息并严重限制嵌入容量。本文通过张量积耦合高阶SO(3)不可约表示并投影到平凡表示，引入了一种原理性的三阶不变量构造方法，得到了一种在严格保持旋转不变性的同时保留相位信息的球面不变双谱。利用这一特性，我们将水印嵌入到高阶球谐系数中，并从不变双谱标量中恢复，从而实现了任意三维旋转下的可靠提取。我们提供了该方法的SO(3)不变性理论证明，并通过实验证明其在连续旋转下具有近乎完美的鲁棒性，同时保持高视觉保真度。

</details>


### [55] [METATR: A Multilingual, Evolving Benchmark for Automatic Text Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26712)
*Mélodie Boillet,Solène Tarride,Christopher Kermorvant*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了METATR (v1.0)，一个多语言、可演进的文本识别基准，用于评估包括视觉大语言模型在内的自动文本识别系统，涵盖29种语言、多种脚本和布局，并建立了标准化提示、归一化方法和动态评估框架。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基准大多只包含英文现代印刷文本，无法反映真实文档的多样性和复杂性，限制了实际应用中的模型评估与选择。

Method: 从多个公开数据集中收集覆盖29种语言、多种脚本和布局的文档构建METATR基准，定义标准化提示与归一化方法，建立可扩展的动态评估框架。

Result: 评估了多种开源和闭源先进模型，发现闭源模型性能最稳定，但不同脚本和布局间仍存在显著差异。

Conclusion: METATR提供了一个面向实践者的多维度框架，用于在真实条件下评估多语言文本识别，并跟踪领域进展。

Original Abstract: Benchmarks that reflect the diversity and complexity of real-world documents are essential for accurately evaluating Automatic Text Recognition (ATR) systems, especially Vision-Large Language Models (vLLMs). Although recent models demonstrate impressive performance, they are often evaluated on datasets containing modern, printed texts mostly written in English, which limits their relevance to many practical applications. Therefore, selecting a model for a specific use case requires evaluating it on data that matches the target documents. This highlights the importance of representative benchmarks for real-world applications. In this paper, we introduce METATR (v1.0), a multilingual, evolving benchmark designed to evaluate ATR models across a wide range of documents, facilitating meaningful model comparison and selection. The benchmark was designed to maximize diversity by including documents from various public collections. These documents cover 29 languages and include texts with multiple scripts and layouts. Beyond the dataset itself, METATR defines a standardized prompting and normalization methodology and establishes a dynamic evaluation framework. This approach is intended to produce reproducible results while remaining extensible over time. We evaluated a wide range of state-of-the-art systems, including open-source models and closed-source models. Results are reported across various dimensions, including performance at the dataset and language levels, robustness to handwritten documents, and computational efficiency. Our findings show that, although proprietary models achieve the most consistent performance, substantial variability persists across scripts and layouts. Overall, METATR provides a multidimensional, practitioner-oriented framework for assessing multilingual ATR in real-world conditions and tracking progress as the field evolves.

中文摘要翻译: 能够反映真实文档多样性和复杂性的基准，对于准确评估自动文本识别（ATR）系统，特别是视觉大语言模型（vLLMs）至关重要。尽管最近的模型表现出了令人印象深刻的性能，但它们通常仅在包含现代印刷文本（主要是英文）的数据集上进行评估，这限制了它们对许多实际应用的相关性。因此，为特定用例选择模型需要在与目标文档匹配的数据上进行评估。这突显了代表性基准对于实际应用的重要性。在本文中，我们介绍了METATR (v1.0)，一个多语言、可演进的基准，旨在评估跨多种文档的ATR模型，促进有意义的模型比较和选择。该基准通过纳入来自多个公开数据集的文档来最大化多样性。这些文档覆盖29种语言，并包含多种脚本和布局的文本。除了数据集本身，METATR还定义了标准化的提示和归一化方法，并建立了一个动态评估框架。这种方法旨在产生可重复的结果，同时随着时间推移保持可扩展性。我们评估了广泛的先进系统，包括开源模型和闭源模型。结果从多个维度报告，包括数据集和语言层面的性能、对手写文档的鲁棒性以及计算效率。我们的研究结果表明，尽管专有模型取得了最一致的表现，但不同脚本和布局之间仍存在显著差异。总体而言，METATR提供了一个面向实践者的多维度框架，用于在真实条件下评估多语言ATR，并跟踪该领域的进展。

</details>


### [56] [Joint 2D-3D Segmentation and Association in Street-level Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26725)
*Amir Melnikov,Masayuki Tanaka,Yusuke Monno,Masatoshi Okutomi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种联合2D-3D分割与关联的统一框架，通过零样本检测、分割与运动恢复结构重建，结合3D驱动的关联机制替代传统2D跟踪，实现街景图像中的跨视角身份保持，在城市场景中性能提升22%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统街景理解方法严重依赖时序帧进行目标跟踪，在宽基线视点及多变成像条件下难以保持稳定的跨视角对应关系，亟需一种不依赖时序信息的几何一致性驱动方法。

Method: 将视觉语义与多视图几何推理相结合，利用零样本检测和分割配合运动恢复结构（SfM）重建建立跨视角对应；采用3D驱动的关联机制替代2D多目标跟踪，通过几何一致性引导身份保持，融合2D纹理线索与全局3D上下文。

Result: 相比最先进的纯2D跟踪方法，在具有挑战性的城市场景中实现了22%的性能提升，显著提高了对真实标注序列的覆盖率和身份保持的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该统一框架适用于可扩展的街景处理，支持多种目标类型，为大规模城市制图和空间数字孪生环境构建提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Accurate interpretation of street-level imagery is essential for large-scale urban mapping and the creation of Spatial Digital Twin (SDT) environments. This work presents a unified framework for joint 2D-3D segmentation and association that integrates visual semantics with multi-view geometric reasoning. Unlike conventional approaches that rely heavily on sequential frames for temporal tracking, our method leverages zero-shot detection and segmentation together with structure-from-motion reconstruction to establish stable cross-view correspondences. A 3D-driven association mechanism replaces traditional 2D multi-object tracking, using geometric consistency to guide identity preservation across wide-baseline viewpoints and varying imaging conditions. By combining 2D texture cues with global 3D context, the proposed pipeline is well-suited for scalable street-level processing and can be used for a variety of object types. Experiments demonstrate substantially improved coverage of ground-truth sequences and more robust identity retention compared to state-of-the-art 2D-only tracking methods, achieving a 22% performance gain in challenging urban scenarios.

中文摘要翻译: 准确理解街景图像对于大规模城市制图和构建空间数字孪生（SDT）环境至关重要。本文提出了一个用于联合2D-3D分割与关联的统一框架，将视觉语义与多视图几何推理相结合。不同于传统方法严重依赖时序帧进行时间跟踪，我们的方法利用零样本检测和分割，配合运动恢复结构重建来建立稳定的跨视角对应关系。一种3D驱动的关联机制取代了传统的2D多目标跟踪，通过几何一致性在宽基线视点和多变成像条件下引导身份保持。通过将2D纹理线索与全局3D上下文相结合，所提出的管线适用于可扩展的街景处理，并可应用于多种目标类型。实验表明，与最先进的纯2D跟踪方法相比，本方法显著提高了对真实标注序列的覆盖率和更鲁棒的身份保持，在具有挑战性的城市场景中实现了22%的性能增益。

</details>


### [57] [CIRCLED: A Multi-turn CIR Dataset with Consistent Dialogues across Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26734)
*Tomohisa Takeda,Yu-Chieh Lin,Yuji Nozawa,Youyang Ng,Osamu Torii,Yusuke Matsui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为CIRCLED的多轮组合图像检索基准数据集，通过扩展三个现有数据集（FashionIQ、CIRR、CIRCO），构建了22,608个多轮会话，显著提升了数据集规模、领域泛化性和对话历史一致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MTCIR数据集存在两个主要问题：缺乏对话历史一致性，且仅局限于时尚领域。为解决这些局限性，作者构建了CIRCLED数据集。

Method: 基于CIReVL的检索流水线生成数据，并采用多个过滤器（检索成功、轮次长度、一致性、信息冗余）进行质量筛选。数据集扩展自FashionIQ、CIRR和CIRCO三个已有数据集，每个轮次的查询逐步逼近目标图像。

Result: 收集了9个子集共22,608个多轮会话，在规模和通用性上显著超过Multi-turn FashionIQ（11,505个会话）。并应用多种基线方法对CIRCLED进行了量化评估。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个实用、高质量的多轮组合图像检索基准，有助于推动该领域的未来研究。数据集和代码已公开。

Original Abstract: Existing Multi-Turn Composed Image Retrieval (MTCIR) datasets lack dialogue-history consistency and are restricted to the fashion domain. To address these limitations, we construct CIRCLED by extending FashionIQ, CIRR, and CIRCO. In CIRCLED, the query at each turn progressively approaches the target image. Data are generated via a CIReVL-based retrieval pipeline and curated with multiple filters on retrieval success, turn length, consistency, and information redundancy to ensure quality. In total, we collect 22,608 multi-turn sessions across nine subsets, substantially exceeding Multi-turn FashionIQ (11,505 sessions) in both scale and generality. We further apply multiple baseline methods and quantitatively assess retrieval accuracy on CIRCLED. Our work provides a practical, high-quality benchmark to facilitate future research on multi-turn CIR. The dataset and code are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/tk1441/CIRCLED and https://github.com/mti-lab/circled.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的多轮组合图像检索（MTCIR）数据集缺乏对话历史一致性，且仅限于时尚领域。为了解决这些局限性，我们通过扩展FashionIQ、CIRR和CIRCO构建了CIRCLED数据集。在CIRCLED中，每一轮的查询逐步逼近目标图像。数据通过基于CIReVL的检索流水线生成，并采用检索成功、轮次长度、一致性和信息冗余等多个过滤器进行质量筛选。我们共收集了9个子集中的22,608个多轮会话，在规模和通用性上均显著超过Multi-turn FashionIQ（11,505个会话）。我们进一步应用了多种基线方法，并在CIRCLED上定量评估了检索准确率。我们的工作为促进多轮组合图像检索的未来研究提供了一个实用、高质量的基准。数据集和代码已在https://huggingface.co/datasets/tk1441/CIRCLED 和 https://github.com/mti-lab/circled 公开。

</details>


### [58] [Once-For-All: A Train-Once and Select-Anytime Framework for Multimodal Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26761)
*Mingkang Dong,Hongyi Cai,Xiwen Lei,Jie Li,Tao Zhang,Muxin Pu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OFA框架，训练一个可迁移的数据选择器，只需训练一次即可跨数据集和模型规模重用，在多模态指令微调中以15%数据达到98.3%全量性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数据选择方法针对特定模型或数据集计算选择信号，当目标模型或候选池变化时需要从头重新计算，成本高昂。亟需一种可重用的、与模型和数据解耦的数据选择方案。

Method: 在冻结的CLIP空间中对多模态指令进行聚类，从聚类结构导出伪标签，训练一个轻量级选择器（少量epoch）；选择器最不确信的样本被判定为信息量最大的样本。选择器在LLaVA-665K上训练一次后，可直接迁移到其他数据集和模型规模。

Result: 在LLaVA-665K上仅选15%数据即达到全量数据98.3%的性能；迁移到未见过的Vision-Flan-186K上超越全量训练10.6%；同一个选择子集可同时提升Qwen2.5-VL-3B和LLaVA-v1.5-7B，无需为每个模型重新计算。

Conclusion: 单个可迁移的选择器能够为高效的多模态指令微调提供有效且可重用的解决方案，实现了数据选择与目标模型和候选池的解耦。

Original Abstract: Multimodal instruction tuning is the de facto recipe for adapting vision language models (VLMs), yet instruction data are highly redundant, making data selection critical for training efficiency. Existing methods derive selection signals from a specific model or dataset, so whenever the target model or candidate pool changes, the criteria must be recomputed from scratch at substantial cost. To address this, we propose OFA, a data selection framework that trains a reusable selector once and applies it to any dataset or model without recomputation. OFA clusters multimodal instructions in a frozen CLIP space, derives pseudo labels from the cluster structure, and trains a lightweight selector for only a few epochs; samples on which this selector is least confident are selected as the most informative. Once trained, the frozen selector transfers directly across datasets and model scales. The selector is trained once on LLaVA-665K and applied both to LLaVA-665K itself and, without any retraining, to the unseen Vision-Flan-186K. Selecting only 15% of the data, OFA achieves 98.3% of full data performance across 10 downstream benchmarks; on the smaller Vision-Flan-186K, the transferred selector surpasses full data training by 10.6%, confirming that the learned signal generalizes to datasets never seen during selector training. The same selected subsets benefit VLMs at both Qwen2.5-VL-3B and LLaVA-v1.5-7B without per model recomputation, decoupling selection from the target model. These results demonstrate that a single, transferable selector provides an effective and reusable solution for efficient multimodal instruction tuning.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态指令微调是适配视觉语言模型的标准方法，然而指令数据高度冗余，使得数据选择对训练效率至关重要。现有方法从特定模型或数据集导出选择信号，因此每当目标模型或候选池变化时，必须从头重新计算标准，成本高昂。为解决此问题，我们提出OFA，一个数据选择框架，该框架训练一次可重用的选择器，无需重新计算即可应用于任何数据集或模型。OFA在冻结的CLIP空间中对多模态指令进行聚类，从聚类结构导出伪标签，并仅训练少量epoch的轻量级选择器；该选择器最不确信的样本被选为信息量最大的样本。一旦训练完成，冻结的选择器可直接跨数据集和模型规模迁移。选择器在LLaVA-665K上训练一次，既应用于LLaVA-665K本身，也无需任何重新训练即可应用于未见过的Vision-Flan-186K。仅选择15%的数据，OFA在10个下游基准测试中达到了全量数据性能的98.3%；在较小的Vision-Flan-186K上，迁移的选择器超越全量训练10.6%，证实学习到的信号泛化到了选择器训练期间从未见过的数据集。同一选择的子集无需为每个模型重新计算即可同时提升Qwen2.5-VL-3B和LLaVA-v1.5-7B的性能，将选择与目标模型解耦。这些结果表明，单个可迁移的选择器为高效的多模态指令微调提供了有效且可重用的解决方案。

</details>


### [59] [Receipt Replay OOD: A Small Benchmark for Screen Replay Detection Under Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26855)
*Alexander Vinogradov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对身份证件呈现攻击检测领域缺乏真实域偏移下跨域鲁棒性评估的问题，提出了一个小型域外基准数据集Receipt Replay OOD，利用收据与身份证件共享的多种视觉特性（平面几何、弯曲边角、磨损伪影、文字/Logo图案），同时避免个人身份信息约束，用于屏幕回放攻击检测的跨域评估。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有公开数据集（DLC-2021、SynID、KID34K等）推动了身份证件呈现攻击检测研究，但对域外（OOD）鲁棒性的评估仍不充分，尤其缺乏真实域偏移场景下的评估基准。

Method: 构建了一个名为Receipt Replay OOD的小型域外基准数据集，利用收据作为身份证件的替代物，在跨域条件下评估文档回放检测模型的泛化性能。

Result: 实验证明了域偏移对文档回放检测模型泛化性能具有显著影响。

Conclusion: Receipt Replay OOD数据集为身份证件呈现攻击检测的跨域鲁棒性评估提供了有效基准，且数据集已公开。

Original Abstract: Public datasets such as DLC-2021, SynID, and KID34K have significantly contributed to research on presentation attack detection for identity documents, including screen replay attacks. However, evaluation of out-of-domain (OOD) robustness remains insufficiently explored, especially under realistic domain shifts. In this work, we introduce Receipt Replay OOD, a small out-of-domain benchmark for screen replay detection. Receipts share several characteristics with identity documents, including planar geometry, curved corners, wear-and-tear artifacts, and text or logo patterns, while avoiding personally identifiable information constraints commonly associated with identity documents. We evaluate document replay detection models under cross-domain conditions and demonstrate the impact of domain shift on generalization performance. The dataset is publicly available.

中文摘要翻译: 诸如DLC-2021、SynID和KID34K等公开数据集极大地推动了针对身份证件的呈现攻击检测（包括屏幕回放攻击）的研究。然而，在域外鲁棒性评估方面仍存在不足，尤其是在真实域偏移场景下。本文提出了Receipt Replay OOD，一个用于屏幕回放检测的小型域外基准数据集。收据与身份证件共享多种特性，包括平面几何、弯曲边角、磨损伪影以及文字或Logo图案，同时避免了身份证件通常涉及的个人身份信息约束。我们在跨域条件下评估了文档回放检测模型，并证明了域偏移对泛化性能的影响。该数据集已公开。

</details>


### [60] [REVERSE: Reinforcing Evidence Verification and Search for Agentic Image geo-localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26861)
*Yong Li,Furong Jia,Dacheng Yin,Kang Rong,Fengyun Rao,Jing Lyu,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: REVERSE是一个通过强化学习训练智能体进行多轮证据搜索与验证的图像地理定位框架，使用4B模型即可超越强检索增强基线与更大规模模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图像地理定位方法仅部分模拟人类专家的迭代推理过程：直接预测方法完全绕过证据获取，检索增强方法引入外部证据但对中间决策（在哪里搜索、如何查询、如何过滤噪声结果）缺乏监督。

Method: 提出REVERSE框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 构建带工具轨迹的训练数据，包含标注的区域选择、搜索观察和地理信息证据标签；(2) 引入视觉定位、查询效用和证据判别三个过程奖励；(3) 设计离线搜索缓存，使检索观察在强化学习训练中保持稳定和可复用，实现对噪声搜索结果的密集监督。

Result: 在Im2GPS3k和YFCC4k数据集上，使用4B参数的REVERSE模型超越了强检索增强基线，并与规模更大的模型性能相当。

Conclusion: 通过强化学习对智能体的证据搜索与验证过程进行密集监督，可以有效提升图像地理定位性能，即使使用较小的模型也能取得优异结果。

Original Abstract: Image geo-localization aims to determine where a photograph was taken, a task that often requires more than recognizing visible landmarks. Human experts typically solve it through an iterative workflow: they inspect informative regions, form location hypotheses, seek external evidence, and revise their judgments as new clues appear.
