Result: 在7组师生模型对（含Qwen、Llama、Gemma、DeepSeek）及推理和代码基准上，该方法超越基于散度的和在线策略的KD基线。关键发现：混合策略的优势来源于减少曝光偏差，而非更接近教师模型。训练成本降低9.7倍。

Conclusion: 混合硬/软标签的知识蒸馏策略能有效减少曝光偏差，Bridge-Garden理论为理解不同生成步骤的蒸馏偏好提供了理论框架，指导高效的自适应混合蒸馏方法，实现高效模型压缩。

Original Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) transfers knowledge from a large teacher model to a smaller student. In language modeling, the student is trained either on tokens sampled from the teacher (hard labels) or the teacher's full next-token distribution (soft labels). Despite soft labels appear strictly richer, we find that mixing hard and soft labels consistently yields better results. Crucially, we show that this gain cannot be explained by closer teacher matching during training. Instead, it comes from reduced exposure bias, the mismatch between training and inference distributions. To explain this phenomenon, we introduce the Bridge-Garden Decomposition theory, which categorizes generation steps into two types: Bridges, where the next token must be exact, and Gardens, where it can be flexible. We show that hard-only KD excels in Bridges by avoiding risky deviations, while soft-only KD preserves diversity in Gardens. A hybrid strategy handles both cases and, as a result, reduces exposure bias across the sequence. Guided by this theory, we develop a family of Bridge-Garden hybrid supervision methods that adaptively balance hard and soft labels. Across a primary suite of seven teacher-student pairs (including Qwen, Llama, Gemma, and DeepSeek) and benchmarks in reasoning and coding, our approach outperforms divergence-based and on-policy KD baselines while reducing training cost by 9.7x, enabling efficient model compression. Code is available at https://github.com/ghwang-s/bridge_garden_hybrid_kd_release.

中文摘要翻译: 知识蒸馏（KD）将知识从大型教师模型迁移到较小的学生模型。在语言建模中，学生模型可以在从教师模型采样的token（硬标签）或教师模型的完整下一个token分布（软标签）上进行训练。尽管软标签在信息上严格更丰富，但我们发现混合硬标签和软标签始终能取得更好的结果。关键的是，我们表明这种收益不能通过训练期间更接近教师模型来解释。相反，它来自减少曝光偏差，即训练分布与推理分布之间的不匹配。为解释这一现象，我们引入了Bridge-Garden分解理论，该理论将生成步骤分为两类：Bridge（桥梁）步骤，其中下一个token必须精确；以及Garden（花园）步骤，其中下一个token可以灵活选择。我们表明，纯硬标签KD在Bridge步骤中通过避免风险偏差表现出色，而纯软标签KD在Garden步骤中保留了多样性。混合策略能同时处理这两种情况，从而减少整个序列中的曝光偏差。在该理论指导下，我们开发了一类Bridge-Garden混合监督方法，能够自适应地平衡硬标签和软标签。在包含七组师生模型对（包括Qwen、Llama、Gemma和DeepSeek）的主要测试集以及推理和代码基准上，我们的方法超越了基于散度的和在线策略的KD基线，同时将训练成本降低了9.7倍，实现了高效的模型压缩。代码已开源：https://github.com/ghwang-s/bridge_garden_hybrid_kd_release。

</details>


### [123] [Scaling World-Model Reinforcement Learning Through Diffusion Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26282)
*Xiaoyuan Cheng,Wenxuan Yuan,Zhancun Mu,Yuanzhao Zhang,Yiming Yang,Hai Wang,Zhuo Sun,Che Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MBDPO框架，通过扩散策略表示统一世界模型中的搜索与策略优化，解决结构不对齐问题，实现可扩展的策略学习。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于世界模型的强化学习方法受限于模型偏差和误差累积，但更关键且未被充分探索的瓶颈是搜索与价值学习之间的结构不对齐，即策略改进往往依赖于由独立非搜索策略诱导的价值函数，导致训练不一致和次优学习。

Method: 提出基于模型的扩散策略优化（MBDPO）框架，将策略优化重新表述为在潜在世界模型中对搜索轨迹的扩散过程，从中提取隐式能量函数来锚定策略，通过细化分数场实现策略优化并缓解不对齐问题。

Result: 在离线多任务预训练、在线学习和离线到在线微调等多种设置下评估，离线场景中通过在大规模数据集上预训练观察模型容量增加时一致且单调的性能提升。

Conclusion: MBDPO通过扩散策略表示统一搜索与策略优化，解锁了世界模型的可扩展策略学习潜力，在多种场景下表现优异且具有良好的扩展性。

Original Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (RL) can be effectively supported at scale through the use of world models. However, in practice, scaling such approaches remains fundamentally limited. A commonly recognized challenge is model bias and error compounding, which degrade long-horizon predictions. Beyond these issues, we identify a more critical yet underexplored bottleneck: a structural misalignment between search and value learning in existing world model approaches. In particular, policy improvement often relies on value functions induced by a separate, non-search policy, resulting in training inconsistency and ultimately suboptimal learning. To address this limitation, we propose Model-Based Diffusion Policy Optimization (MBDPO) in world models, a framework that unifies search and policy optimization through diffusion policy representations, thereby unlocking the potential of world models for scalable policy learning. Instead of constructing an explicit planner over a learned world model, we reformulate policy optimization as a diffusion process over searched trajectories in latent world models. In this view, we extract an implicit energy function from the collected dataset that anchors the policy, enabling MBDPO to refine the score field for policy optimization while mitigating misalignment. We evaluate MBDPO across a wide range of settings, including multi-task offline pretraining, online learning, and offline-to-online fine-tuning. In the offline regime, we further investigate its scaling behavior by pretraining on large-scale datasets, observing consistent and monotonic performance gains with increasing model capacity.

中文摘要翻译: 基于模型的强化学习可以通过使用世界模型在大规模下得到有效支持。然而，在实践中，扩展此类方法仍然受到根本性限制。一个广泛认可的挑战是模型偏差和误差累积，这会降低长期预测的质量。除这些问题外，我们发现了一个更关键但尚未充分探索的瓶颈：现有世界模型方法中搜索与价值学习之间的结构不对齐。具体而言，策略改进通常依赖于由独立的非搜索策略所诱导的价值函数，导致训练不一致并最终产生次优学习。为解决这一局限，我们提出了基于模型的扩散策略优化（MBDPO）框架，该框架通过扩散策略表示统一了搜索与策略优化，从而释放了世界模型在可扩展策略学习中的潜力。我们并未在学到的世界模型上构建显式规划器，而是将策略优化重新表述为潜在世界模型中对搜索轨迹的扩散过程。基于此视角，我们从收集的数据集中提取隐式能量函数来锚定策略，使得MBDPO能够细化分数场以进行策略优化，同时缓解不对齐问题。我们在多种设置下评估了MBDPO，包括多任务离线预训练、在线学习以及离线到在线微调。在离线场景中，我们进一步通过在大规模数据集上进行预训练来研究其扩展行为，观察到随着模型容量增加，性能呈一致且单调的提升。

</details>


### [124] [Two-Parameter Flows for Learning Population Dynamics of Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26285)
*Paul Schwerdtner,Tobias Blickhan,Benjamin Peherstorfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需轨迹数据、仅依赖未标记样本的高维概率密度演化学习方法。通过两参数流先学习采样时间传输，再回归合成轨迹提取物理时间速度，并证明该动力学唯一且具有正则性。方法可扩展至高维，支持非梯度动力学，能解释旋转或循环物理现象。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法学习高维概率密度动力学时，通常需要轨迹信息或每步最优传输耦合，难以扩展到高维且无法处理旋转/循环等非梯度物理现象。本文旨在解决这些问题。

Method: 引入两参数流：首先利用条件流匹配技术学习从基分布到每个边际分布的采样时间传输，然后通过回归耦合的合成轨迹来提取物理时间速度，从而得到完整的动力学描述。

Result: 理论上证明了所得物理时间动力学的唯一性及其从采样时间传输继承的正则性。方法可扩展至高维，避免了每步最优传输耦合，同时支持非梯度动力学，能够解释旋转或循环物理现象。

Conclusion: 本文提出的两参数流方法为高维概率密度动力学学习提供了一种无需轨迹信息、可扩展且支持非梯度动力学的有效框架，具有理论保证和良好的物理可解释性。

Original Abstract: This work addresses the problem of learning the dynamics of high-dimensional probability densities over time using unlabeled samples, without assuming access to trajectory information. We introduce two-parameter flows that learn only sampling-time transports from a base distribution to each marginal and then extract a physics-time velocity by regressing on coupled synthetic trajectories. We prove that the resulting physics-time dynamics are unique and inherit regularity from the sampling-time transports. Because we can build on standard, well-developed conditional flow matching techniques for learning the base-to-marginal transports, our approach scales to high dimensions and avoids per-step optimal-transport couplings, while allowing admissible non-gradient dynamics that can naturally explain rotational or circulating physics phenomena.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究如何利用未标记样本学习高维概率密度随时间演化的动力学问题，而不假设可以访问轨迹信息。我们引入两参数流，首先学习从基分布到每个边际分布的采样时间传输，然后通过对耦合的合成轨迹进行回归来提取物理时间速度。我们证明了所得到的物理时间动力学是唯一的，并且从采样时间传输继承了正则性。由于我们可以基于标准且成熟的条件流匹配技术来学习基到边际的传输，我们的方法可扩展至高维，避免了每步最优传输耦合，同时允许可接纳的非梯度动力学，从而自然解释旋转或循环的物理现象。

</details>


### [125] [Stateful Inference for Low-Latency Multi-Agent Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26289)
*Victor Norgren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种有状态推理架构，通过持久化KV缓存和增量计算，将多智能体工具调用的每轮推理成本从O(n_t)降低到O(Δ_t)，在6轮工作流中提速2.1倍，35轮工作流中位轮次提速4.2倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有LLM推理框架将每次工具调用视为独立请求，重新处理整个对话历史，但实际85-95%的提示内容与上一轮相同，导致大量冗余计算。

Method: 提出有状态推理架构：(1) 持久化KV缓存跨轮次存活，仅增量摄入新令牌；(2) 基数前缀缓存扩展至交错的多智能体流量；(3) 提示查找推测解码器加速结构化输出。

Result: 参考实现相比vLLM和SGLang，在6轮智能体工作流中每轮快2.1倍，在35轮工作流的中位轮次快4.2倍，端到端耗时减半。优势源于有状态复用和推测而非缓存。

Conclusion: 有状态推理架构通过增量处理和推测解码，能显著提升多智能体工具调用场景下的推理效率，且性能提升来自架构设计而非传统缓存。

Original Abstract: Multi-agent tool calling is becoming the dominant interaction pattern for LLM-based systems, yet existing inference frameworks treat each tool call as an independent request, re-processing the entire conversation from scratch even though 85-95% of the prompt is unchanged from the previous turn.
  We present a stateful inference architecture that converts the $O(n_t)$ per-turn cost of conventional serving into an $O(Δ_t)$ delta-only cost: a persistent KV cache lives across turns and advances by ingesting only the new tokens, while a radix prefix cache extends this across interleaved multi-agent traffic and a prompt-lookup speculative decoder accelerates structured output. Against vLLM and SGLang on novel, fully-generated workloads, the reference implementation is $2.1\times$ faster per turn on a 6-turn agentic workflow and $4.2\times$ on the median turn of a 35-turn one, halving end-to-end wall time. The advantage comes from stateful reuse and speculation, not caching.

中文摘要翻译: 多智能体工具调用正成为基于LLM系统的主导交互模式，然而现有推理框架将每次工具调用视为独立请求，从头重新处理整个对话——尽管85-95%的提示内容与上一轮相比并未改变。我们提出一种有状态推理架构，将传统服务中每轮O(n_t)的计算成本转化为仅需O(Δ_t)的增量成本：持久化KV缓存跨轮次存活，仅通过摄入新令牌来推进；基数前缀缓存将此机制扩展至交错的多智能体流量；提示查找推测解码器加速结构化输出。在与vLLM和SGLang的对比中，我们的参考实现在6轮智能体工作流上每轮快2.1倍，在35轮工作流的中位轮次上快4.2倍，端到端耗时减半。这一优势来源于有状态复用与推测，而非传统缓存。

</details>


### [126] [Curriculum Learning for Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26315)
*Sandeep Kumar,Virginia Smith,Chhavi Yadav*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Staged-Competence课程学习框架，通过难度分级的偏好数据、能力采样和渐进更新参考模型，提升DPO安全对齐的鲁棒性，在三个模型族上平均降低OOD有害响应率16%、攻击成功率20%，且仅需75%训练数据即可达到基线安全水平。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有DPO方法在安全对齐中表现脆弱，缺乏分布外泛化能力，亟需提升其鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Staged-Competence框架，该框架按难度组织偏好数据，采用基于能力的采样策略，并在训练过程中渐进式更新参考模型。

Result: 在三个模型族上平均：OOD有害响应率降低16%，越狱攻击成功率降低20%，几乎零过度拒绝；仅用75%训练数据即可匹配基线安全性能；更好地区分安全与不安全响应。

Conclusion: Staged-Competence能有效提升DPO安全对齐的鲁棒性和泛化能力，且与策略优化损失无关，可扩展至其他DPO变体和对齐领域。

Original Abstract: Direct Preference Optimisation (DPO) is widely used for safety alignment in large language models. However, prior work shows it is brittle and exhibits poor out-of-distribution (OOD) generalisation. In this paper, we investigate whether Curriculum Learning can improve the robustness of DPO-based safety alignment. We propose Staged-Competence, a curriculum-based framework that organises preference data by difficulty, employs competence-based sampling, and progressively updates the reference model during training. Averaged across three model families, Staged-Competence reduces OOD harmful response rates by 16% and jailbreak attack success rates by 20%, while preserving general capabilities with near-zero over-refusal. We further show that Staged-Competence (1) matches baseline safety with only 75% of the training data and (2) yields better separation between safe and unsafe responses. Staged-Competence is agnostic to the policy optimisation loss and can extend to other DPO variants and alignment domains. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/Sandeep5500/curriculum-learning-for-safety.

中文摘要翻译: 直接偏好优化（DPO）被广泛用于大语言模型的安全对齐。然而，先前研究表明DPO方法脆弱且分布外泛化能力差。本文探究课程学习能否提升基于DPO的安全对齐的鲁棒性。我们提出Staged-Competence，一个基于课程的框架，该框架按难度组织偏好数据，采用基于能力的采样，并在训练过程中渐进式更新参考模型。在三个模型族上平均，Staged-Competence将OOD有害响应率降低16%，越狱攻击成功率降低20%，同时几乎零过度拒绝地保持了通用能力。我们进一步证明Staged-Competence（1）仅用75%的训练数据即可匹配基线安全水平，（2）能更好地区分安全与不安全响应。Staged-Competence与策略优化损失无关，可扩展至其他DPO变体和对齐领域。我们的代码和数据可在https://github.com/Sandeep5500/curriculum-learning-for-safety获取。

</details>


### [127] [Semigroup Consistency as a Diagnostic for Learned Physics Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26324)
*Lennon J. Shikhman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出归一化半群误差作为评估学习型物理模拟器长期 rollout 质量的诊断指标，发现其与轨迹退化正相关，但作为训练目标效果有限。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的一步或短时预测误差无法捕捉学习型物理模拟器在时间组合和长期 rollout 中的失效问题，需要更好的诊断指标。

Method: 提出归一化半群误差（Normalized Semigroup Error），通过比较直接演化 s+t 步与先演化 s 步再演化 t 步的预测结果，作为模型无关的后验诊断指标。

Result: 在一维热方程和 Burgers 动力学上，使用时间条件 ConvNet 和 FNO 基线，半群误差与 rollout 退化呈正相关，轨迹级 Spearman 相关系数 ρ=0.635，95% CI [0.621, 0.649]。半群正则化效果不一。

Conclusion: 半群误差主要作为评估诊断工具更有效，而非普适优越的训练目标。

Original Abstract: Learned physics simulators are often evaluated by one-step or short-horizon prediction error, but these metrics can miss failures in temporal composition and long-horizon rollout. For autonomous, state-complete systems, exact solution maps satisfy a semigroup law: direct evolution over $s+t$ should agree with evolution over $s$ followed by $t$. We propose normalized semigroup error as a post hoc, model-agnostic diagnostic comparing these direct and composed learned predictions. On one-dimensional heat and Burgers dynamics with time-conditioned ConvNet and FNO baselines, semigroup error is positively associated with rollout degradation, with trajectory-level Spearman correlation $ρ= 0.635$ and $95%$ CI $[0.621, 0.649]$. Semigroup regularization has mixed effects, supporting semigroup consistency primarily as an evaluation diagnostic rather than a universally beneficial training objective.

中文摘要翻译: 学习型物理模拟器通常通过一步或短时预测误差来评估，但这些指标可能无法捕捉时间组合和长期 rollout 中的失效。对于自主的、状态完整的系统，精确解映射满足半群律：直接演化 s+t 步应与先演化 s 步再演化 t 步的结果一致。我们提出归一化半群误差作为一种后验的、模型无关的诊断指标，用于比较上述直接预测与组合预测的结果。在一维热方程和 Burgers 动力学上，使用时间条件 ConvNet 和 FNO 基线，半群误差与 rollout 退化呈正相关，轨迹级 Spearman 相关系数 ρ=0.635，95% 置信区间 [0.621, 0.649]。半群正则化效果不一，表明半群一致性主要作为评估诊断工具更有价值，而非普适优越的训练目标。

</details>


### [128] [QAM-W: Joint 2D Codebook Quantization for LLM Weights via Hadamard Rotation and Activation-Aware Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26339)
*Preetam Sharma,Kacper Dobek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QAM-W是一种针对权重的正交幅度调制量化编解码器，通过L2归一化、块哈达玛旋转、2D坐标配对和基于单位圆形高斯的Lloyd-Max码本量化，恢复权值行内被标量量化器丢弃的成对坐标结构。在5.5 bpw下保持与BF16差不多的困惑度（±0.4%），匹配SmoothQuant W8A8质量但节省32%比特数。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标量后训练量化器丢弃了权值行内的成对坐标结构（即行内元素间的联合相关性），现有方法无法充分保留这种结构信息，导致量化精度损失。作者希望设计一种编解码器来恢复并利用这种结构，从而在较低比特率下实现高质量量化。

Method: QAM-W方法：对每个权值行进行L2归一化，应用块哈达玛旋转，将元素配对为2D坐标，然后针对单位圆形高斯分布训练单个Lloyd-Max码本进行量化，并结合激活感知的逐通道缩放。

Result: 跨5个LLM（4个系列，1.1B-13B参数）8种量化配置下：5.5 bpw变体在每个模型上困惑度与BF16偏差在±0.4%内，匹配SmoothQuant W8A8但节省32%比特；联合2D编码在等比特率下优于极坐标编码2-15pp ΔPPL；配对KL散度与ΔPPL%的Spearman ρ=0.99。3.5 bpw变体在量化容忍架构上有竞争力，严格4 bpw下QTIP更优。

Conclusion: QAM-W在5-6 bpw波段实现了质量保持的权重量化，通过恢复行内成对结构在高比特率区间取得优异效果，但极低比特率（4 bpw）下不如QTIP，贡献在于该中等比特率区间的性能提升。

Original Abstract: Scalar post-training quantizers discard pairwise coordinate structure within weight rows. We introduce QAM-W (Quadrature Amplitude Modulation for Weights), a codec that recovers this structure: each row is L2-normalized, block-Hadamard rotated, paired into 2D coordinates, and quantized against a single Lloyd-Max codebook trained on the unit circular Gaussian, with activation-aware per-channel scaling. In a cross-model study spanning five LLMs from four families (1.1B--13B parameters) and eight quantized configurations, the activation-aware variant at $\approx 5.5$ bpw stays within $\pm 0.4\%$ of BF16 WikiText-2 perplexity on every model, matching the SmoothQuant W8A8 quality envelope at $32\%$ fewer weight bits. Joint 2D coding outperforms polar (amplitude $\times$ phase) coding by 2--15~pp $Δ$PPL at equal bitrate, and paired KL against BF16 tracks $Δ$PPL\% at Spearman $ρ= 0.99$ across 37 (method, model) rows, consistent with a monotone composite bound from codec distortion to KL divergence. A 3.5~bpw variant is competitive on quantization-tolerant architectures. At strict 4~bpw, the rotated-codebook frontier method QTIP outperforms QAM-W; the contribution is the quality-preserving 5--6~bpw band.

中文摘要翻译: 标量后训练量化器丢弃了权值行内的成对坐标结构。我们提出QAM-W（权重的正交幅度调制），一种恢复该结构的编解码器：对每一行进行L2归一化、块哈达玛旋转、配对为2D坐标，并使用在单位圆形高斯分布上训练的单一Lloyd-Max码本进行量化，同时结合激活感知的逐通道缩放。在涵盖四个系列五种大语言模型（1.1B-13B参数）和八种量化配置的跨模型研究中，激活感知变体在约5.5 bpw下，每个模型的BF16 WikiText-2困惑度偏差保持在±0.4%以内，以少32%的权值比特数匹配了SmoothQuant W8A8的质量包络。在等比特率下，联合2D编码比极坐标（幅度×相位）编码在ΔPPL上优2-15个百分点；在37个（方法，模型）组合上，配对KL与BF16的ΔPPL%的Spearman ρ=0.99，与从编解码器失真到KL散度的单调复合界一致。3.5 bpw变体在量化容忍架构上具有竞争力。在严格的4 bpw下，旋转码本前沿方法QTIP优于QAM-W；本文的贡献在于5-6 bpw质量保持波段。

</details>


### [129] [MechRL: Reinforcement Learning Agents Perform Circuit Discovery for Mechanistic Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26343)
*Barsat Khadka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将电路发现重新表述为强化学习问题：一个PPO智能体在GPT-2 small的144个注意力头上进行离散动作选择，通过对比奖励（目标任务损失与通用预测损失之差）来识别因果非冗余的注意力头。在归纳头（induction）和IOI两个训练任务及一个留出任务（docstring）上，该方法均能匹配或接近oracle性能，且发现的头部与文献中经典因果分析结果高度一致。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的机械可解释性方法在恢复特定任务的电路（circuit）时，通常需要为每个新任务构建定制化的分析流程，缺乏通用性和可迁移性。为了克服这一瓶颈，作者希望探索一种无需人工干预、可跨任务迁移的电路发现方法。

Method: 将电路发现建模为强化学习问题。智能体以GPT-2 small的144个注意力头为离散动作空间，每次动作执行零消融（zero-ablation）并计算对比奖励：目标任务的性能损失减去通用下一个词预测的性能损失。使用PPO算法在多任务向量化环境中训练，同时优化归纳（induction）和IOI两个任务。训练后的策略无需额外任务信号即可直接用于留出任务（docstring）。

Result: （1）单个PPO策略在两个训练任务上达到每幕oracle性能，在留出的docstring任务上也达到oracle水平；（2）智能体优先选择的注意力头与经典文献中经单头消融验证为因果非冗余的头部完全一致，而被文献识别为冗余的头部则被正确降权；（3）在留出任务上，best-of-five规划在无任何任务信号输入的情况下恢复了oracle上限的96%。

Conclusion: 基于因果干预的强化学习是识别机械可解释电路中单头瓶颈的可行且可迁移的方法，与现有的路径修补（path-patching）方法互补。

Original Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability has identified small sets of attention heads that implement specific behaviours in transformer language models, but recovering these circuits typically requires a bespoke analytical pipeline for each new task. We recast circuit discovery as a reinforcement-learning problem. An agent operates over the 144 attention heads of GPT-2 small as a discrete action space; each action triggers a zero-ablation and a contrastive reward that subtracts the ablation's damage to general next-token prediction from its damage to the target task. A single PPO policy, trained on two tasks (induction and IOI) in a vectorised multi-task environment, attains the per-episode oracle on both training tasks and on a held-out third task (docstring completion). Its preferred heads coincide with the canonical heads of established literature on precisely the axes those papers identify as causally non-redundant under single-head ablation; the categories they identify as redundant are correctly de-prioritised by the agent. On the held-out task, best-of-five planning recovers 96\% of the oracle ceiling with no task signal supplied at evaluation. These results indicate that reinforcement learning over causal interventions is a viable, transferable substrate for identifying the single-head bottlenecks of mechanistic circuits, complementary to existing path-patching approaches.

中文摘要翻译: 机械可解释性已经识别出Transformer语言模型中实现特定行为的一小组注意力头，但恢复这些电路通常需要为每个新任务构建定制化的分析流程。我们将电路发现重新表述为一个强化学习问题。一个智能体以GPT-2 small的144个注意力头作为离散动作空间进行操作；每个动作触发一次零消融和一个对比奖励，该奖励从目标任务的性能损伤中减去该消融对通用下一个词预测的损伤。一个单一的PPO策略，在向量化多任务环境中对两个任务（归纳和IOI）进行训练，达到了两个训练任务以及一个留出第三任务（docstring补全）的每幕oracle性能。该策略偏好的头部与既有文献中经典头部恰好一致，且吻合于这些论文在单头消融下识别为因果非冗余的轴线；而被识别为冗余的类别则被智能体正确地降权。在留出任务上，best-of-five规划在评估时未提供任何任务信号的情况下恢复了oracle上限的96%。这些结果表明，基于因果干预的强化学习是一种识别机械电路中单头瓶颈的可行且可迁移的方法，与现有的路径修补方法互补。

</details>


### [130] [When Correct Demonstrations Hurt: Rethinking the Role of Exemplars in In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26350)
*Chenghao Qiu,Chunli Peng,Yufeng Yang,Kuan-Hao Huang,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了上下文学习（ICL）中的一个反直觉现象：正确的示例不一定有用，某些正确示例甚至会降低ICL性能。作者引入“任务保持扰动”框架，系统研究正确性与效用性之间的差距，并形式化为“上下文证据偏移”这一失效模式。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究通常直观地认为，上下文学习中提供正确的输入-输出示例有助于模型表现。本文旨在揭示并研究一个反直觉现象：示例的正确性并不保证其有用性，部分正确示例反而会降低ICL准确率，即存在正确性-效用性差距。

Method: 作者提出“任务保持扰动”框架，该框架仅改变示例输入，同时保持该示例仍是同一任务的正确实例。具体包括两类扰动：(1) 标签更新扰动（label-updating perturbations），即任务相关语义改变后重新计算目标标签；(2) 目标保持扰动（target-preserving perturbations），即原始目标标签仍然有效。作者将该失效模式形式化为“上下文证据偏移”（contextual evidence shift），即任务保持扰动会改变模型进行上下文推理时使用的证据混合比例，从而使示例正确性与效用性分离。

Result: 在情感分类、逻辑推理和数学应用题三个任务上的实验表明，任务保持扰动的示例会显著降低ICL性能，尤其是对于较小规模的模型、较难的任务以及较高的扰动比例。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的上下文学习不仅需要评估示例是否正确，还需要评估它们如何影响上下文推理过程。

Original Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) is often motivated by the intuition that demonstrations help because they provide correct input-output examples. However, we reveal a counterintuitive phenomenon: correctness does not guarantee exemplar utility, and some correct demonstrations can even reduce ICL accuracy. To study this correctness-utility gap, we introduce task-preserving perturbations, where only the exemplar input is changed, while the example remains a correct instance of the same task. Concretely, each perturbed exemplar is assigned the target induced by the task mapping. This framework covers both label-updating perturbations, where task-relevant semantics change and targets are recomputed, and stricter target-preserving perturbations, where the original target remains valid. We formalize the resulting failure mode as contextual evidence shift: task-preserving perturbations can change the effective mixture of evidence used by the model for contextual inference, thereby separating exemplar correctness from exemplar utility. Across sentiment classification, logical reasoning, and math word problems, we find that task-preserving perturbed demonstrations can substantially degrade ICL performance, especially for smaller models, harder tasks, and higher perturbation ratios. Our results show that robust ICL requires evaluating not only whether demonstrations are correct, but also how they influence contextual inference. Code is available at https://github.com/Chenghao-Qiu/Task-Preserving-ICL.

中文摘要翻译: 上下文学习（ICL）通常源于一种直观认识，即示例之所以有帮助是因为它们提供了正确的输入-输出对。然而，我们发现了一个反直觉的现象：正确性并不能保证示例的效用性，有些正确的示例甚至可能降低ICL的准确率。为了研究这种正确性-效用性差距，我们引入了任务保持扰动（task-preserving perturbations），即只改变示例的输入，同时确保该示例仍然是同一任务的正确实例。具体来说，每个被扰动的示例被分配以任务映射所确定的目标标签。该框架涵盖了标签更新扰动（label-updating perturbations，即任务相关语义发生变化后重新计算目标标签）以及更严格的目标保持扰动（target-preserving perturbations，即原始目标标签仍然有效）。我们将由此产生的失效模式形式化为上下文证据偏移（contextual evidence shift）：任务保持扰动可以改变模型用于上下文推理的有效证据混合比例，从而使示例的正确性与效用性分离。在情感分类、逻辑推理和数学应用题上的实验表明，经过任务保持扰动的示例会显著降低ICL性能，尤其是对于较小规模的模型、较难的任务以及较高的扰动比例。我们的结果表明，鲁棒的ICL不仅需要评估示例是否正确，还需要评估它们如何影响上下文推理。代码可在 https://github.com/Chenghao-Qiu/Task-Preserving-ICL 获取。

</details>


### [131] [Balancing Plasticity and Stability with Fast and Slow Successor Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26357)
*Raymond Chua,Doina Precup,Blake Richards*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度强化学习在持续渐变非平稳环境中的适应性，发现稳定性（如突触巩固）比可塑性（如参数重置）更重要，并提出多时间尺度的后继特征巩固方法，在渐变环境中取得优异效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 真实世界环境通常通过持续渐变（gradual drift）而非突变方式演化，但现有RL研究集中于突变式非平稳性。这种区别对"稳定性-可塑性困境"有重要影响，因为突变任务可能需要更多可塑性，而渐变环境可能更依赖稳定性。作者希望探究在持续渐变非平稳环境中，稳定性和适应性如何影响性能。

Method: 1. 修改3D Miniworld和MuJoCo环境，引入自然主义的持续渐变非平稳性；2. 比较稳定性方法（突触巩固）与可塑性方法（参数重置）在渐变环境中的性能；3. 将神经启发的突触巩固应用于后继特征（Successor Features, SFs），并在多个时间尺度上稳定SFs以捕获渐变环境的互补方面。

Result: 1. 稳定性方法（突触巩固）优于可塑性方法（参数重置）；2. 对SFs进行突触巩固比巩固Q值效果更好；3. 多时间尺度巩固SFs能最有效地适应持续渐变环境，不同时间尺度捕获环境变化的不同方面。

Conclusion: 在持续渐变环境中，稳定性比可塑性更为关键；多时间尺度的预测表征巩固是一种有效的持续学习策略，为深度RL在真实非平稳场景中的应用提供了指导。

Original Abstract: A hallmark of intelligence is the ability to adapt in non-stationary environments, yet deep Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents often struggle in such settings. Prior studies introduce non-stationarity through abrupt shifts in features or dynamics, whereas real-world environments often evolve gradually through continual drift. This distinction has important implications for the "stability-plasticity dilemma" in RL, as abrupt task changes may demand more plasticity than naturalistic settings. To address this, we modify existing 3D Miniworld and MuJoCo environments to incorporate naturalistic, continual non-stationarity, and use them to examine how stability and adaptation affect performance under continuous environmental change. We find that methods favoring stability, such as synaptic consolidation, outperform approaches focused on plasticity, such as parameters resetting. Motivated by this result, and prior evidence that Successor Features (SFs) reduce interference, we investigate whether SFs are better consolidation targets than Q-values. Across both environments, applying neuro-inspired synaptic consolidation to SFs yields superior performance on continually changing settings. Moreover, consolidation is most effective when SFs are stabilized across multiple timescales, which capture complementary aspects of gradual environmental change. Together, these results suggest that stability is more critical in continual learning when changes are gradual, and that multi-timescale consolidation of predictive representations is an effective approach.

中文摘要翻译: 智能的一个标志性特征是在非平稳环境中适应的能力，然而深度强化学习（RL）智能体在此类环境中常常表现不佳。先前的研究通过特征的突变或动力学突变来引入非平稳性，而真实世界环境通常通过持续渐变逐步演化。这一区别对RL中的"稳定性-可塑性困境"具有重要意义，因为突变的任务变化可能比自然场景需要更多的可塑性。为解决这一问题，我们修改了现有的3D Miniworld和MuJoCo环境，融入了自然主义的持续非平稳性，并利用它们来考察在持续环境变化下，稳定性和适应如何影响性能。我们发现，偏好稳定性的方法（如突触巩固）优于侧重可塑性的方法（如参数重置）。受此结果以及后继特征（SFs）能减少干扰的已有证据启发，我们探究了SFs是否比Q值更适合作为巩固目标。在两个环境中，将神经启发的突触巩固应用于SFs，在持续变化的环境下均取得了更优的性能。此外，当在多个时间尺度上稳定SFs时，巩固最为有效，这些时间尺度捕捉了渐进环境变化的互补方面。综合来看，这些结果表明，在变化逐渐发生时，稳定性在持续学习中更为关键，而多时间尺度的预测表征巩固是一种有效的方法。

</details>


### [132] [Online Learning on Hidden-Convex Losses via Algorithmic Equivalence: Optimal Regret, Geometric Barrier, and Bandit Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26373)
*Anas Barakat,Andreas Kontogiannis,Vasilis Pollatos,Ioannis Panageas,Antonios Varvitsiotis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在隐凸损失假设下，在线梯度下降（OGD）可以达到最优的$\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$遗憾界，回答了Ghai等人的开放问题；同时提出了Hessian相容性条件替代对角雅可比条件，扩展了可允许重参数化类别，并给出了紧的下界分析。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Ghai等人(2022)证明了隐凸损失下OGD可实现$\mathcal{O}(T^{2/3})$遗憾，但最优的$\Theta(\sqrt{T})$遗憾是否可达是开放问题。本文旨在回答这一问题，同时澄清算法等价性所需的几何条件，并扩展可允许重参数化类别。

Method: 通过更精细的离散时间算法等价性论证，证明OGD在隐凸损失下等价于OMD在底层凸损失上的优化；提出Hessian相容性条件（必要性且充分性）替代原有的对角雅可比充分条件；扩展分析到单点bandit反馈场景。

Result: (1) OGD在隐凸损失下达到$\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$遗憾，匹配最优最坏情况界；(2) 提出Hessian相容性条件替代对角雅可比条件，扩大重参数化类别；(3) 给出$\Omega(T)$下界，证明Hessian相容性是OGD的必要条件；(4) bandit OGD达到$\mathcal{O}(T^{3/4})$期望遗憾界。

Conclusion: 隐凸损失设置下OGD可以恢复在线凸优化的最优遗憾界，且Hessian相容性是算法等价性的关键几何条件；该结果填补了隐凸在线学习领域的重要理论空白。

Original Abstract: We study adversarial online learning with hidden-convex losses, i.e., nonconvex losses that become convex after a nonlinear reparameterization. Ghai, Lu and Hazan (2022) proved that, under geometric and smoothness assumptions, online gradient descent (OGD) on such nonconvex losses approximately simulates online mirror descent (OMD) on the underlying convex losses with a suitable regularizer, yielding $\mathcal{O}(T^{2/3})$ regret. They left open whether the optimal $Θ(\sqrt{T})$ regret from online convex optimization can be recovered in this hidden-convex setting. We answer this question affirmatively. More specifically, via a sharper discrete-time algorithmic equivalence argument, we prove that OGD achieves $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$ regret under the same assumptions, matching the optimal worst-case rate for adversarial online convex optimization. We also address another open question of Ghai, Lu and Hazan (2022) by clarifying the geometry required for this algorithmic equivalence. We replace the diagonal-Jacobian sufficient condition with a necessary-and-sufficient Hessian compatibility condition, thereby expanding the class of admissible reparameterizations. We complement our tight regret bound with a lower bound showing that the Hessian compatibility assumption is essential for OGD; when it fails, we construct a smooth reparameterization and an adversarial sequence of hidden-convex losses for which OGD suffers $Ω(T)$ regret. Finally, we extend our analysis to one-point bandit feedback and prove a $\mathcal{O}(T^{3/4})$ expected regret bound for bandit OGD with spherical smoothing, matching its classical rate on convex losses.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究具有隐凸损失的对抗性在线学习，即经过非线性重参数化后变为凸的非凸损失。Ghai, Lu和Hazan (2022)证明，在几何和光滑性假设下，此类非凸损失上的在线梯度下降(OGD)近似模拟了具有合适正则化项的底层凸损失上的在线镜像下降(OMD)，实现了$\mathcal{O}(T^{2/3})$遗憾。他们留下了开放问题：在线凸优化中最优的$\Theta(\sqrt{T})$遗憾是否能在隐凸设置中恢复。我们肯定地回答了这个问题。具体而言，通过更精细的离散时间算法等价性论证，我们证明在相同假设下OGD实现了$\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})$遗憾，匹配了对抗性在线凸优化的最优最坏情况界。我们还解决了Ghai, Lu和Hazan (2022)的另一个开放问题，澄清了该算法等价性所需的几何条件。我们用必要且充分的Hessian相容性条件替代了对角雅可比充分条件，从而扩展了可允许重参数化类别。我们通过下界补充了紧的遗憾界：当Hessian相容性不成立时，我们构造了一个光滑重参数化和一个对抗性的隐凸损失序列，使得OGD遭受$\Omega(T)$遗憾。最后，我们将分析扩展到单点bandit反馈，证明了具有球形平滑的bandit OGD的$\mathcal{O}(T^{3/4})$期望遗憾界，匹配了其在凸损失上的经典界。

</details>


### [133] [Function-Valued Causal Influence in Nonlinear Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26408)
*Valentina V. Kuskova,Dmitry Zaytsev,Michael Coppedge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出时间序列因果发现中，使用非线性模型得到的因果效应通常被压缩为标量边分数，这丢失了重要的状态依赖信息。作者提出了基于函数值因果影响的框架，利用个体条件期望（ICE）从训练好的神经加性向量自回归模型中估计因果响应函数，实验表明标量分数无法区分单调、阈值、饱和及符号翻转等不同功能行为。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有非线性时间序列因果发现方法几乎总是将因果关系统计为标量边分数，这忽略了非线性模型本质上学习的是状态依赖的函数，其效应随机制、幅度和上下文变化。标量因果分数构成了严重的信息瓶颈，混淆了状态间变化与状态内残差噪声。

Method: 以神经加性向量自回归（NAVAR）为代表架构，基于个体条件期望（ICE）构建了一个直接从训练好的模型中估计因果响应函数的实用框架。

Result: 通过受控合成实验证明，标量分数无法区分的边可能具有单调、阈值、饱和及符号翻转等截然不同的函数行为；在民主发展的应用案例中，函数值分析揭示了分数中心方法系统遗漏的特定机制和非对称因果结构。

Conclusion: 应放弃用标量分数总结非线性因果关系的做法，转而采用函数值因果分析来捕捉更丰富、更真实的因果结构。

Original Abstract: Causal discovery in time series is increasingly performed using nonlinear machine-learning models, yet the resulting causal relationships are almost always summarized by scalar edge scores. We argue that this practice obscures the true object learned by nonlinear autoregressive models: a state-dependent function whose effect varies across regimes, magnitudes, and contexts. We formalize function-valued causal influence for additive, contribution-decomposable architectures and show that scalar causal scores constitute a severe information bottleneck, conflating between-state variation with within-state residual noise. Using Neural Additive Vector Autoregression as a representative architecture, we introduce a practical framework based on Individual Conditional Expectation for estimating causal response functions directly from trained models. Through controlled synthetic experiments, we demonstrate that edges with indistinguishable scalar scores can exhibit qualitatively different functional behaviors, including monotonic, thresholded, saturating, and sign-changing effects. An applied case study on democratic development further shows that function-valued analysis reveals regime-specific and asymmetric causal structure systematically missed by score-centric approaches.

中文摘要翻译: 时间序列中的因果发现越来越多地使用非线性机器学习模型进行，但由此产生的因果关系几乎总是被总结为标量边分数。我们认为，这种做法掩盖了非线性自回归模型真正学习到的对象：一个状态依赖的函数，其效应随机制、幅度和上下文而变化。我们为加性的、贡献可分解的架构形式化了函数值因果影响，并表明标量因果分数构成了严重的信息瓶颈，混淆了状态间变化与状态内残差噪声。以神经加性向量自回归作为代表性架构，我们引入了一个基于个体条件期望的实用框架，用于直接从训练好的模型中估计因果响应函数。通过受控合成实验，我们证明具有无法区分的标量分数的边可以表现出性质不同的函数行为，包括单调、阈值、饱和和符号翻转效应。一个关于民主发展的应用案例进一步表明，函数值分析揭示了分数中心方法系统遗漏的特定机制和非对称因果结构。

</details>


### [134] [When Does Deep RL Beat Calibrated Baselines? A Benchmark Study on Adaptive Resource Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26418)
*Guilin Zhang,Chuanyi Sun,Kai Zhao,Shahryar Sarkani,John Fossaceca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过构建RLScale-Bench基准测试，发现校准后的基于规则的自适应伸缩器在成本上优于六种主流深度强化学习算法，指出DRL在资源控制中的瓶颈不在于算法选择，而在于基线校准、奖励工程和评估协议。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究深度强化学习在自适应资源控制中是否真的有效，以及在何种场景下能发挥优势，挑战常见假设。

Method: 构建RLScale-Bench可重复基准测试和评估协议，在匹配的架构、训练预算和奖励函数下，将PPO、DQN、A2C、SAC、TD3、DDPG六种DRL算法与校准后的基于规则基线进行对比，在Kubernetes水平Pod自动伸缩上实例化，测试六种工作负载模式和分布偏移泛化能力。

Result: (i) 校准后的规则控制器在所有六种工作负载上成本最低，但在突发流量场景下落后于最佳RL智能体；(ii) 离散动作算法在约束违反方面优于连续动作算法一到两个数量级；(iii) 没有单一算法在所有工作负载上占优，排名波动可达四个位置。

Conclusion: 基于RL的资源控制瓶颈不在于算法选择，而在于基线校准、奖励工程和现实评估协议。

Original Abstract: A properly calibrated rule-based autoscaler can beat every one of six mainstream deep reinforcement learning (DRL) algorithms on cost across every workload we test - so when, if ever, does DRL actually help? We study this in RLScale-Bench, a reproducible benchmark and evaluation protocol for DRL on adaptive resource control, where an agent allocates compute to a dynamic workload under cost and service-level constraints. We evaluate PPO, DQN, A2C, SAC, TD3, and DDPG under matched architectures, training budgets, and reward functions against a calibrated rule-based baseline across six workload patterns and five seeds (240 runs), instantiate the benchmark on Kubernetes Horizontal Pod Autoscaling, and probe distribution-shift generalization. Three findings challenge common assumptions: (i) the calibrated controller achieves the lowest cost on all six workloads, though it trails the best RL agents on bursty and flash traffic; (ii) discrete-action algorithms outperform continuous-action ones by one to two orders of magnitude in constraint violations due to action-space mismatch; and (iii) no single algorithm dominates across workloads, with rankings shifting by up to four positions. The bottleneck in RL-based resource control is not algorithm selection but baseline calibration, reward engineering, and realistic evaluation protocols.

中文摘要翻译: 一个经恰当校准的基于规则的自适应伸缩器，在我们测试的每一种工作负载上，其成本都能击败六种主流深度强化学习（DRL）算法。那么，深度强化学习究竟何时（如果有的话）能真正发挥作用？我们在RLScale-Bench中研究这一问题，这是一个针对自适应资源控制中深度强化学习的可重复基准测试与评估协议，其中智能体在成本和服务等级约束下为动态工作负载分配计算资源。我们在匹配的架构、训练预算和奖励函数下，将PPO、DQN、A2C、SAC、TD3和DDPG与经校准的基于规则基线进行对比，涵盖六种工作负载模式和五个随机种子（共240次运行），在Kubernetes水平Pod自动伸缩上实例化该基准测试，并探究分布偏移下的泛化能力。三个发现挑战了常见假设：(i) 经校准的控制器在所有六种工作负载上实现了最低成本，但在突发流量和闪电流量上落后于最佳RL智能体；(ii) 由于动作空间不匹配，离散动作算法在约束违反方面比连续动作算法优一到两个数量级；(iii) 没有单一算法在所有工作负载上占优，排名波动可达四个位置。基于RL的资源控制瓶颈不在于算法选择，而在于基线校准、奖励工程和现实评估协议。

</details>


### [135] [Aperiodic and Low-Frequency Spectral Bias in Reconstruction based EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26434)
*Aditya Kommineni,Emily Zhou,Kleanthis Avramidis,Simon Bock Segaard,Jeppe Roden Münster,Andreas Peter Juhl Hansen,Takfarinas Medani,Tiantian Feng,Richard Leahy,Shrikanth Narayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基于重构的EEG基础模型在低资源场景下表现不佳的根本原因：其嵌入表征偏向于捕获EEG信号中的非周期成分（高功率），而低估了振荡成分（低功率、高频），导致模型更多地编码受试者身份而非任务相关信息。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 解释EEG基础模型在低资源场景下表现不如小型监督模型的原因，揭示其表征学习中的根本性缺陷。

Method: 通过控制变量法使用合成生成的EEG输入进行机制分析，并结合线性探测评估在真实BCI数据集上进行验证。

Result: 1）EEG基础模型嵌入偏向捕获非周期成分，低估振荡成分（尤其是高频）；2）嵌入编码中受试者身份信息强于任务相关信息，强化了低频和非周期成分的偏差。

Conclusion: 基于重构的EEG基础模型存在系统性失败模式，未来工作需要引入明确针对高频振荡结构的辅助损失函数，以构建更强大、更可泛化的EEG表征。

Original Abstract: EEG foundation models, pre-trained on large-scale unlabelled EEG data, have emerged as a promising direction towards learning generalizable EEG representations. Despite showing positive results in data-rich regimes, they often fail to outperform significantly smaller supervised models in low-resource settings compared to fully supervised models. We provide a mechanistic account of this shortcoming, attributing it to a fundamental mismatch between reconstruction-based pretext tasks and the idiosyncratic spectral structure of EEG signals, which decompose into distinct high-power aperiodic and low-power oscillatory components. Using controlled, synthetically-generated EEG inputs, we demonstrate that EEG foundation model embeddings are biased to capture the aperiodic components of the EEG signal while under-representing oscillatory components, particularly at higher frequencies. Additionally, linear probe evaluations on real-world BCI datasets further reveal that embeddings encode subject identity more strongly than task-relevant information, thereby reinforcing the low-frequency and aperiodic component bias in foundation model embeddings trained primarily on reconstruction based objectives. Together, these findings elucidate a failure mode in reconstruction based EEG foundation models and motivate future work to incorporate auxiliary losses explicitly targeting high-frequency oscillatory structure as a path toward more capable and generalizable EEG representations.

中文摘要翻译: 在大规模无标签EEG数据上预训练的EEG基础模型，已成为学习可泛化EEG表征的一个有前景的方向。尽管在数据充裕的场景下展现出积极效果，但在低资源条件下，与全监督模型相比，它们往往显著逊色于规模更小的监督模型。我们对此不足提供了一种机制性解释，将其归因于基于重构的预训练任务与EEG信号特有的频谱结构之间的根本性错配——EEG信号可分解为高功率的非周期成分和低功率的振荡成分。通过使用受控的合成生成EEG输入，我们证明了EEG基础模型的嵌入偏向于捕获EEG信号中的非周期成分，而低估了振荡成分，尤其是在较高频率上。此外，在真实BCI数据集上的线性探测评估进一步揭示，嵌入对受试者身份的编码强于对任务相关信息的编码，从而强化了主要基于重构目标训练的EEG基础模型中的低频和非周期成分偏差。综上，这些发现阐明了基于重构的EEG基础模型中的一种失败模式，并为未来工作提供了动机——即引入明确针对高频振荡结构的辅助损失函数，作为通往更强大、更可泛化的EEG表征的路径。

</details>


### [136] [MuCon: Clipped Muon Updates for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26459)
*Albert Yi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究MuCon优化器，它是Muon优化器的变体，通过对Muon矩阵进行奇异值裁剪（MClip_τ）来替代极分解。论文探讨了无需完整稠密SVD即可近似实现MuCon裁剪步骤的条件，并指出了数值不稳定的来源——接近阈值的奇异值会导致符号判断和有理求解病态。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Muon风格的优化器通过将矩阵值动量或预处理更新替换为其规范部分极因子来工作，而MuCon变体引入了奇异值裁剪。本文的核心动机是探索是否可以在不执行完整稠密SVD的情况下高效近似MuCon的裁剪步骤，从而降低计算开销。

Method: 记录了两个精确恒等式：极/绝对值公式和标量根公式，并导出了一个用于裁剪半正定因子的有理牛顿滤波器。分析了两种方法共同的数值障碍：接近阈值的奇异值会导致符号决策和有理求解变得病态。

Result: 矩阵函数方法仅在配合稳定的极/平方根原语或裁剪边界附近的显式正则化时才有效。接近阈值的奇异值会引发严重的数值病态问题。

Conclusion: 要实现MuCon裁剪步骤的高效近似，必须采用稳定的极分解或平方根数值原语，并在裁剪边界附近引入显式正则化，否则直接使用矩阵函数方法不可靠。

Original Abstract: Muon-style optimizers take a matrix-valued momentum or preconditioned update $B = U \operatorname{diag}(σ_1,\ldots,σ_r) V^\top$ and replace it with its canonical partial polar factor $\operatorname{Pol}(B) = U V^\top$. This maps every nonzero singular value to one. MuCon is the clipped-Muon variant studied here: it applies singular-value clipping to the same Muon matrix, $D^{\mathrm{MuCon}}\_τ(B) = \operatorname{MClip}\_τ(B) = U \operatorname{diag}\bigl(\min\{σ\_i,τ\}\bigr) V^\top, \qquad τ> 0$. Thus, $\operatorname{MClip}\_τ$ denotes the mathematical clipping operator, while MuCon denotes the optimizer primitive that substitutes this clipped direction for Muon's polar direction. The Muon/MuCon scaling parameterization used in this work is called $\text{SpectralP}$: it is the hidden-matrix scaling recipe under which polar Muon or clipped MuCon directions are applied. The map $\operatorname{MClip}\_τ$ is the Frobenius projection onto the spectral-norm ball $\{X : \|X\|_2 \le τ\}$: it leaves singular values at or below $τ$ unchanged and modifies only the violating singular directions. This paper asks when the MuCon clipping step can be approximated without a full dense SVD. We record two exact identities, a polar/absolute-value formula and a scalar-root formulation leading to a rational Newton filter for the clipped positive-semidefinite factor, and identify the numerical obstruction common to both: singular values near the threshold make sign decisions and rational solves ill-conditioned. Matrix-function methods are therefore useful only when paired with stable polar/square-root primitives or explicit regularization near the clipping boundary.

中文摘要翻译: Muon风格的优化器采用矩阵值动量或预处理更新 $B = U \operatorname{diag}(σ_1,\ldots,σ_r) V^\top$，并将其替换为规范部分极因子 $\operatorname{Pol}(B) = U V^\top$。这一操作将所有非零奇异值映射为1。MuCon是本文研究的裁剪-Muon变体：它对相同的Muon矩阵应用奇异值裁剪，$D^{\mathrm{MuCon}}_τ(B) = \operatorname{MClip}_τ(B) = U \operatorname{diag}\bigl(\min\{σ_i,τ\}\bigr) V^\top, \qquad τ> 0$。因此，$\operatorname{MClip}_τ$表示数学裁剪算子，而MuCon表示优化器原语，它将此裁剪方向替代Muon的极方向。本文使用的Muon/MuCon缩放参数化称为$\text{SpectralP}$：这是一种隐藏矩阵缩放策略，在该策略下应用极Muon或裁剪MuCon方向。映射$\operatorname{MClip}_τ$是到谱范数球$\{X : \|X\|_2 \le τ\}$的Frobenius投影：它保持不超过$τ$的奇异值不变，仅修改违反约束的奇异方向。本文探讨何时可以在无需完整稠密SVD的情况下近似MuCon裁剪步骤。我们记录了两个精确恒等式——一个极/绝对值公式和一个标量根公式，进而导出了用于裁剪半正定因子的有理牛顿滤波器，并指出了两者共有的数值障碍：接近阈值的奇异值使符号决策和有理求解变得病态。因此，矩阵函数方法仅在配合稳定的极/平方根原语或裁剪边界附近的显式正则化时才有用。

</details>


### [137] [Diffuse to Detect: Generative Diffusion Models for Unsupervised IC Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26468)
*Yuxuan Yin,Chen He,Todd Jacobs,Jialei He,Boxun Xu,Robert Jin,Peng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出首个基于扩散Transformer的无监督异常检测框架，用于半导体晶圆级潜在缺陷筛查，在16nm IC测试数据上取得最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有潜在缺陷筛查面临极低失效率、高维测试数据以及缺乏标注异常样本三大挑战，亟需无需标签和人工特征工程的无监督检测方法。

Method: 首先利用自编码器压缩原始测试数据，然后将其重塑为结构化token序列，并加入正弦位置编码和每设备晶圆位置嵌入；采用扩散Transformer计算中程扩散时间步上的噪声预测误差作为异常分数，实现无标签快速晶圆级筛查。

Result: 在工业16nm IC测试数据上，在极端类别不平衡条件下取得了最先进的检测性能，并能通过潜在空间重建残差实现可解释的故障定位。

Conclusion: 所提无监督框架有效解决了半导体制造中潜在缺陷筛查的关键难题，兼顾了高检测性能和可解释性。

Original Abstract: Latent defect screening is challenged by extremely low failure rates, high-dimensional test data, and absence of labeled anomalies. We propose the first unsupervised anomaly detection framework incorporating a Diffusion Transformer. Raw test measurements are first compressed by an autoencoder, then reshaped into a structured token sequence enriched with sinusoidal and per-device wafer-position embeddings. Anomaly scores are derived from the noise-prediction error over mid-range diffusion timesteps, enabling fast wafer-scale screening without any labeled defects or manual feature engineering. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance on industrial 16nm IC test data under extreme class imbalance, offering interpretable failure localization through latent-space reconstruction residuals.

中文摘要翻译: 潜在缺陷筛查面临着极低失效率、高维测试数据和缺乏标注异常样本的挑战。我们提出了首个融合扩散Transformer的无监督异常检测框架。原始测试测量数据首先经过自编码器压缩，然后被重塑为结构化的token序列，并辅以正弦位置编码和每设备晶圆位置嵌入。异常分数通过中程扩散时间步上的噪声预测误差导出，从而无需任何标注缺陷或人工特征工程即可实现快速晶圆级筛查。我们的方法在极端类别不平衡条件下的工业16nm IC测试数据上取得了最先进的性能，并通过潜在空间重建残差提供可解释的故障定位。

</details>


### [138] [Extra-Merge: Tracing the Rank-1 Subspace of Model Merging in Language Model Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26484)
*Wenjie Zhou,Bohan Wang,Hongtao Zhang,Chenxi Jia,Wei Chen,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型在预训练后期存在“秩1子空间”现象：虽然原始优化步骤剧烈震荡，但连续合并检查点坍缩到近似一维的稳定流形上。基于此提出Extra-Merge，一种无需训练的外推策略，沿该子空间外推以降低损失，在GPT-2和LLaMA系列上优于标准合并方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 模型合并虽已广泛用于增强大语言模型，但其底层机制尚不清晰。作者希望从预训练后期的优化轨迹中揭示合并为何有效，从而设计更优的无需额外训练的合并策略。

Method: 提出Extra-Merge方法：首先发现连续合并检查点形成近似一维的线性子空间（秩1子空间），基于“河-谷”景观分析将合并理解为几何低通滤波器，消除高曲率噪声以揭示最优下降方向；然后沿该子空间进行外推，无需额外梯度更新即可降低损失。

Result: 在GPT-2和LLaMA系列（124M到2B）上，Extra-Merge始终优于标准模型合并基线；在Pythia-12B下游任务上取得一致的零样本准确率提升；该方法还能有效泛化到Muon优化器。

Conclusion: Extra-Merge是一种无需训练的轻量级模型增强策略，通过沿预训练后期出现的秩1子空间外推有效降低损失，为理解模型合并机制提供了理论视角，并在多个规模的语言模型上验证了其有效性。

Original Abstract: Model merging has emerged as a lightweight paradigm for enhancing Large Language Models (LLMs), yet its underlying mechanisms remain poorly understood. In this work, we analyze late-stage pre-training trajectories and uncover a \textbf{Rank-1 Subspace} phenomenon: while raw optimization steps oscillate violently, consecutive \emph{merged} checkpoints collapse onto a stable, approximately one-dimensional linear manifold. We theoretically ground this observation in a \emph{river-valley} landscape analysis: averaging acts as a geometric low-pass filter that dampens high-curvature noise to reveal the optimal descent direction. Capitalizing on this insight, we propose \textbf{Extra-Merge}, a training-free strategy that extrapolates along this subspace to minimize loss without additional gradient updates. Extensive experiments across GPT-2 and LLaMA families (124M to 2B) demonstrate that Extra-Merge consistently outperforms standard merging baselines. Notably, it yields consistent zero-shot accuracy gains on Pythia-12B downstream tasks and generalizes effectively to the Muon optimizer \citep{jordan2024muon}.

中文摘要翻译: 模型合并已成为增强大语言模型（LLMs）的一种轻量级范式，但其底层机制仍不清晰。在本文中，我们分析了预训练后期的轨迹，并发现了一个\textbf{秩1子空间}现象：虽然原始优化步骤剧烈震荡，但连续的\textemph{合并}检查点坍缩到一条稳定的、近似一维的线性流形上。我们从理论上将这一观察归结为\textemph{河-谷}景观分析：平均操作作为一种几何低通滤波器，抑制高曲率噪声，从而揭示最优下降方向。基于这一洞见，我们提出了\textbf{Extra-Merge}，一种无需训练的策略，沿该子空间外推以最小化损失，而无需额外的梯度更新。在GPT-2和LLaMA系列（124M到2B）上的大量实验表明，Extra-Merge始终优于标准合并基线。值得注意的是，它在Pythia-12B下游任务上取得了一致的零样本准确率提升，并能有效泛化至Muon优化器（\citep{jordan2024muon}）。

</details>


### [139] [The Stability of Singular Distribution: A Spectral Perspective on the Two-Phase Dynamics of Language Model Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26489)
*Hongtao Zhang,Wenjie Zhou,Chenxi Jia,Wei Chen,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现大语言模型预训练中存在一个称为“奇异分布稳定性（SoSD）”的谱现象：迹归一化奇异值谱在早期就趋于稳定，即使参数矩阵仍在演化。SoSD与慢速下降阶段的同步在各种架构和设置中普遍存在。通过简化Transformer分析，证明权重范数的增长必然导致早期SoSD阈值，之后损失下降率受奇异分布变化的理论约束。WSD和Muon等策略可通过调节SoSD尺度来影响训练效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 揭示大语言模型预训练两阶段现象（快速初始损失下降后进入缓慢改进）背后的根本机制，从谱角度理解训练动力学，并解释不同学习率调度和优化器对训练效率的影响。

Method: 1) 提出奇异分布稳定性（SoSD）概念，观察迹归一化奇异值谱的演化；2) 在GPT-2、LLaMA等多种架构和不同调度、优化器设置下验证SoSD的普适性；3) 通过简化Transformer进行理论分析，建立权重范数增长与SoSD阈值的关系；4) 从SoSD角度解释WSD、Muon等策略的有效性。

Result: 1) SoSD现象在多种架构（GPT-2、LLaMA）、调度（Step-wise、WSD、Cosine Decay）、权重衰减和优化器（AdamW、Muon）下普遍存在；2) 理论证明权重范数增长必然导致早期SoSD阈值，之后损失下降率受奇异分布变化的理论上限约束；3) WSD和Muon等策略可通过调节SoSD尺度来提升预训练效率。

Conclusion: SoSD为大语言模型预训练的两阶段动力学提供了统一的谱视角解释，揭示了奇异谱稳定性与损失下降率之间的理论关系，并为理解高效预训练策略（如WSD和Muon）提供了新的谱分析框架。

Original Abstract: Large language model pre-training typically exhibits a two-phase trajectory: a fast initial loss drop followed by a prolonged slow improvement. We identify an underlying spectral phenomenon, Stability of Singular Distribution (SoSD), where the trace-normalized singular value spectrum stabilizes early, even as parameter matrices continue to evolve. We demonstrate that synchronization between SoSD and the slow-descent regime is widely observed across diverse architectures (GPT-2, LLaMA) and settings, including various schedules (Step-wise, WSD, Cosine Decay), weight decays, and optimizers (AdamW, Muon). By analyzing a simplified Transformer, we prove that growing weight norms inevitably precipitate an early SoSD threshold, after which the rate of loss decrease becomes theoretically bounded by the variation in the singular distribution. We further interpret strategies like WSD and Muon through their ability to modulate the SoSD scale, offering a spectral lens for understanding efficient pre-training dynamics.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型预训练通常呈现出两阶段轨迹：快速初始损失下降后紧随一个漫长的缓慢改进阶段。我们识别出一个潜在的谱现象——奇异分布稳定性（Stability of Singular Distribution, SoSD），其中迹归一化奇异值谱在早期就趋于稳定，即使参数矩阵仍在持续演化。我们证明，SoSD与慢速下降阶段之间的同步现象在多种架构（GPT-2、LLaMA）和设置中广泛存在，包括不同的调度策略（Step-wise、WSD、Cosine Decay）、权重衰减以及优化器（AdamW、Muon）。通过分析一个简化Transformer，我们证明了权重范数的增长必然诱发一个早期SoSD阈值，此后的损失下降速率在理论上受限于奇异分布的变化量。我们进一步解释了WSD和Muon等策略通过调节SoSD尺度来发挥作用，从而为理解高效预训练动力学提供了一个谱视角。

</details>


### [140] [Beyond Pairwise Preferences: Listwise Reward-Aware Alignment for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26491)
*Austin Wang,Jiaqi Han,Stefano Ermon,Yisong Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Diffusion LAIR，一种基于奖励感知的列表级偏好优化方法，通过将多张候选图像的奖励分数转化为优势权重，并优化带二次惩罚的隐式奖励回归目标，从而在文本到图像扩散模型对齐中超越现有的成对偏好优化方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有偏好优化方法将监督信号简化为二元成对比较（赢家/输家），未能充分利用训练数据中同一提示对应多张候选图像以及连续奖励分数比单一胜负标签包含更丰富信息的特点。

Method: 对于每个提示，将一组候选图像的奖励分数转化为中心化的优势权重，然后优化一个关于隐式奖励（定义为当前模型相对于固定参考模型的去噪损失改进）的优势加权回归目标，并添加二次惩罚项以正则化隐式奖励的幅度。该方法同时使用所有候选图像而非选择成对组合，通过控制隐式奖励幅度保持保守性。

Result: 在SD1.5和SDXL上，Diffusion LAIR在文本到图像生成、组合生成和图像编辑基准测试中均优于强偏好优化基线方法。该目标在隐式奖励空间中存在有界闭式最优解，阐明了正则化强度如何控制偏好更新的幅度。

Conclusion: 列表级偏好优化方法Diffusion LAIR通过同时利用多张候选图像和连续奖励分数，并配合隐式奖励正则化，为扩散模型对齐提供了一种更有效且理论上清晰的偏好优化范式。

Original Abstract: Preference optimization has emerged as an efficient alternative to online reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) for aligning text-to-image diffusion models. However, existing methods largely reduce supervision to binary pairwise comparisons. This pairwise reduction is limiting when training data naturally contains multiple candidate images for the same prompt, and when continuous reward scores can provide richer information than a single winner-loser label. To address these limitations, we propose Diffusion LAIR, a reward-aware listwise preference optimization method for diffusion models. For each prompt, LAIR converts reward scores across a group of candidate images into centered advantage weights, then optimizes an advantage-weighted regression objective on the implicit reward, defined as the denoising-loss improvement of the current model over a fixed reference model, with a quadratic penalty that regularizes the magnitude of the implicit reward. The resulting objective uses all candidates simultaneously rather than selecting pairs, and remains conservative by explicitly controlling the magnitude of the implicit reward. The LAIR objective admits a bounded closed-form optimum in implicit-reward space, clarifying how the regularization strength controls the magnitude of the preference update. Experiments show that Diffusion LAIR outperforms strong preference optimization baselines on SD1.5 and SDXL across text-to-image generation, compositional generation, and image editing benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 偏好优化已成为从人类反馈中在线强化学习（RLHF）的一种有效替代方案，用于对齐文本到图像扩散模型。然而，现有方法大多将监督信号简化为二元成对比较。当训练数据自然包含同一提示的多张候选图像，且连续奖励分数能比单一胜负标签提供更丰富的信息时，这种成对简化具有局限性。为解决这些问题，我们提出Diffusion LAIR，一种用于扩散模型的奖励感知列表级偏好优化方法。对于每个提示，LAIR将一组候选图像的奖励分数转化为中心化的优势权重，然后在隐式奖励上优化一个优势加权回归目标，该隐式奖励定义为当前模型相对于固定参考模型的去噪损失改进，并加入二次惩罚项以正则化隐式奖励的幅度。所得目标同时使用所有候选图像而非选择成对组合，并通过显式控制隐式奖励幅度保持保守性。LAIR目标在隐式奖励空间中具有有界闭式最优解，阐明了正则化强度如何控制偏好更新的幅度。实验表明，在SD1.5和SDXL上，Diffusion LAIR在文本到图像生成、组合生成和图像编辑基准测试中均优于强偏好优化基线方法。

</details>


### [141] [Dense2MoE: Pushing the Pareto Frontier of On-Device LLMs via Unified Pruning and Upcycling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26496)
*Fengfa Li,Hongjin Ji,Yifeng Ding,Lei Ren,Chen Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Dense2MoE框架，通过层融合升级（LF-UC）统一剪枝和升级策略，将冗余层的注意力模块剪除，将其MLP重用于MoE专家，从而高效地将稠密LLM转换为适合端侧部署的MoE模型，在推理延迟与模型精度的帕累托前沿上显著超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: MoE架构在资源受限的端侧部署中极具潜力，但从头训练成本过高；现有升级（upcycling）方法引入参数冗余降低推理效率，而层剪枝虽能缓解冗余但会损害模型精度。亟需一种既能减少冗余又能保持性能的方法。

Method: 提出Dense2MoE框架，核心是层融合升级（Layer Fusion UpCycling, LF-UC）：基于硬件Roofline理论，从冗余层中剪除带宽密集的注意力模块，同时将其MLP层重用作MoE专家，通过选择性token路由严格限制激活参数数量。

Result: 大量实验表明，Dense2MoE显著推进了端侧推理延迟与模型精度的帕累托前沿，在性能上优于稠密基线、最先进的压缩方法以及标准的升级方法。

Conclusion: Dense2MoE通过融合剪枝与升级的策略，以适度的持续预训练预算，能够高效地将公开可用的稠密大语言模型转换为适合端侧部署的MoE模型，有效解决了推理内存墙问题。

Original Abstract: The Mixture of Experts MoE architecture is highly promising for resource constrained on device deployments yet training these models from scratch incurs prohibitive costs Current methods attempt to alleviate this by upcycling dense models into MoEs however they often introduce parameter redundancy that degrades inference efficiency Alternatively standard layer pruning mitigates redundancy but inevitably compromises model accuracy To resolve this dilemma we propose Dense2MoE a novel framework that unifies pruning and upcycling through Layer Fusion UpCycling LF UC Guided by hardware Roofline theory Dense2MoE systematically overcomes the inference memory wall by pruning bandwidth heavy attention modules from redundant layers while repurposing their Multi Layer Perceptrons MLPs into MoE experts This structural innovation preserves the models core capabilities and strictly limits active parameters via selective token routing With a modest continual pre training budget Dense2MoE efficiently converts publicly available dense LLMs into on device ready MoE models Extensive experiments demonstrate that Dense2MoE significantly advances the Pareto frontier for on device inference latency versus model accuracy outperforming dense baselines state of the art compression and standard upcycling methods

中文摘要翻译: 混合专家（MoE）架构在资源受限的端侧部署中极具前景，然而从头训练这些模型的成本高得令人望而却步。当前的方法试图通过将稠密模型升级（upcycling）为MoE来缓解这一问题，但它们往往会引入参数冗余，从而降低推理效率。另一方面，标准的层剪枝方法虽能缓解冗余，但不可避免地会损害模型精度。为解决这一困境，我们提出了Dense2MoE——一种通过层融合升级（Layer Fusion UpCycling, LF-UC）将剪枝与升级统一起来的新颖框架。在硬件Roofline理论的指导下，Dense2MoE通过从冗余层中剪除带宽密集的注意力模块，同时将其多层感知机（MLP）重用作MoE专家，系统性地克服了推理内存墙。这种结构创新保留了模型的核心能力，并通过选择性token路由严格限制激活参数的数量。借助适度的持续预训练预算，Dense2MoE能够高效地将公开可用的稠密大语言模型转换为可直接在端侧部署的MoE模型。大量实验表明，Dense2MoE显著推进了端侧推理延迟与模型精度的帕累托前沿，在性能上优于稠密基线、最先进的压缩方法以及标准的升级方法。

</details>


### [142] [Open-Weight LLM Fine-Tuning Defenses are Susceptible to Simple Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26526)
*Kevin Kuo,Chhavi Yadav,Virginia Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了当前针对开放权重大语言模型的安全防护措施容易受到两种低成本攻击（abliteration和prefilling）的威胁，并提出abliteration-resistant tuning (ART)方法以增强模型鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有安全防护措施假设新的有害行为只能通过微调习得，而忽略了预训练模型本身已编码大量有害知识，可能通过越狱方式直接诱导出有害行为。作者旨在系统评估这种攻击可能性并寻求防御方案。

Method: 1) 评估两种不依赖梯度优化的低成本攻击策略：abliteration和prefilling；2) 提出abliteration-resistant tuning (ART)，将基于abliteration的目标融入训练过程，可叠加到现有防御之上。

Result: 在BeaverTails、HarmBench和AdvBench三个有害性评估基准上，攻击成功率从低于10%提升至16%-96%区间；ART可将攻击成功率降低10%-20%。

Conclusion: 开放权重模型的安全攻击面比此前描述的要更广，安全防护评估应纳入除对抗性微调之外更多样化的攻击策略。

Original Abstract: Recent defenses for safeguarding open-weight large language models (LLMs) are intended to prevent adversarial usage. Underlying these defenses is an assumption that new harmful behavior is learned through fine-tuning rather than elicited by jailbreaking the model. Yet, pretrained LLMs already encode substantial harmful knowledge across many domains, which raises an important question: can an adversary jailbreak safeguarded models, to achieve harmful usage without fine-tuning at all? In this paper, we show that open-weight safeguards are susceptible to simpler strategies that, despite being well known, have not been systematically evaluated against these safeguards. Specifically, we evaluate two low-cost attacks--abliteration and prefilling--that do not rely on gradient-based optimization. Across three harmfulness evaluation benchmarks (BeaverTails, HarmBench, and AdvBench), these attacks increase attack success rates against safeguarded open-weight models from below 10\% to a range of 16%-96%. To mitigate this vulnerability, we introduce abliteration-resistant tuning (ART), which incorporates an abliteration-based objective into training. ART can be layered onto existing defenses and reduces the success rates of abliteration, prefilling, and their combination by 10%-20%. These findings indicate that the attack surface for open-weight models is broader than previously characterized, and that evaluations of safeguarding defenses should incorporate a more diverse set of attack strategies beyond adversarial fine-tuning.

中文摘要翻译: 最近针对开放权重大语言模型的安全防护措施旨在防止对抗性使用。这些防御措施背后有一个假设，即新的有害行为是通过微调习得的，而非通过越狱模型来诱导。然而，预训练的大语言模型已经在许多领域编码了大量有害知识，这引发了一个重要问题：攻击者能否在不进行任何微调的情况下，通过越狱手段突破安全防护模型以实现有害使用？在本文中，我们展示了开放权重安全防护措施容易受到一些简单策略的攻击，这些策略虽广为人知，但尚未针对这些防护进行系统性评估。具体而言，我们评估了两种低成本攻击方法——abliteration和prefilling，它们不依赖于基于梯度的优化。在三个有害性评估基准（BeaverTails、HarmBench和AdvBench）上，这些攻击将针对开放权重安全防护模型的攻击成功率从低于10%提升至16%-96%的范围。为了缓解这一漏洞，我们引入了abliteration-resistant tuning (ART)，该方法将基于abliteration的目标融入训练过程。ART可以叠加到现有防御之上，并将abliteration、prefilling及其组合的攻击成功率降低10%-20%。这些发现表明，开放权重模型的攻击面比此前描述的要更广，对安全防护措施的评估应纳入除对抗性微调之外更多样化的攻击策略。

</details>


### [143] [Recursive Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26535)
*Jiahe Huang,Sihan Xu,Sharvaree Vadgama,Rose Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出递归流匹配（RecFM）框架，通过自一致性约束实现复杂时空动力学的高保真、少步生成，在科学仿真中相比扩散模型提升20倍速度且预测精度更优。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有生成模型在求解物理系统和建模复杂时空动力学时，面临速度与保真度之间的根本性权衡，高物理精度往往伴随高计算成本，亟需一种兼具高效与高精度的生成框架。

Method: 提出递归流匹配（Recursive Flow Matching, RecFM）框架，通过施加自一致性约束，使不同离散化尺度下的轨迹对齐，从而减少离散化误差，支持一步到少步（2-4步）的高保真动态生成。

Result: 在多个科学基准上，RecFM相比领先的扩散仿真器实现高达20倍加速，同时提升预测精度；与普通流匹配相比，均方误差降低超过15%。

Conclusion: RecFM是首个在科学系统中实现与多步求解器相当性能的单步/少步动态生成方法，为实时科学仿真提供了可扩展且高效的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Generative models have emerged as a powerful paradigm for solving physics systems and modeling complex spatiotemporal dynamics. However, achieving high physical accuracy without incurring high computational cost remains a fundamental challenge, as existing approaches face a critical speed-fidelity trade-off. In this work, we introduce Recursive Flow Matching (RecFM), a generative framework for forecasting complex spatiotemporal dynamics. RecFM enforces self-consistency to align trajectories across discretization scales, reducing discretization errors and improving performance across metrics for physics-based tasks. To our knowledge, this is the first method to achieve high-fidelity one- and few-step (2-4 step) dynamic generation for scientific systems with performance comparable to state-of-the-art multi-step solvers. Across challenging scientific benchmarks, RecFM achieves up to a 20$\times$ speedup over leading diffusion-based emulators while improving predictive accuracy. Furthermore, RecFM reduces mean squared error by over 15% compared to vanilla flow matching, offering a scalable and efficient solution for real-time scientific emulation.

中文摘要翻译: 生成模型已成为求解物理系统和建模复杂时空动力学的一种强大范式。然而，在不产生高计算成本的情况下实现高物理精度仍然是一个基本挑战，因为现有方法面临着关键的速度-保真度权衡。在这项工作中，我们提出了递归流匹配（Recursive Flow Matching, RecFM），一种用于预测复杂时空动力学的生成框架。RecFM通过施加自一致性约束，使不同离散化尺度下的轨迹对齐，从而减少离散化误差，并提升基于物理任务各项指标的性能。据我们所知，这是首个在科学系统中实现高保真单步和少步（2-4步）动态生成的方法，其性能可与最先进的多步求解器相媲美。在多个具有挑战性的科学基准上，RecFM相比领先的扩散仿真器实现了高达20倍的加速，同时提高了预测精度。此外，与普通流匹配相比，RecFM将均方误差降低了15%以上，为实时科学仿真提供了一种可扩展且高效的解决方案。

</details>


### [144] [Linear and Neural Dueling Bandits with Delayed Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26554)
*Xiangyi Wang,Pingchen Lu,Jie Mao,Mingze Kong,Zhi Hong,Zhiyong Wang,Zhongxiang Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对上下文对决赌博机在随机延迟反馈场景下的问题，提出线性（LDB-DF）和神经（NDB-DF）两种算法，通过将逆概率加权（IPW）机制直接融入损失函数来消除延迟/缺失反馈带来的偏差，并给出了理论遗憾界和实验验证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准上下文对决赌博机算法假设即时反馈，但实际应用中（如提示词优化）反馈常存在随机延迟，且对决赌博机估计器缺乏闭式解，简单套用标准加权方法会有偏，亟需专门针对延迟反馈的理论与算法。

Method: 提出LDB-DF和NDB-DF两种算法，核心创新是将逆概率加权（IPW）机制直接嵌入损失函数中，实现对延迟或缺失反馈的无偏校正；线性设置下推导O(d√T)遗憾界，神经设置下给出次线性遗憾界。

Result: 线性设置下建立了O(d√T)的遗憾界，神经设置下建立了次线性遗憾保证；在模拟和真实数据集上的大量实验验证了所提算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文首次形式化了带随机延迟反馈的上下文对决赌博机问题，所提出的IPW嵌入损失函数方法能有效消除延迟反馈偏差，理论和实验共同证明了方法的可靠性和实用性。

Original Abstract: Contextual dueling bandits form a cornerstone of preference-based decision-making, with critical applications in
  recommender systems and large language model alignment. However, standard algorithms rely on the idealized assumption
  of immediate feedback, a condition frequently violated in real-world scenarios such as prompt optimization. This
  setting introduces a unique theoretical challenge: unlike linear bandits, dueling bandit estimators lack closed-form
  solutions, rendering naive adaptations of standard weighting techniques biased. To address this, we formalize the
  problem of Contextual Dueling Bandits with Stochastic Delayed Feedback and propose two novel algorithms: Linear
  (LDB-DF) and Neural (NDB-DF) Dueling Bandits with Delayed Feedback. Central to our approach is a novel estimator that
  integrates an Inverse Probability Weighting (IPW) mechanism directly into the loss function, ensuring unbiased
  correction for delayed or missing feedback. We provide comprehensive theoretical analysis, establishing an
  O(d*sqrt(T)) regret bound for the linear setting and sub-linear guarantees for the neural setting. Extensive
  experiments on both simulated and real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our propose.

中文摘要翻译: 上下文对决赌博机构成了基于偏好决策的基石，在推荐系统和大型语言模型对齐中具有关键应用。然而，标准算法依赖于即时反馈的理想化假设，而这一条件在提示词优化等真实场景中经常被违反。该设置带来了独特的理论挑战：与线性赌博机不同，对决赌博机估计器缺乏闭式解，导致对标准加权技术的简单适配会产生偏差。为解决这一问题，我们形式化了带随机延迟反馈的上下文对决赌博机问题，并提出了两种新颖算法：线性延迟反馈对决赌博机（LDB-DF）和神经延迟反馈对决赌博机（NDB-DF）。我们方法的核心是一种新颖的估计器，它将逆概率加权（IPW）机制直接集成到损失函数中，确保了对延迟或缺失反馈的无偏校正。我们提供了全面的理论分析，为线性设置建立了O(d√T)的遗憾界，为神经设置建立了次线性保证。在模拟和真实数据集上的广泛实验证明了我们提出方法的有效性。

</details>


### [145] [Auditing and Fixing Economic Validity in Tabular Foundation Models for Discrete Choice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26559)
*Yingshuo Wang,Xian Sun,Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan,Zexin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段适配器方法，将表格基础模型的预测嵌入到效用最大化框架中，在保持经济一致性的同时提升预测精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有表格基础模型在选择预测任务上准确率高，但常违反经济逻辑（如价格上涨反而需求增加、意愿支付估计为负），需要在不牺牲准确率的前提下保证经济理论的一致性。

Method: 两阶段适配器：第一阶段估计符合经济理论约束的标准选择模型；第二阶段冻结参数，训练一个修正项来融入基础模型的预测信息，从而在保证单调价格-需求关系的同时继承基础模型的精度优势。

Result: 在两个交通数据集上，适配器相较于标准logit模型恢复了高达13个百分点的准确率，同时保持完美经济一致性，原始基础模型和传统蒸馏方法均无法实现。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效融合基础模型的预测能力与经济理论约束，实现精度与可解释性的兼顾，且能提供可解析计算的权衡指标。

Original Abstract: Tabular foundation models achieve strong accuracy on choice prediction tasks, but their predictions often violate the economic logic those tasks require: raising a price sometimes increases predicted demand, and implied willingness-to-pay estimates are frequently negative or implausible. We propose a two-stage adapter that embeds foundation model predictions within a utility-maximization framework. In the first stage, we estimate a standard choice model whose parameters are constrained to obey economic theory. In the second stage, we freeze those parameters and train a correction term that incorporates the foundation model's predictions as additional information. The result is a model that inherits the foundation model's accuracy gains while guaranteeing monotonic price-demand relationships under policy perturbation and producing analytically computable trade-off measures. On two transportation datasets, the adapter recovers up to 13 percentage points of accuracy over a standard logit model while maintaining perfect economic consistency, something neither the raw foundation models nor conventional distillation achieve.

中文摘要翻译: 表格基础模型在选择预测任务上取得了很高的准确率，但其预测常常违背这些任务所要求的经济逻辑：提高价格有时反而会增加预测需求，且隐含的支付意愿估计经常为负或不可信。我们提出了一种两阶段适配器方法，将基础模型的预测嵌入到效用最大化框架中。第一阶段，我们估计一个参数受经济理论约束的标准选择模型。第二阶段，我们冻结这些参数，并训练一个修正项，将基础模型的预测作为附加信息融入。最终得到的模型继承了基础模型的准确率提升，同时保证政策扰动下价格-需求关系的单调性，并能提供可解析计算的权衡指标。在两个交通数据集上，该适配器相较于标准logit模型恢复了高达13个百分点的准确率，同时保持了完美的经济一致性——这是原始基础模型和传统蒸馏方法均无法实现的。

</details>


### [146] [Distribution-Aware Conformal Prediction: A Framework for generating efficient prediction intervals for time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26569)
*Daniel Schweizer,Peter Kuhn,Jayant Sharma,Shivali Dubey,Malte von Ramin,Christoph Brockt-Haßauer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出分布感知共形预测（DCP）框架，集成多种概率预测器与共形校准，生成有效且高效的预测区间，在合成和真实时序数据上验证了自适应校准能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法（如CQR、Conformalized MC）在预测器与评分函数的组合上受限于特定配对应用，缺乏统一、灵活的框架来适应不同不确定性场景和动态环境。

Method: 采用数值反演方法构造区间边界，支持任意分布生成预测器（Monte Carlo dropout、深度集成、分位回归）与非一致性评分的组合，并结合修正的Winkler评分显式惩罚欠覆盖。

Result: 在合成和实际时间序列数据上的基准分析表明，DCP能够在不同不确定性模式下自适应校准预测区间，其模块化设计支持灵活的即插即用实验。

Conclusion: DCP推广并扩展了现有方法，模块化设计为进一步拓展奠定了基础，推动了动态环境和高风险应用中的不确定性量化研究。

Original Abstract: We present Distribution-aware Conformal Prediction (DCP), a unified framework integrating probabilistic predictors like Monte Carlo dropout, deep ensembles, and quantile regression with score-agnostic conformal calibration to produce valid and efficient prediction intervals. Leveraging a numerical inversion approach to construct interval bounds, DCP accommodates arbitrary combinations of distribution generating predictors and nonconformity scores. Benchmark analysis on synthetic and real-world time series data demonstrate DCP's ability to adaptively calibrate prediction intervals under varying uncertainty regimes. Crucially, DCP's modular design facilitates plug-and-play experimentation with different predictor-score pairings, quantitatively supported by a newly introduced modified Winkler score that balances validity and efficiency by explicitly penalizing undercoverage. While DCP generalizes and extends existing approaches like Conformalized Quantile Regression and Conformalized Monte Carlo, its modular design allows further extensions, setting a foundation for advancing uncertainty quantification in dynamic environments and high-risk applications.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了分布感知共形预测（DCP），这是一个统一框架，将概率预测器（如Monte Carlo dropout、深度集成和分位回归）与评分无关的共形校准相结合，以生成有效且高效的预测区间。DCP利用数值反演方法构造区间边界，支持任意分布生成预测器与非一致性评分函数的组合。在合成和真实世界时间序列数据上的基准分析表明，DCP能够在不同不确定性模式下自适应地校准预测区间。关键在于，DCP的模块化设计便于不同预测器-评分配对的即插即用实验，这一能力通过新引入的修正Winkler评分得到定量支持，该评分通过显式惩罚欠覆盖来平衡有效性与高效性。虽然DCP推广并扩展了现有方法（如共形化分位回归和共形化Monte Carlo），但其模块化设计允许进一步拓展，为推进动态环境和高风险应用中的不确定性量化奠定了基础。

</details>


### [147] [Separate Aggregation of Split Network for Personalized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26571)
*Yunseok Kang,Jaeyoung Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PGFedSplit，一种针对严重客户端异质性场景的个性化联邦学习框架。它采用拆分架构和自适应聚合调度，结合本地特征与服务器端高斯统计生成的合成特征，在提升个性化性能的同时保持全局泛化能力。在多个图像数据集上，该方法在高度异质环境下表现优于现有SOTA方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有PFL方法在全局共享与局部自适应之间存在根本性权衡：更强的全局共享会削弱局部特化，而更强的局部自适应在有限数据、标签不平衡和缺失类别场景下容易导致过拟合。现有方法难以同时兼顾个性化性能与全局泛化能力。

Method: PGFedSplit采用拆分架构，根据模型不同组件的角色设计自适应聚合调度策略，实现稳定的知识共享与客户端特定适应性。每个客户端进一步融合本地提取的特征表示与从服务器端高斯统计生成的合成特征表示，以提升在标签不平衡和缺失类别条件下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在Fashion MNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和Tiny ImageNet数据集上的大量实验表明，PGFedSplit在高度异质设置下相较现有最优PFL方法取得了一致的性能提升，具有稳定的收敛性和优越的个性化表现。

Conclusion: PGFedSplit通过拆分架构和自适应聚合调度，以及本地-合成特征混合机制，有效解决了PFL中全局共享与局部自适应的权衡问题，在严重客户端异质性下同时提升了个性化和全局泛化性能。

Original Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative model training without sharing raw data, but its performance can degrade substantially under heterogeneous client data distributions. A single global model often cannot satisfy diverse client requirements, so personalized federated learning has therefore been explored to improve client specific performance while preserving global generalization. Existing PFL methods often face a fundamental tradeoff in which stronger global sharing can undermine local specialization, whereas stronger local adaptation can lead to overfitting under limited data, label imbalance, and missing class scenarios. In this work, we propose PGFedSplit, a personalized federated learning framework that improves both personalization and global generalization under severe client heterogeneity. PGFedSplit adopts a split architecture and performs adaptive aggregation scheduling tailored to the roles of different model components, enabling stable knowledge sharing while maintaining client specific adaptation. Each client further leverages a mixture of locally extracted representations and synthetic representations generated from server side Gaussian statistics, improving robustness under label imbalance and missing class conditions. Extensive experiments on Fashion MNIST, CIFAR 10, CIFAR 100, and Tiny ImageNet demonstrate consistent improvements over state of the art PFL methods, with stable convergence and superior personalization in highly heterogeneous settings.

中文摘要翻译: 联邦学习能够在无需共享原始数据的情况下实现协作式模型训练，但在客户端数据分布异构时其性能会显著下降。单一的全局模型通常无法满足不同客户端的多样化需求，因此个性化联邦学习被提出，以在保持全局泛化能力的同时提升客户端特定性能。现有的PFL方法往往面临一个根本性权衡：增强全局共享会削弱局部特化，而增强局部自适应则会在数据有限、标签不平衡和缺失类别场景下导致过拟合。在本文中，我们提出PGFedSplit，一种在严重客户端异质性下同时提升个性化和全局泛化性能的个性化联邦学习框架。PGFedSplit采用拆分架构，并根据不同模型组件的作用设计自适应聚合调度策略，在保持客户端特定自适应的同时实现稳定的知识共享。每个客户端进一步融合本地提取的特征表示与从服务器端高斯统计生成的合成特征表示，从而提升在标签不平衡和缺失类别条件下的鲁棒性。在Fashion MNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和Tiny ImageNet上的大量实验表明，该方法在高度异质设置下相较于现有最优PFL方法取得了一致的性能提升，具有稳定的收敛性和优越的个性化表现。

</details>


### [148] [Focal Reward: Balanced Reinforcement Learning under Rubric-Based Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26579)
*Yu Huang,Zihua Zhao,Zhaoxin Huan,Wanli Gu,Feng Hong,Xinmu Ge,Lin Yuan,Weichang Wu,Qiang Hu,Xiaolu Zhang,Jun Zhou,Jiangchao Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Focal Reward 方法，通过反向量化投影机制估计每条评价标准的饱和程度，并自动重加权各标准在强化学习训练中的奖励信号，以解决多维度评价下奖励极化导致的部分维度训练不足问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有LLM基于多维度评价体系的强化学习训练存在严重的奖励极化问题，即模型在某些维度获得高奖励后，其他维度仍存在严重缺陷，导致用户体验下降。

Method: 首先通过反向量化投影机制估计每条评价标准的饱和程度，据此校准奖励方向；然后设计自动重加权系数对各标准进行细粒度平衡。

Result: 在3种模型规模和6个基准测试上，Focal Reward 在所有18个模型-基准对比中均优于最强的静态聚合基线方法。进一步分析表明，性能提升源于对仍有改进空间的评价标准进行在线、饱和感知的奖励重分配。

Conclusion: Focal Reward 通过在线、饱和感知的奖励重分配，有效解决了多维度评价下的奖励极化问题，显著提升了LLM强化学习训练的均衡性和最终性能。

Original Abstract: The open-ended generation in LLMs usually requires multi-dimensional rubrics to adequately assess quality and guide the improvement of reinforcement learning. However, a critical dilemma inherent in this training paradigm is the imbalanced reward polarization along different rubric dimensions. Under this bottleneck, even if LLMs achieve relatively high rewards after training, they may still exhibit severe deficiencies in certain dimensions, leading to a direct deterioration in user experience. To address this problem, we propose Focal Reward, a novel objective to automatically balance the training of reinforcement learning under rubric-based rewards. Specifically, we first leverage an inverse reward projection mechanism to estimate the saturation degree of each criterion in the rubric, which forms the basis to calibrate the reward direction. Then, the final objective is designed with an automatically reweighting coefficient for each criterion to achieve the fine-grained balancing. Extensive experiments across three model scales and six benchmarks demonstrate that our Focal Reward method outperforms the strongest static aggregation baseline in all 18 model-benchmark comparisons. Rollout, mechanism, and ablation analyses further show that these gains arise from online, saturation-aware reallocation toward rubrics that still have room for improvement.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型中的开放式生成通常需要多维度评价体系来充分评估质量并指导强化学习的改进。然而，这种训练范式固有的一大关键困境是沿不同评价维度存在的奖励极化不平衡问题。在此瓶颈下，即使大语言模型在训练后获得了较高的整体奖励，它们仍可能在某些维度上存在严重缺陷，直接导致用户体验下降。为解决这一问题，我们提出 Focal Reward——一种新的目标函数，用于在基于评价体系的奖励下自动平衡强化学习的训练过程。具体而言，我们首先利用反向量化投影机制来估计评价体系中每条标准的饱和程度，以此为基础校准奖励方向。随后，最终目标函数针对每条标准设计了自动重加权系数，以实现细粒度的平衡。在三种模型规模和六个基准测试上进行的大量实验表明，我们的 Focal Reward 方法在所有18个模型-基准对比中均优于最强的静态聚合基线方法。进一步的分析表明，这些性能提升源于对仍有改进空间的评价标准进行在线、饱和感知的奖励重分配。

</details>


### [149] [On the Error-Correcting Effects of Stochasticity in Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26582)
*William Yuan,Sungwon Jeong,Amirali Aghazadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了离散扩散模型中马尔可夫转移的随机性程度对采样效率与质量权衡的影响，发现高度确定性转移收敛快但误差累积严重，而适度随机性转移虽收敛慢但能通过冗余转移的纠错效应提升最终样本质量。基于此提出DCRS算法，通过交替前向和反向扩散过程注入受控随机性，在图像生成中实现高达10倍采样步数缩减，在语言生成中效果取决于具体流程。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 离散扩散模型在文本和图像生成中表现出色，但推理速度慢，且采样效率与样本质量之间存在固有权衡。现有方法未能系统理解这一权衡的底层机制，因此亟需深入分析随机性程度如何影响采样过程，并据此设计更优的采样算法。

Method: 1) 信息论分析：从理论上揭示冗余转移（对称交换状态间的概率质量）具有纠错效应，能收缩采样误差；2) 提出DCRS（离散搅动与重启采样）算法：通过交替执行前向扩散和反向扩散过程，在采样过程中注入受控随机性，以平衡收敛速度与样本质量。

Result: 在合成数据集和大规模基准测试上，DCRS在低函数评估次数场景下改善了速度-质量权衡。图像数据集上，相比标准采样器，DCRS在保持相当样本质量的同时将采样步数减少高达10倍。语言基准上，效果更依赖于具体的损坏过程和采样流程，表现出更复杂的规律。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型的采样存在确定性-随机性的本质权衡：高度确定性导致快速收敛但误差累积，适度随机性通过冗余转移的纠错机制实现更高质量但收敛更慢。DCRS通过受控随机性注入有效改善了这一权衡，尤其在图像生成任务中优势显著。

Original Abstract: Discrete diffusion models achieve strong performance in text and image generation, but their inference remains slow and must inherently balance sampling efficiency and sample quality. In this work, we present a systematic study of how the \emph{degree of stochasticity} in Markov transitions governs the sampling tradeoff. We show that highly deterministic transitions converge rapidly but suffer from error accumulation, while more stochastic transitions converge more slowly yet can achieve higher final sample quality. Using an information-theoretic analysis, we identify the underlying mechanism as an error-correcting effect induced by \emph{redundant transitions} that symmetrically exchange mass between states, and show that these transitions can provably contract sampling errors. Motivated by this analysis, we propose \emph{Discrete Churn and Restart Sampling} (DCRS), a novel inference algorithm that injects controlled stochasticity by alternating between forward and reverse diffusion processes. Experiments on synthetic datasets and large-scale benchmarks show that DCRS improves the speed-quality tradeoff in the low number of function evaluations regime. On image datasets, DCRS achieves up to a $10\times$ reduction in sampling steps compared to standard samplers while maintaining competitive sample quality, whereas on language benchmarks, we observe more nuanced behavior depending on the corruption process and sampling procedure.

中文摘要翻译: 离散扩散模型在文本和图像生成中取得了强劲的性能，但其推理过程仍然缓慢，且必须在采样效率与样本质量之间进行固有的权衡。在本工作中，我们对马尔可夫转移中"随机性程度"如何支配采样权衡进行了系统性研究。我们发现，高度确定性的转移收敛迅速但容易产生误差累积，而更具随机性的转移收敛较慢，却能达到更高的最终样本质量。通过信息论分析，我们识别出其底层机制是一种由"冗余转移"（即在状态之间对称交换概率质量）引发的纠错效应，并证明这些转移能够可证明地收缩采样误差。受此分析启发，我们提出了"离散搅动与重启采样"（DCRS），一种新型推理算法，通过交替进行正向和反向扩散过程来注入受控随机性。在合成数据集和大规模基准上的实验表明，DCRS在低函数评估次数区间改善了速度-质量权衡。在图像数据集上，与标准采样器相比，DCRS在保持竞争性样本质量的同时实现了高达10倍的采样步数缩减；而在语言基准上，我们观察到效果更依赖于具体的损坏过程和采样流程，呈现出更细微的行为差异。

</details>


### [150] [Near-Optimal Regret in Adversarial Kernel Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26585)
*Yu-Jie Zhang,Hao Qiu,Jonathan Scarlett,Kevin Jamieson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究对抗性核赌博机问题，提出了一种基于正则化重要性加权损失估计器的指数权重算法，并引入显式校正项消除正则化偏差，实现了与随机核赌博机问题已知最优率相匹配的遗憾界。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有对抗性核赌博机问题的遗憾界不够理想，且Chatterji等人[2019]的分析依赖于秩为一的对手假设。本文旨在移除该限制，同时改进遗憾界，使其匹配随机核赌博机问题的已知最优率。

Method: 提出一种指数权重算法，构建正则化重要性加权损失估计器，并添加一个显式校正项来抵消正则化带来的偏差。通过有效维度d_*(λ)刻画核的复杂度，推导出遗憾上界。

Result: 遗憾界为Õ(√(T·d_*(λ)·log|X|))；对于Matérn(ν,d)核，遗憾界为Õ(T^{(ν+d)/(2ν+d)})，改进了Chatterji等人[2019]的结果，且与随机核赌博机的最优率一致，同时匹配并发工作的下界（最多相差log T因子）。

Conclusion: 本文提出的算法在对抗性核赌博机问题中达到了与随机情形相同的最优遗憾率，且不再需要秩为一对手假设，显著推广了现有结果。

Original Abstract: We study the adversarial kernel bandit problem, in which the loss at each round is induced by an arbitrary bounded element of a reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS). We propose an exponential-weights algorithm built on a regularized importance-weighted loss estimator, together with an explicit correction term that cancels the bias introduced by the regularization. Our main result bounds the regret by $\widetilde{O}\big(\sqrt{T\, d_*(λ)\,\log|{X}|}\big)$, where $d_*(λ)$ is a widely-adopted notion of effective dimension that captures the complexity of the kernel. Up to logarithmic factors, this matches the known rate achieved in the related stochastic kernel bandit problem. A notable application is the Matérn$(ν,d)$ kernel with smoothness parameter $ν$ on $\mathbb{R}^d$, for which our bound specializes to $\widetilde{O}\big(T^{(ν+d)/(2ν+d)}\big)$, improving over the best-known prior rate of Chatterji et al. [2019] while simultaneously removing the rank-one adversary assumption required by their analysis. Moreover, this rate is the same as the known optimal rate for stochastic kernel bandits, and also matches a lower bound from concurrent work up to a $\log T$ factor.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了对抗性核赌博机问题，其中每一轮的损失由再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）中任意有界元素诱导产生。我们提出了一种基于正则化重要性加权损失估计器的指数权重算法，并加入了一个显式校正项来抵消正则化引入的偏差。我们的主要结果将遗憾界限制为Õ(√(T·d_*(λ)·log|X|))，其中d_*(λ)是广泛采用的有效维度概念，用于刻画核的复杂度。在忽略对数因子的情况下，该结果与相关随机核赌博机问题中已知的速率相匹配。一个显著的应用是ℝ^d上光滑参数为ν的Matérn(ν,d)核，此时我们的界特化为Õ(T^{(ν+d)/(2ν+d)})，在改进Chatterji等人[2019]先前最优结果的同时，还移除了其分析所需的秩为一对手假设。此外，该速率与随机核赌博机已知的最优速率相同，并且与并发工作的下界相差最多一个log T因子。

</details>


### [151] [Few-shot Cross-country Generalization of Tabular Machine Learning and Foundation Models for Childhood Anemia Prediction under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26589)
*Yusuf Brima,Marcellin Atemkeng,Lansana Hassim Kallon,David Niyukuri,Antoine Vacavant,Samuel Saidu,Ding-Geng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了基于Transformer的表格基础模型TabPFN在跨国家、数据稀缺场景下预测儿童贫血的性能，发现其在小样本（<200）条件下优于传统模型，但整体预测性能更多受人群变异驱动而非模型选择。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 儿童贫血影响因素复杂多样，导致模型泛化能力受限。现有研究缺乏对跨国家、数据稀缺场景下不同模型（特别是新兴的基础模型）的系统比较评估。

Method: 使用来自16个国家的DHS数据（n=68,856），比较逻辑回归、XGBoost、LightGBM和TabPFN v2.6五种模型；采用留一国家法、反向留一国家法和少样本设置评估泛化能力；使用AUC-ROC、Brier分数和ECE评估性能；通过SHAP分析特征重要性。

Result: TabPFN在低数据场景（<200样本）下表现出更高的判别能力和更好的校准性能；跨国家时Brier分数最低（0.042），ECE最低（0.203）；全数据下AUC-ROC为0.59-0.76，模型间差异小（≤0.05）；儿童年龄、海拔和身高别年龄Z评分是主要预测因子。

Conclusion: 儿童贫血预测性能更多由人群变异驱动而非模型选择；TabPFN在低资源场景下通过改进判别和校准性能展现出优势，突显了基础模型作为数据稀缺全球健康预测工具的前景。

Original Abstract: Childhood anemia affects around 40% of children aged 6-59 months globally and arises from heterogeneous factors, limiting model generalizability. We evaluate a transformer-based tabular foundation model against classical supervised methods under cross-country and data-scarce settings.
  We used DHS data from 16 countries across Africa, Asia, Latin America, the Caucasus, and the Middle East (n=68,856). We compared Logistic Regression, XGBoost, LightGBM, and TabPFN v2.6. Performance was assessed using AUC-ROC, Brier score, and ECE. Generalization was evaluated using leave-one-country-out (LOCO), reverse-LOCO, and few-shot settings. Subgroup analyses included sex, age, residence, maternal education, and wealth. Feature importance was estimated using SHAP.
  TabPFN outperformed classical models in low-data regimes (<200 samples), showing higher discrimination and better calibration. Across countries, it achieved the lowest Brier score (0.042) and ECE (0.203). Under full-data settings, AUC-ROC ranged from 0.59-0.76 with small between-model differences ($\leq 0.05$). LOCO performance was stable (0.58-0.69), driven by country context. Reverse-LOCO showed asymmetric transferability. Subgroup performance was consistent with no systematic demographic bias. SHAP identified child age, altitude, and height-for-age z-score as dominant predictors, followed by wealth and maternal education.
  Performance in childhood anemia prediction is driven more by population variation than model choice. TabPFN provides advantages in low-resource settings through improved discrimination and calibration, highlighting foundation models as promising tools for data-scarce global health prediction.

中文摘要翻译: 全球约40%的6-59个月儿童患有贫血，其病因具有异质性，限制了模型的泛化能力。本研究评估了一种基于Transformer的表格基础模型与经典监督方法在跨国家和数据稀缺场景下的表现。我们使用了来自非洲、亚洲、拉丁美洲、高加索和中东地区16个国家的DHS数据（n=68,856）。比较了逻辑回归、XGBoost、LightGBM和TabPFN v2.6。通过AUC-ROC、Brier分数和ECE评估性能。采用留一国家法、反向留一国家法和少样本设置评估泛化能力。亚组分析包括性别、年龄、居住地、母亲教育程度和财富水平。使用SHAP评估特征重要性。TabPFN在低数据场景（<200个样本）下优于经典模型，表现出更高的判别能力和更好的校准性能。跨国家分析中，其Brier分数（0.042）和ECE（0.203）均为最低。全数据设置下，AUC-ROC范围为0.59-0.76，模型间差异较小（≤0.05）。留一国家法性能稳定（0.58-0.69），受国家背景驱动。反向留一国家法显示出非对称的可迁移性。亚组性能一致，未发现系统性人口统计学偏差。SHAP识别出儿童年龄、海拔和身高别年龄Z评分为主导预测因子，其次是财富水平和母亲教育程度。儿童贫血预测性能更多由人群变异驱动而非模型选择。TabPFN通过改进判别和校准性能在低资源场景下提供优势，突显了基础模型作为数据稀缺全球健康预测工具的前景。

</details>


### [152] [Spend Your Rollouts Where It Counts: Rollout Allocation for Group-Based RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26606)
*Woojeong Kim,Ziyi Yang,Jing Nathan Yan,Jialu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Pilot-Commit框架，通过在分组策略优化中引入预算感知的试拍-提交机制，动态评估提示信息量并差异化分配rollout预算，在不牺牲准确率的前提下显著降低在线强化学习的采样成本。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在线on-policy强化学习中，rollout生成占训练计算成本主导地位。现有的分组策略优化方法为每个提示分配多个rollout，但机械地为所有提示分配相同预算，在奖励分布已坍塌的低信息量提示上浪费了大量昂贵的rollout资源。

Method: 提出Pilot-Commit框架，将rollout分配解耦为两个阶段：(1) Pilot阶段，使用少量预算评估每个提示的信息量（即奖励方差）；(2) Commit阶段，将剩余rollout预算集中分配给高信息量提示，而低信号提示则被跳过。

Result: 在多个数学推理基准和1.5B到14B参数规模模型上，Pilot-Commit在达到目标准确率时，累积rollout量比GRPO快1.9倍，比DAPO快4.0倍，且不牺牲基线准确率。

Conclusion: 分组策略优化的有效性取决于奖励方差的高低，提示信息量必须在线估计而非预计算。Pilot-Commit通过预算感知的差异化分配，在不损失性能的前提下大幅提升采样效率。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is the dominant paradigm for post-training large language models. However, in the online, on-policy setting, rollout generation dominates the computational cost of training. Group-based policy optimization methods compute advantages from multiple rollouts per prompt, yet they indiscriminately allocate budget to prompts with collapsed reward distributions, wasting expensive rollouts on negligible learning signals. We demonstrate that group-based updates are most effective in regimes of high reward variance. Since the policy evolves throughout training, prompt informativeness must be estimated online rather than precomputed, but exhaustively evaluating every prompt is computationally prohibitive. We introduce Pilot-Commit, a budget-aware rollout allocation framework for group-based RL post-training. Pilot-Commit decouples prompt evaluation from exploitation: a pilot stage estimates per-prompt informativeness using a fraction of the budget, and the remaining rollouts are allocated to high-leverage prompts while low-signal prompts are skipped. Across multiple math reasoning benchmarks and model scales from 1.5B to 14B parameters, Pilot-Commit matches baseline accuracy with significantly lower sampling costs, reaching target accuracy up to $1.9\times$ faster than GRPO and $4.0\times$ faster than DAPO in cumulative rollouts.

中文摘要翻译: 强化学习（RL）是大语言模型后训练的主流范式。然而，在在线、on-policy设定下，rollout生成主导了训练的计算成本。基于分组的策略优化方法为每个提示计算多个rollout的优势，但它们不加区分地将预算分配给奖励分布已坍塌的提示，将昂贵的rollout浪费在可忽略的学习信号上。我们证明，分组式更新在高奖励方差的区间中最有效。由于策略在整个训练过程中不断演化，提示的信息量必须在线估计而非预计算，但穷举评估每个提示在计算上不可行。我们引入Pilot-Commit，一种面向分组RL后训练的预算感知型rollout分配框架。Pilot-Commit将提示评估与利用解耦：Pilot阶段使用部分预算估计每个提示的信息量，然后将剩余rollout分配给高杠杆提示，同时跳过低信号提示。在多个数学推理基准和1.5B至14B参数规模的模型上，Pilot-Commit以显著更低的采样成本匹配基线准确率，在累积rollout量上到达目标准确率的速度比GRPO快1.9倍，比DAPO快4.0倍。

</details>


### [153] [PIDM-DP: Physics-Informed Diffusion with Dormand-Prince Integration for Chaotic System Identification and State Reconstruction across Multiple Dynamical Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26619)
*Shailendra Dabral*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PIDM-DP，将5阶Dormand-Prince ODE求解器嵌入扩散模型反向采样过程，通过物理残差反向传播约束混沌系统轨迹重建，在稀疏噪声观测下实现高精度状态估计，显著优于未约束扩散模型和集合卡尔曼滤波。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 从稀疏含噪观测中重建混沌动力系统的连续状态轨迹是非线性科学中的基本开放问题，现有方法（如未约束扩散模型和集合卡尔曼滤波）在刚度系统上因梯度爆炸或协方差坍塌而表现不佳。

Method: 将完全可微的5阶Dormand-Prince (DP-RK45) ODE积分器嵌入去噪扩散概率模型(DDPM)的反向采样循环；每个去噪步通过自动微分反向传播物理残差，强制生成轨迹满足5阶精度的系统控制方程；采用线性调度引导机制，在高噪声阶段将物理权重从零逐渐增至干净数据附近的满值，防止刚度系统梯度爆炸。
