Method: FA-OPD包含两阶段：首先从演示数据中学习一个流匹配（FM）教师模型，然后将其与轻量级MLP学生模型进行对抗性协同训练。教师模型在学生轨迹上提供两种互补信号：奖励通道通过状态-动作对的专家似然目标驱动在线探索和长程策略优化；动作通道在学生访问的状态上提供密集的局部目标，稳定利用过程。两者耦合使奖励蒸馏能实现超越点状演示的泛化，动作蒸馏将探索锚定在接近专家行为的区域。

Result: 在机器人导航、操作和运动控制六大基准任务上，FA-OPD超越了强基线方法，并在噪声或有限演示条件下展现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FA-OPD通过对抗性双在线策略蒸馏有效解决了演示学习中的分布偏移问题，结合奖励蒸馏的泛化能力和动作蒸馏的稳定探索，在多种具身控制任务上取得了显著性能提升。

Original Abstract: Learning from demonstrations in embodied control is often cast as behavioral cloning, and recent diffusion or flow-matching policies improve this paradigm by modeling multi-modal expert actions. Yet these methods remain offline supervised learners: the policy is trained only on expert states and receives no corrective signal on the states it actually visits. On-policy distillation (OPD) offers a natural remedy, but standard OPD assumes a strong fixed teacher, which is unavailable in demonstration-only control. We propose \textbf{FA-OPD}, an \emph{adversarial dual on-policy distillation} method in which a Flow Matching (FM) teacher is learned from demonstrations and co-trained with a lightweight MLP student. The teacher provides two complementary signals on student rollouts. The reward channel learns an expert-likeness objective over state-action pairs and drives online exploration through long-horizon policy optimization. The action channel supplies dense local targets at student-visited states, stabilizing exploitation. FA-OPD couples them so that reward distillation enables generalization beyond point-wise demonstrations, while action distillation keeps exploration anchored near expert-like behavior. Across six robot navigation, manipulation, and locomotion benchmarks, FA-OPD beats strong baselines and shows much stronger robustness under noisy or limited demonstrations.

中文摘要翻译: 在具身控制中，从演示中学习通常被形式化为行为克隆，而最近的扩散或流匹配策略通过建模多模态专家行为改进了这一范式。然而，这些方法仍然是离线监督学习：策略仅根据专家状态进行训练，无法获得其实际访问状态上的纠正信号。在线策略蒸馏（OPD）提供了一种自然的解决方案，但标准OPD假设存在强大的固定教师，这在仅依赖演示的控制中不可用。我们提出\textbf{FA-OPD}，一种\textit{对抗性双在线策略蒸馏}方法，其中从演示中学习一个流匹配（FM）教师，并与轻量级MLP学生协同训练。教师在学生轨迹上提供两种互补信号。奖励通道学习状态-动作对上的专家似然目标，并通过长程策略优化驱动在线探索。动作通道在学生访问的状态上提供密集的局部目标，稳定利用过程。FA-OPD将两者耦合，使得奖励蒸馏能够实现超越点状演示的泛化，而动作蒸馏则将探索锚定在接近专家行为的区域。在六个机器人导航、操作和运动控制基准任务上，FA-OPD超越了强基线方法，并在噪声或有限演示条件下展现出更强的鲁棒性。

</details>


### [184] [DEI: Diversity in Evolutionary Inference for Quality-Diversity Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27130)
*John Donaghy,Shikhar Rastogi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DEI（多样进化推理）框架，一种分布式质量-多样性（QD）搜索方法，通过将异构大语言模型作为变异算子，在非阻塞集体通信的节点间协作。实验表明，模型多样性（而非单纯并行化）是分布式LLM-QD搜索性能提升的关键驱动力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有同构并行搜索复制单一模型的归纳偏置，限制了行为新颖性；而不同LLM拥有各异的创作先验，如何有效利用这种模型多样性来提升QD搜索的鲁棒性和覆盖度是核心动机。

Method: 基于Digital Red Queen框架扩展，DEI将异构LLM分配为对等节点上的变异算子，节点间通过非阻塞集体操作通信；每轮结束后节点共享局部最优解以播种下一轮种群，从而产生跨模型的对抗压力，超越单模型自对弈的鲁棒性。

Result: 在Core War领域（Redcode程序对战）上，四节点异构集成（GPT-5.4-mini, Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-5.2, Claude Haiku 4.5）相比单节点基线，合并存档QD分数提升124%（45.90 vs 20.46），覆盖率提升28%（80.6% vs 63.0%）；且在QD分数、覆盖率和保留解泛化性上均优于同预算的同构集成。

Conclusion: 首次提供实证证据表明，模型多样性而非单纯并行化是分布式LLM-QD搜索性能提升的关键驱动因素。

Original Abstract: We present DEI: Diversity in Evolutionary Inference, a distributed Quality-Diversity (QD) search framework that assigns heterogeneous large language models (LLMs) as mutation operators across peer nodes communicating with non-blocking collective operations. Unlike homogeneous parallel search, which replicates a single model's inductive biases across all workers, DEI treats each LLM's distinct creative prior as a complementary source of behavioral novelty. Extending the Digital Red Queen framework with DEI, nodes share local optimal solutions at the end of each round to seed the next round's population. This creates cross-model adversarial pressure that drives robustness beyond intra-model self-play. Evaluated on the Core War domain, a competitive programming benchmark in which Redcode warrior programs battle inside a simulated machine, a four-node heterogeneous ensemble (GPT-5.4-mini, Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-5.2, and Claude Haiku 4.5) achieves 124 percent higher merged-archive QD-Score (45.90 vs. 20.46) and 28 percent higher coverage (80.6 percent vs. 63.0 percent of cells) than a single-node baseline at equal total LLM-call budget. The heterogeneous ensemble also outperforms an equally-budgeted homogeneous ensemble on QD-Score, coverage, and held-out solution generality across all four model families. These results provide the first empirical evidence that model diversity, not merely parallelism, is the key driver of gain in distributed LLM-based QD search.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了DEI（多样进化推理）：一种分布式质量-多样性（QD）搜索框架，该框架将异构大语言模型（LLM）分配为对等节点上的变异算子，节点间通过非阻塞集体操作进行通信。与复制单一模型归纳偏置至所有工作节点的同构并行搜索不同，DEI将每个LLM独特的创作先验视为行为新颖性的互补来源。通过DEI扩展Digital Red Queen框架，节点在每轮结束时共享局部最优解以播种下一轮种群。这产生了跨模型的对抗压力，推动了超越单模型自对弈的鲁棒性。在Core War领域（一种竞争性编程基准，其中Redcode勇士程序在模拟机器内对战）上进行评估，四节点异构集成（GPT-5.4-mini、Claude Sonnet 4.6、GPT-5.2和Claude Haiku 4.5）在相等的总LLM调用预算下，相比单节点基线，合并存档的QD分数提升了124%（45.90对比20.46），覆盖率提升了28%（80.6%对比63.0%）。该异构集成在QD分数、覆盖率以及所有四个模型系列的保留解泛化性上也优于同等预算的同构集成。这些结果首次提供了实证证据，表明模型多样性（而非单纯并行化）是分布式基于LLM的QD搜索中性能增益的关键驱动因素。

</details>


### [185] [The Role of Causal Features in Strategic Classification for Robustness and Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27163)
*Antonio Gois,Sophia Gunluk,Nir Rosenfeld,Nidhi Hegde,Simon Lacoste-Julien,Dhanya Sridhar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究因果模型在战略分类中的应用，建立了因果性与战略分类之间的新关联：因果分类在大规模用户适应后达到最优错误率；当假设不成立时，非最优性可分解为分布外偏置项和未使用全部特征的项；使用因果特征可对齐机构与用户的长期激励。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 战略分类中用户为提升分类效用会主动调整自身特征，导致分布偏移。传统方法难以应对这种由用户适应引起的分布变化，因此作者转向因果模型，希望借助其分布外风险的理论优势，建立因果性与战略分类之间的理论联系。

Method: 理论上，作者在噪声有界条件下证明因果分类在大规模适应后达到最优错误率；然后分解分布外交叉熵风险为偏置项和特征缺失项；最后分析因果特征对长期激励对齐的作用。实验上，使用合成数据验证理论预测。

Result: 1) 噪声有界时，因果分类在足够大的用户适应后达到最优分类错误率；2) 当假设不成立时，最优分类器的分布外交叉熵风险可分解为分布外偏置项与未使用全部观测特征带来的误差项；3) 使用因果特征有助于对齐机构与用户的长期激励，缓解此前研究中指出的社会成本问题；4) 合成数据实验验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 因果模型能够有效应对战略分类中的分布偏移问题，因果分类在大规模适应后具有最优性，且因果特征的使用有助于对齐机构与用户的长期激励，降低了社会成本。

Original Abstract: In strategic classification, an institution (e.g., a bank) anticipates adaptation from users who change their features to increase utility in a classification task (e.g., loan repayment). Since a key challenge is the distribution shift induced by users, we turn to causal models, which have been shown to bound the worst-case out-of-distribution (OOD) risk, and establish several new results that link causality and strategic classification. First, we show that causal classification leads to optimal classification error after any sufficiently large adaptation, when the noise is bounded in a certain way. Second, when these assumptions do not hold, we show OOD cross-entropy risk of optimal classifiers decomposes into an OOD bias term and a term arising from not using all observable features, allowing us to understand when causal classifiers have an advantage. Finally, we show that the use of causal features can allow alignment of long-term incentives between institutions and users, contrasting with previous work that highlights social costs of such approaches. We validate our theory empirically on synthetic data, finding that our results predict behavior in practice.

中文摘要翻译: 在战略分类问题中，机构（如银行）需要预判用户为在分类任务（如贷款偿还预测）中提高自身效用而改变特征的行为。由于用户适应行为带来的分布偏移是核心挑战，我们转向已被证明能约束最坏情况分布外风险的因果模型，并建立了连接因果性与战略分类的多项新结果。首先，我们证明在噪声以特定方式有界时，因果分类在用户进行足够大规模适应后能达到最优分类错误率。其次，当这些假设不成立时，我们证明最优分类器的分布外交叉熵风险可分解为分布外偏置项与未使用全部观测特征带来的误差项，这有助于理解因果分类器何时具有优势。最后，我们证明使用因果特征能够对齐机构与用户之间的长期激励，这与先前工作中强调此类方法会带来社会成本的结论形成对比。我们在合成数据上进行了理论验证，发现实验结果与理论预测一致。

</details>


### [186] [Nonlinear Data Integration via Kernel Methods for Data Collaboration Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27219)
*Yamato Suetake,Yuta Kawakami,Shunnosuke Ikeda,Yuichi Takano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出非线性核集成方法（NKI），用于隐私保护的数据协作分析，通过核技巧解决非线性降维后中间表示的对齐问题，并引入图正则化和中心化约束以引入几何与目标变量信息，实验表明NKI在图像分类任务上优于现有线性集成方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数据协作分析方法多采用线性变换进行数据混淆与集成，但线性变换存在重建风险；而非线性降维虽能降低该风险，但传统线性集成方法无法准确对齐非线性变换产生的中间表示；此外，现有集成方法主要最小化各方差异，未显式融合对下游分析有用的几何信息或目标变量信息。

Method: 首先将线性核集成（LKI）形式化为线性集成方法，然后通过核化得到非线性核集成（NKI）。NKI通过核岭回归和特征值问题求得全局最优解；同时引入图正则化和中心化约束，使目标表示能捕获几何和目标变量信息。

Result: 在图像分类任务上，NKI在非线性降维条件下相比现有线性集成方法提升了分类准确率；引入目标变量感知的图正则化和中心化进一步带来增益；此外，降维方式的选择对分类准确率和重建风险均有显著影响。

Conclusion: NKI有效解决了非线性降维下中间表示的对齐问题，同时通过图正则化和中心化融入了下游任务有用的信息，在隐私保护数据协作分析中具有优势。

Original Abstract: Collaborative analysis of decentralized confidential datasets is important, but direct sharing of original datasets is often restricted by privacy and institutional constraints. Data collaboration (DC) analysis transforms each dataset into privacy-preserving intermediate representations via party-specific obfuscation functions and integrates them into common collaboration representations using an anchor dataset. However, many existing DC analysis methods rely on linear transformations for data obfuscation and integration, which may increase reconstruction risk. Although nonlinear dimensionality reduction can mitigate this risk, conventional linear integration methods cannot accurately align intermediate representations produced by nonlinear transformations. Moreover, existing integration methods mainly minimize discrepancies among parties and do not explicitly incorporate geometric or target-variable information useful for downstream analysis. To overcome these limitations, we first formulate linear kernel integration (LKI) as a linear integration method and then kernelize it to obtain nonlinear kernel integration (NKI). NKI admits a globally optimal solution via kernel ridge regression and an eigenvalue problem. We also introduce graph regularization and a centering constraint so that the target representation can capture geometric and target-variable information useful for downstream analysis. Experiments on image classification tasks demonstrate that NKI improves classification accuracy over existing linear integration methods under nonlinear dimensionality reduction, with further gains from target-variable-aware graph regularization and centering. The results also show that dimensionality reduction choices substantially affect both classification accuracy and reconstruction risk.

中文摘要翻译: 对去中心化保密数据集的协作分析具有重要意义，但原始数据集的直接共享常受隐私和制度约束。数据协作分析通过各参与方特有的混淆函数将每个数据集转换为隐私保护的中间表示，并利用锚定数据集将其集成到公共协作表示中。然而，许多现有数据协作分析方法依赖线性变换进行数据混淆与集成，这可能增加重建风险。尽管非线性降维可以减轻这一风险，但传统的线性集成方法无法准确对齐由非线性变换产生的中间表示。此外，现有集成方法主要致力于最小化各参与方之间的差异，并未显式融入对下游分析有用的几何信息或目标变量信息。为克服这些局限，我们首先将线性核集成形式化为一种线性集成方法，然后对其进行核化以得到非线性核集成。NKI通过核岭回归和一个特征值问题求得全局最优解。我们还引入了图正则化和中心化约束，使目标表示能够捕获对下游分析有用的几何和目标变量信息。在图像分类任务上的实验表明，在非线性降维条件下，NKI相比现有线性集成方法提升了分类准确率，而目标变量感知的图正则化和中心化进一步带来了性能增益。结果还显示，降维方式的选择对分类准确率和重建风险均有显著影响。

</details>


### [187] [Explainable Comparison of Feature-Based and Deep Learning Models for TROPOMI Methane Plume Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27236)
*Solomiia Kurchaba,Joannes D. Maasakkers,Berend J. Schuit,Ilse Aben*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 比较基于特征的传统机器学习模型（SVC、随机森林、XGBoost）和基于图像的深度学习模型（ResNet-18、ResNet-34）用于TROPOMI甲烷羽流与伪影分类，发现模型性能取决于评估设置，并提供运营筛选流程的模型选择建议。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有甲烷羽流检测中存在大量由地形、反照率、气溶胶等导致的伪影，此前基于SVC的特征工程方法受限于专家设计特征，破坏了像素空间关系并丢失信息，亟需更有效的分类方法。

Method: 在平衡和不平衡评估设置下，系统比较基于特征的传统机器学习模型（SVC、随机森林、XGBoost）和基于图像的深度学习模型（ResNet-18、ResNet-34）的甲烷羽流-伪影分类性能，并应用SHAP可解释性分析。

Result: 给出了两种模型族在平衡和不平衡设置下的性能对比结果，并通过SHAP解释揭示了模型决策依据。

Conclusion: 为CAMS甲烷热点探测等运营筛查流程中的模型选择提供了实践指导，指出需要根据具体评估设置选择合适的模型。

Original Abstract: Continuous and global detection of large methane emissions is a crucial step for global warming mitigation. Satellite observations, such as from S5P/TROPOMI, combined with plume detection algorithms, can play a key role in this effort. However, not all TROPOMI plume detections that look like methane emission plumes are the result of actual emissions. A significant part of the plume-like features in the data are retrieval artifacts. Such artifacts could be the result of variations in elevation or albedo gradients, high concentrations of aerosols, coastal lines, water bodies, etc. Previous work approached the problem of plume-artifact classification by means of a Support Vector Machine Classifier (SVC), trained on an extensive set of observation-based scalar features designed by domain experts. However, such an approach limits the information scope received by the algorithm to what is deemed to be important by the experts, breaks the spatial relationship between pixels, and loses information during the process of statistical aggregation. In this study, we compare feature-based (SVC, Random Forest, XGBoost) and image-based (ResNet-18, ResNet-34) models for methane plume-artifact classification under balanced and imbalanced evaluation settings. To interpret the results, we apply SHAP-based explainability to both model families. Our findings provide practical guidance for model selection in operational methane-screening workflows such as the CAMS Methane Hotspot Explorer.

中文摘要翻译: 持续、全球性地检测大型甲烷排放是全球变暖减缓的关键一步。结合羽流检测算法的卫星观测（如S5P/TROPOMI）在这一工作中可以发挥重要作用。然而，并非所有看起来像甲烷排放羽流的TROPOMI羽流检测都是实际排放的结果。数据中相当一部分羽流状特征是检索伪影。这类伪影可能源于高程或反照率梯度变化、高浓度气溶胶、海岸线、水体等。此前的工作通过领域专家设计的大量基于观测的标量特征，训练支持向量机分类器（SVC）来解决羽流-伪影分类问题。然而，这种方法将算法接收的信息范围局限于专家认为重要的内容，破坏了像素之间的空间关系，并在统计聚合过程中丢失信息。本研究在平衡和不平衡评估设置下，比较了基于特征（SVC、随机森林、XGBoost）和基于图像（ResNet-18、ResNet-34）的模型用于甲烷羽流-伪影分类。为解释结果，我们对两类模型族应用了基于SHAP的可解释性分析。我们的研究结果为CAMS甲烷热点探测等运营甲烷筛查流程中的模型选择提供了实践指导。

</details>


### [188] [Symbolic Regression via Latent Iterative Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27245)
*Xieting Chu,Sriram Vishwanath,Vijay Ganesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Latent Equation Embedding (LEE)框架，通过迭代摊销推理和函数接地潜空间，缩小符号回归中神经方法的摊销差距，在保持高精度的同时生成更简单的表达式。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 神经符号回归方法虽然能通过单次前向传播直接映射观测数据到表达式，但这种摊销推理存在"摊销差距"——单次预测与真实后验之间存在偏差。现有方法无法有效弥补这一差距。

Method: LEE学习一个共享潜空间Z，包含三个组件：编码器f_θ联合嵌入符号标记和数值观测；表达式解码器g_expr从z重建公式；评估解码器g_eval从z预测函数值，将潜空间显式接地到函数行为。推理时通过将解码表达式与观测联合重新编码进行迭代精炼，并交织可微梯度下降与离散重新编码，形成混合迭代-梯度优化过程。

Result: 在SRBench上三个噪声水平下，对比19个基线方法（包括遗传编程、符号-神经混合方法、预训练Transformer），LEE生成的表达式比最强精度导向基线（Operon、GP-GOMEA、TPSR、RAG-SR、GenSR等）简单2-10倍，复杂度为8-11对比20-90。推动了精度-复杂度帕累托前沿的低复杂度区域，且随噪声增加表现优雅退化。

Conclusion: LEE通过迭代摊销推理有效弥合了神经符号回归中的摊销差距，在保持竞争性精度的同时显著降低了表达式复杂度，为符号回归提供了一种兼顾精度与简洁性的有效框架。

Original Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) seeks closed-form mathematical expressions that fit observed data. Neural SR methods amortize the search by training an encoder to map observations directly to expressions in a single pass, but this amortized inference leaves a residual amortization gap between its one-shot prediction and the true posterior. We propose Latent Equation Embedding (LEE), a framework that closes this gap through iterative amortized inference in a functionally grounded latent space. LEE learns a shared latent space Z equipped with three components: an encoder f_theta that jointly embeds symbolic tokens and numerical observations into a single latent vector z; an expression decoder g_expr that reconstructs formulas from z; and an evaluation decoder g_eval that predicts function values from z, explicitly grounding the latent space in functional behavior. At inference, LEE performs iterative refinement by re-encoding decoded expressions jointly with observations, progressively improving the latent estimate. LEE uses the encoder itself as a learned inference optimizer: each re-encoding step implicitly computes the mismatch between the candidate and the data. Because g_eval is differentiable in z, we additionally interleave continuous gradient descent with discrete re-encoding, yielding a hybrid iterative and gradient refinement procedure. On SRBench across three noise levels, against 19 baselines spanning genetic programming, symbolic-neural hybrids, and pre-trained Transformers, LEE produces expressions 2--10x simpler than the strongest accuracy-oriented baselines, including Operon, GP-GOMEA, TPSR, RAG-SR, and GenSR, with complexity 8--11 versus 20--90. These results advance the low-complexity region of the accuracy-complexity Pareto frontier and show graceful degradation as noise increases.

中文摘要翻译: 符号回归旨在寻找拟合观测数据的闭式数学表达式。神经符号回归方法通过训练编码器将观测数据直接单次映射为表达式来摊销搜索过程，但这种摊销推理在其单次预测与真实后验之间留下了残余的"摊销差距"。我们提出潜方程嵌入（LEE）框架，通过在函数接地的潜空间中进行迭代摊销推理来弥合这一差距。LEE学习一个共享潜空间Z，配备三个组件：编码器f_θ将符号标记和数值观测联合嵌入为单一潜向量z；表达式解码器g_expr从z重建公式；评估解码器g_eval从z预测函数值，从而将潜空间显式接地到函数行为。在推理时，LEE通过将解码后的表达式与观测联合重新编码来执行迭代精炼，逐步改进潜估计。LEE将编码器本身用作学习到的推理优化器：每次重新编码步骤隐式计算候选与数据之间的不匹配度。由于g_eval关于z可微，我们还将连续梯度下降与离散重新编码交替进行，形成混合的迭代与梯度精炼过程。在SRBench上跨越三个噪声水平，对比19个涵盖遗传编程、符号-神经混合方法和预训练Transformer的基线方法，LEE生成的表达式比最强精度导向基线（包括Operon、GP-GOMEA、TPSR、RAG-SR和GenSR）简单2-10倍，复杂度为8-11对比20-90。这些结果推进了精度-复杂度帕累托前沿的低复杂度区域，并展现出随噪声增加而优雅退化的特性。

</details>


### [189] [Kan Extension Transformers: A Categorical Unification of Attention, Diffusion, and Predict-Detach Self-Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27259)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kan扩展变换器（KETs）作为统一多种Transformer实现的范畴论框架，将Transformer层视为加权结构化扩展算子，并在严格因果和预测-分离两种机制下对12种实现进行实验验证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有Transformer实现种类繁多，缺乏统一的数学框架来描述和理解它们之间的结构关系，尤其是标准注意力、几何Transformer和高阶情形之间的内在联系。

Method: 提出KETs范畴论框架，将Transformer层统一视为加权结构化扩展算子：标准注意力对应单邻域情形，几何Transformer的关联混合对应稀疏边受限情形，KET对应高阶单纯复形情形。同时提出"预测-分离"机制，让扩展算子作用于分离的预测载体而非教师强制的隐藏状态，作为有效的自条件化机制。

Result: 在Penn Treebank、WikiText-2和WikiText-103上验证了12种Transformer实现。严格因果设置下，二次KET在WikiText-2和WikiText-103上是最强的因果架构。但所有数据集上最大的收益来自预测-分离机制，而非仅改变邻域族。

Conclusion: KETs框架成功统一了多种Transformer实现，揭示了其内在结构联系；预测-分离机制带来的性能提升比单纯改变注意力邻域结构更为显著。

Original Abstract: We propose Kan Extension Transformers (KETs) as a unifying categorical framework for a diverse group of Transformer implementations. The core claim is that a Transformer layer can be viewed as a weighted structured extension operator: standard attention is the singleton-neighborhood case, Geometric Transformer style incidence mixing is a sparse edge-restricted case, and KET is the higher-order simplicial case. This lens also clarifies a bridge to diffusion-style completion. When the extension operator acts on detached predictive carriers instead of teacher-forced hidden states, it becomes a valid self-conditioning mechanism that exposes noncausal structure without leaking gold future tokens. We include a comprehensive experimental validation of 12 different Transformer implementations varying across strict-causal and predict-detach regimes on Penn Treebank, WikiText-2, and WikiText-103. In the strict-causal setting, quadratic KET is the strongest model among the compared causal architectures on WikiText-2 and WikiText-103. Across all datasets, however, the largest gains come from the predict-detach regime rather than from changing the neighborhood family alone.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了Kan扩展变换器（KETs），作为多种Transformer实现的统一范畴论框架。核心主张是Transformer层可以视为一种加权结构化扩展算子：标准注意力是单邻域情形，几何Transformer风格的关联混合是稀疏边受限情形，而KET则是高阶单纯复形情形。这一视角还阐明了通向扩散式补全的桥梁。当扩展算子作用于分离的预测载体而非教师强制的隐藏状态时，它成为一种有效的自条件化机制，能够在不会泄露未来金标令牌的情况下暴露非因果结构。我们在Penn Treebank、WikiText-2和WikiText-103上，对严格因果和预测-分离两种机制下的12种不同Transformer实现进行了全面的实验验证。在严格因果设置中，二次KET是WikiText-2和WikiText-103上被比较的因果架构中最强的模型。然而，在所有数据集上，最大的收益来自预测-分离机制，而非仅改变邻域族。

</details>


### [190] [Transfer Learning using 66 Diseases for Disease Forecasting Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27269)
*Lauren J Beesley,Alexander C Murph,Dave Osthus,Lauren A Castro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在66种传染病和多数据流上训练机器学习模型，发现整合多数据流能改善84.9%时间序列的预测性能，但数据质量至关重要，并构建了公开可用的传染病预测数据库。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统的疾病预测模型通常依赖单一数据流，当历史数据短或噪声大时模型变得脆弱。近期研究表明整合多种报告系统或使用迁移学习能提升性能，但现有工作覆盖范围有限，缺乏对多疾病、多数据流系统性的价值评估。

Method: 在涵盖66种传染病和多个数据流的大规模数据集上训练机器学习模型，系统性地评估整合不同数据流对20个疾病数据流预测的影响，并分析数据质量与相似性对预测效果的作用。

Result: 整合多数据流在84.9%的时间序列和模型结构中提升了预测性能；但数据质量至关重要，加入与目标数据流差异过大的数据有时会降低预测性能；构建了公开可用的传染病预测数据库。

Conclusion: 多数据流融合能显著改善传染病预测，但需谨慎选择数据源，确保数据质量与相关性。该工作的主要贡献是构建了面向传染病预测社区的公开数据库。

Original Abstract: Disease forecasting models typically rely on a single data stream, making models brittle when histories are short or noisy. Recent top-performing models have shown that synthesizing multiple reporting systems for the same disease improves performance. Other recent work takes this idea a step further, using transfer learning to train a forecasting model for one disease using data from a different disease. We expand upon each of these approaches greatly, training machine learning models on data that span 66 infectious diseases and several data streams. We investigate the value of incorporating different data streams for forecasting 20 different disease data streams. We find that incorporating other data streams improves forecasting in the vast majority (84.9%) of time series and model structures considered. However, our work highlights that the quality of the added data matters, where adding data extremely different from the target data stream can sometimes degrade forecast performance. A major contribution of this work is in compiling a publicly-available database of data for use by the infectious disease forecasting community.

中文摘要翻译: 疾病预测模型通常依赖于单一数据流，这使得模型在历史数据较短或噪声较大时变得脆弱。近期一些性能最佳的模型表明，综合同一疾病的多种报告系统能够提升预测性能。其他近期工作则更进一步，利用迁移学习使用一种疾病的数据来训练另一种疾病的预测模型。我们在这些方法的基础上进行了大幅扩展，在涵盖66种传染病和多个数据流的数据上训练机器学习模型。我们研究了整合不同数据流对预测20个不同疾病数据流的价值。结果发现，在绝大多数（84.9%）时间序列和所考虑的模型结构中，整合其他数据流改善了预测性能。然而，我们的工作强调，所加入数据的质量至关重要，当加入与目标数据流差异极大的数据时，有时会降低预测性能。本工作的一项主要贡献是构建了一个公开可用的数据库，供传染病预测社区使用。

</details>


### [191] [Causal Risk Minimization for High-Dimensional Treatments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27281)
*Nikita Dhawan,Arnav Paruthi,Andrew Kim,Lovedeep Gondara,Jekaterina Novikova,Chris J. Maddison*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将因果推断转化为学习问题的方法，以处理高维干预空间（如文本字符串），通过矩平衡误差分解和降维投影实现因果效应的有效估计。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 经典因果估计器假设所有可能的干预都被观测到，但当干预空间高维且多样化（如所有文本字符串空间）时，这一假设不可行，亟需能够处理高维干预空间的因果估计方法。

Method: 将因果推断重新表述为学习问题，在标准假设（如无未观测混杂）下，将因果误差分解为一系列递增阶数的矩平衡误差，并设计直接优化因果估计的目标函数；同时提出将高维干预效应投影到低维干预属性上，使单一模型可回答多个因果问题而无需额外的属性特定训练。

Result: 在高维连续、离散和文本干预（使用亚马逊评论的半合成数据集）上进行了实验，证明高阶平衡误差优化的优势，以及投影因果估计与属性特定估计器相比具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的高阶矩平衡误差优化和干预投影方法能够有效处理高维干预空间的因果估计问题，并在多种数据类型上取得有竞争力的结果。

Original Abstract: Predicting the effect of interventions with many possible variations, e.g., therapeutic content that affects mental health outcomes or an earnings call transcript that drives movement in share price, is useful across several domains. However, classical causal estimators tend to assume that all possible interventions are observed, which is infeasible when interventions vary widely, for instance, in the space of all text strings. We adapt a well-known approach of recasting causal inference as a learning problem, to address high-dimensional treatment spaces. Specifically, under standard assumptions like no unobserved confounding, we show that causal error decomposes into a series of moment-balancing errors of increasing order, and design objectives that directly improve causal estimation. We also show how to project the effect of a high-dimensional treatment onto lower-dimensional treatment attributes, which allows a single model to answer several causal questions without additional attribute-specific training. We empirically evaluate our estimators in settings with high-dimensional continuous, discrete, and text treatments, the last of which used a semi-synthetic dataset of Amazon Reviews. Our experiments demonstrate the benefit of higher-order balance error optimization and competitive performance of projected causal estimates with attribute-specific estimators.

中文摘要翻译: 预测具有多种可能变化的干预效果（例如影响心理健康结果的治疗内容，或驱动股价波动的财报电话会议记录）在多个领域都很有用。然而，经典因果估计器往往假设所有可能的干预都被观测到，这在干预变化广泛（例如在所有文本字符串空间中）时是不可行的。我们采用了将因果推断重新表述为学习问题的著名方法，以处理高维干预空间。具体而言，在标准假设（如无未观测混杂）下，我们证明了因果误差可以分解为一系列递增阶数的矩平衡误差，并设计了直接改进因果估计的目标函数。我们还展示了如何将高维干预的效应投影到低维干预属性上，这使得单个模型无需额外的属性特定训练即可回答多个因果问题。我们在高维连续、离散和文本干预（最后一种使用了亚马逊评论的半合成数据集）场景下对我们的估计器进行了实证评估。实验证明了高阶平衡误差优化的优势，以及投影因果估计与属性特定估计器相比具有竞争力的性能。

</details>


### [192] [Falcon-X: A Time Series Foundation Model for Heterogeneous Multivariate Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27286)
*Yiding Liu,Yifan Hu,Hongjie Xia,Peiyuan Liu,Hongzhou Chen,Xilin Dai,Zewei Dong,Jiang-Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Falcon-X 提出将变量从原始空间解耦并映射到统一的潜在原型空间中，通过原型差异注意力机制和潜在实体注意力实现跨变量交互建模，在多元时间序列预测基准上达到了 SOTA 性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型大多为单变量模型，少数支持跨变量建模的方法仍在原始变量空间中操作，缺乏语义对齐机制且无法捕获变量间复杂的协同与拮抗关系。

Method: Falcon-X 包含三个核心模块：(1) 统一原型差异注意力机制，将变量映射到潜在原型空间并显式评估正负语义亲和性；(2) 潜在实体注意力，在共享空间中高效执行跨变量交互；(3) 变量重组路由器，通过请求-分发机制重建各变量轨迹。

Result: 在 GIFT-Eval 和 fev-bench 两个基准上，Falcon-X 实现了最先进的预测性能。

Conclusion: Falcon-X 提供了一种原则性且可扩展的多元时间序列预测范式，通过解耦变量空间和显式建模正负交互，有效解决了语义对齐和关系表达性的根本限制。

Original Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) are transforming the forecasting paradigm through large-scale cross-domain pretraining. However, most existing TSFMs remain univariate, and recent efforts to enable cross-variate modeling still operate directly within the raw variate space. This design introduces fundamental limitations in semantic alignment and relational expressivity. Specifically, raw-space group mixing lacks a dedicated mechanism to align heterogeneous physical quantities, while standard non-negative attention fails to capture the complex synergistic and antagonistic interactions ubiquitous in real-world systems. To address these challenges, we propose Falcon-X, decouples variates from the raw space and maps them into a unified latent prototype space. Falcon-X employs a Unified Prototype Diff-Attention mechanism that explicitly evaluates both positive and negative semantic affinities to explicitly align heterogeneous variates. Cross-variate interactions are then efficiently performed within this shared space via Latent Entity Attention, naturally facilitating zero-shot structural transfer. Finally, a Variate Reassembly Router robustly reconstructs variate-specific trajectories via a request-and-dispatch mechanism. Extensive evaluations on the GIFT-Eval and fev-bench benchmarks demonstrate that Falcon-X achieves state-of-the-art forecasting performance, offering a principled and scalable paradigm for complex multivariate environments. Falcon-X is publicly released to support future research.

中文摘要翻译: 时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）正在通过大规模跨领域预训练重塑预测范式。然而，现有的大多数 TSFM 仍是单变量的，近期一些实现跨变量建模的工作仍然直接在原始变量空间中操作。这种设计在语义对齐和关系表达性方面引入了根本性限制。具体而言，原始空间中的组混合缺乏对齐异构物理量的专用机制，而标准的非负注意力机制无法捕捉现实系统中普遍存在的复杂协同与拮抗交互。为应对这些挑战，我们提出 Falcon-X，将变量从原始空间解耦并映射到统一的潜在原型空间中。Falcon-X 采用统一原型差异注意力机制，显式评估正负语义亲和性以对齐异构变量。跨变量交互通过潜在实体注意力在共享空间中高效执行，天然支持零样本结构迁移。最后，变量重组路由器通过请求-分发机制鲁棒地重建各变量轨迹。在 GIFT-Eval 和 fev-bench 基准上的广泛评估表明，Falcon-X 达到了最先进的预测性能，为复杂多元环境提供了一种原则性且可扩展的范式。Falcon-X 已公开发布以支持未来研究。

</details>


### [193] [Detectability in Diversity: Improved Canary Crafting for Privacy Auditing in One Run](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27292)
*Mathieu Dagréou,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对单次运行隐私审计的哨点（canary）优化方法，通过贪婪初始化与双层优化相结合，使哨点既高度可检测又最小化相互干扰，从而在更低计算成本下获得更强的隐私泄露估计。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有单次运行的隐私审计方法中，多个哨点（canary）之间的相互干扰导致隐私泄露估计弱于多次运行的方法。因此，需要一种能够同时提高哨点可检测性并减少相互干扰的优化方法。

Method: 方法结合了基于影响函数的贪婪初始化与双层优化过程：贪婪初始化用于选择初始哨点集；双层优化则在最大化可区分性的同时促进哨点在嵌入空间中的多样性，使得可以使用计算高效的双层优化算法。

Result: 实验表明，该方法相比现有哨点生成方法，能够在更低计算成本下获得更强的隐私泄露估计。

Conclusion: 通过优化哨点的可检测性与多样性，可以显著提升单次运行隐私审计的效果，为差分隐私参数的下界估计提供更高效的工具。

Original Abstract: Privacy auditing aims to empirically assess privacy leakage in machine learning models using membership inference attacks (MIAs), and to derive lower bounds on differential privacy (DP) parameters. Recent one-run auditing methods address the high cost of standard approaches by relying on a single training run with multiple "canary" points whose inclusion or exclusion must be detected by the auditor. In this work, we study the problem of efficiently crafting canaries for one-run privacy auditing. Motivated by recent theoretical insights suggesting that interference between canaries contributes to weaker leakage estimates compared to multi-run methods, we propose to optimize canaries to be both highly detectable and minimally interfering. Our approach combines a greedy initialization based on influence functions with a bilevel optimization procedure that maximizes distinguishability while promoting diversity in embedding space, enabling the use of computationally efficient bilevel algorithms. Experiments show that our method achieves stronger privacy leakage estimates at a lower computational cost than existing canary crafting approaches.

中文摘要翻译: 隐私审计旨在通过成员推断攻击（MIA）实证评估机器学习模型中的隐私泄露，并推导差分隐私（DP）参数的下界。最新的单次运行审计方法通过依赖单一训练轮次和多个"哨点"（canary）来降低标准方法的高昂成本，审计者需要检测这些哨点是否被包含在训练集中。本文研究高效构建用于单次运行隐私审计的哨点问题。受最新理论洞见启发——哨点之间的相互干扰会导致比多次运行方法更弱的泄露估计——我们提出优化哨点，使其既高度可检测又最小化相互干扰。我们的方法结合了基于影响函数的贪婪初始化与双层优化过程，在最大化可区分性的同时促进嵌入空间中的多样性，从而能够使用计算高效的双层优化算法。实验表明，该方法相比现有哨点生成方法，能够在更低计算成本下获得更强的隐私泄露估计。

</details>


### [194] [Normal Guidance is what Attention Needs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27306)
*Ethan Harvey,Dennis Johan Loevlie,Michael C. Hughes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Normal Guidance的正则化技术，通过鼓励注意力分布服从钟形曲线，显著提升了基于注意力和Transformer的多实例学习方法在3D医学图像切片级分类中的定位性能，同时在整体扫描分类中保持竞争力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有弱监督（仅体素级标签）的3D医学图像分类方法中，一个忽视图像内容的简单中心基线方法在切片级分类上反而优于复杂的注意力与Transformer方法。作者受此启发，希望提升注意力机制在切片级定位上的表现。

Method: 提出Normal Guidance正则化技术，在注意力机制中引入先验知识，鼓励模型学习的注意力分布服从钟形（正态）曲线，从而在弱监督场景下实现更好的切片级定位。

Result: 在三个医学影像数据集（总计超过400万张2D切片）上的实验表明，Normal Guidance能使基于注意力和Transformer的MIL方法在切片级定位上显著优于现有最先进方法，同时在整体扫描分类上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: Normal Guidance作为一种简单有效的正则化方法，能够在不牺牲整体分类性能的前提下，大幅提升弱监督3D医学图像中切片级定位的准确性。

Original Abstract: We consider training classifiers for 3D medical images using only one binary label for the entire volume rather than a label for each 2D slice. In such weakly supervised settings, can we learn accurate classifiers for slice-level predictions? Attention-based multiple instance learning (MIL) can produce an attention score for every slice. Yet recent work demonstrates that a simple center-focused baseline that ignores image content can outperform attention-based and transformer-based MIL at slice-level classification of 3D brain scans. We show this baseline also outperforms existing MIL at slice-level classification of thoracic and abdominal CT scans. Motivated by this baseline, we propose Normal Guidance, a regularization technique that encourages the learned attention distribution to follow a bell-shaped curve. Across three medical imaging datasets totaling over 4 million 2D slices, we show our Normal Guidance enables attention-based and transformer-based MIL methods to deliver significantly better slice-level localization than the state-of-the-art while remaining competitive at whole-scan classification.

中文摘要翻译: 我们考虑在仅使用整个体素的一个二分类标签（而非每个2D切片一个标签）的情况下训练3D医学图像分类器。在这种弱监督设置下，能否学习到准确的切片级分类器？基于注意力的多实例学习（MIL）方法可以为每个切片生成注意力得分。然而，近期研究表明，一个忽视图像内容的简单中心基线方法在3D脑部扫描的切片级分类上可以超越基于注意力和Transformer的MIL方法。我们证明该基线方法在胸部与腹部CT扫描的切片级分类上也优于现有的MIL方法。受此基线启发，我们提出了一种名为Normal Guidance的正则化技术，它鼓励学习到的注意力分布服从钟形曲线。在三个医学影像数据集（总计超过400万张2D切片）上的实验表明，Normal Guidance使基于注意力和Transformer的MIL方法在切片级定位上显著优于当前最先进方法，同时在整体扫描分类上保持竞争力。

</details>


### [195] [Greening AI Inference with Accuracy and Latency-aware User Incentives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27309)
*Vasilios A. Siris,Adamantia Stamou,George D. Stamoulis,Konstantinos Varsos,Ramin Khalili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于用户对推理质量和延迟的估值以及环境意识的AI推理激励机制框架，通过双层服务订阅提供折扣以换取碳排放减少。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: AI服务的广泛使用引发了环境可持续性担忧，而AI推理的碳排放是主要贡献因素，需要设计合理的激励机制来平衡碳排放与用户体验质量（QoE）。

Method: 构建了一个基于用户对推理质量和延迟的估值以及环境意识的激励框架，考虑碳排放与两个QoE参数之间的权衡，并通过实用的双层服务订阅（标准层与折扣层）实现，在碳强度高时以较低质量和较高延迟服务部分请求。

Result: 该框架能够适应不同AI模型规模和资源分配下的碳排放-QoE权衡，通过折扣订阅方式激励用户接受碳排放降低的服务，为AI提供商提供灵活的服务调度能力。

Conclusion: 提出的双层订阅激励机制能够有效平衡AI推理的碳排放与用户体验质量，为AI服务的环境可持续性提供了可行的商业和实践方案。

Original Abstract: The widespread use of AI services has raised concerns for its environmental sustainability, towards which recent studies have identified carbon emissions of AI inference as the major contributor. This paper introduces a framework for designing AI inference incentives based on the users' valuation for inference quality and latency, together with their environmental consciousness, while accounting for the tradeoff between carbon emissions and the two QoE parameters. Our approach can accommodate different tradeoffs, that depend on the size and complexity of the AI models and the allocation of resources to serve inference requests. The incentives can be offered through a practical two-tier service subscription that offers users a discount in exchange for reduced carbon emissions. The discounted service option gives the AI provider the flexibility to serve some percentage of inference requests at a lower quality and higher latency during periods of high carbon intensity.

中文摘要翻译: AI服务的广泛使用引发了对其环境可持续性的担忧，近期研究指出AI推理的碳排放是主要贡献因素。本文介绍了一个框架，用于基于用户对推理质量和延迟的估值及其环境意识来设计AI推理激励机制，同时兼顾碳排放与两个QoE参数之间的权衡。我们的方法能够适应不同的权衡关系，这些权衡取决于AI模型的规模和复杂度以及用于服务推理请求的资源分配。这些激励可以通过一种实用的双层服务订阅来实现，为用户提供折扣以换取减少的碳排放。折扣服务选项使AI提供商能够在碳强度较高的时期灵活地以较低质量和较高延迟来服务一定百分比的推理请求。

</details>


### [196] [Probabilistic Smoothing with Ratio-Monotone Transforms for Global Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27316)
*Kukyoung Jang,Taehyun Cho,Junrui Zhang,Ping Xu,Kyungjae Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合灵活对称单峰核与单调比变换的通用概率平滑框架，在不依赖递减平滑调度的情况下保持全局最优性，并提供随机梯度上升的复杂度界和方差缩减证明，实验表明在对抗攻击等任务中鲁棒性更强。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于概率平滑的全局优化方法依赖高斯核和特定变换，超参数敏感且鲁棒性有限，亟需一种更通用、更鲁棒的平滑框架。

Method: 提出通用平滑框架：将灵活对称单峰核与单调比变换相结合。在温和条件下证明平滑后的目标函数保持全局最大值，且当放大系数足够大时所有驻点集中在真实最优点附近，无需递减平滑调度；进一步给出了随机梯度上升的显式复杂度界，并证明留一法基线能有效降低方差。

Result: 在基准测试和黑盒对抗攻击实验上，所提方法在鲁棒性和性能上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的通用平滑框架在理论上保证了全局最优性的保持与驻点的收敛性，在实践中展现出更强的鲁棒性和竞争力。

Original Abstract: Probabilistic smoothing is a standard tool for global optimization, but existing methods rely on Gaussian kernels and specific transforms, often resulting in strong hyperparameter sensitivity and limited robustness. We propose a general smoothing framework that combines flexible symmetric unimodal kernels with monotonic ratio-based transformations. Under mild conditions, we show that the smoothed objective preserves the global maximizer and that all stationary points concentrate near the true optimum for sufficiently large amplification, without requiring a decreasing smoothing schedule. We further provide explicit complexity bounds for stochastic gradient ascent and show that a leave-one-out baseline provably reduces variance. Experiments on high-dimensional benchmarks and black-box adversarial attacks demonstrate improved robustness and competitive performance.

中文摘要翻译: 概率平滑是全局优化的标准工具，但现有方法依赖高斯核和特定变换，通常导致超参数敏感且鲁棒性有限。本文提出一种通用平滑框架，将灵活对称单峰核与单调比变换相结合。在温和条件下，我们证明了平滑后的目标函数保持全局最大值，且当放大系数足够大时所有驻点集中在真实最优点附近，无需递减平滑调度。我们进一步给出了随机梯度上升的显式复杂度界，并证明留一法基线能有效降低方差。在高维基准测试和黑盒对抗攻击实验上，该方法展现出更强的鲁棒性和有竞争力的性能。

</details>


### [197] [From Scores to Gibbs Correctors: Accelerating Uniform-Rate Discrete Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27352)
*Yuchen Liang,Ness Shroff,Yingbin Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于吉布斯采样的离散扩散模型加速矫正器GADD，无需额外训练即可实现polylog级别的采样复杂度，并在多个任务上验证了其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 离散扩散模型（尤其是均匀速率模型）生成样本需要大量步数，现有加速方法要么依赖额外训练，要么存在混合缓慢的问题。

Method: 利用具体得分函数的结构直接构造吉布斯后验似然，无需额外训练即可作为矫正器（corrector）嵌入预测-矫正框架中，并引入基于归纳论证的理论分析框架来追踪预测器迭代中的误差传播。

Result: 理论上证明了GADD达到$\mathcal{O}(\mathrm{polylog} (\varepsilon^{-1}))$的整体采样复杂度，这是均匀速率离散扩散模型的首个此类速率；实验上在合成数据、零样本文本采样和零样本条件音乐生成中均优于欧拉方法和CTMC矫正器等基线。

Conclusion: GADD是一种高效、无需额外训练的离散扩散模型加速方法，在理论和实践上都优于现有方法，且其理论分析框架对预测-矫正方法的分析具有独立价值。

Original Abstract: Discrete diffusion models have achieved strong empirical performance in text and other symbolic domains, but, especially for uniform-rate models, they often require many steps to generate a single sample. Existing acceleration methods either rely on training additional quantities or suffer from slow mixing. In this work, we propose a novel Gibbs-based corrector for discrete diffusion models, termed Gibbs-Accelerated Discrete Diffusion (GADD). GADD leverages the structure of the concrete score function to construct Gibbs posterior likelihoods directly, without requiring any additional training beyond standard score estimation. We show that GADD achieves an overall sampling complexity of $\mathcal{O}(\mathrm{polylog} (\varepsilon^{-1}))$, yielding the first such rate for diffusion-based samplers for uniform-rate discrete diffusion models. We also conduct numerical experiments demonstrating the practical advantages of GADD across synthetic data, zero-shot text sampling, and zero-shot conditional music generation. These results corroborate the theory and show that GADD consistently improves sample quality and wall-clock efficiency over standard baselines, including vanilla Euler methods and CTMC correctors. Beyond this, our theoretical analysis introduces a novel framework for analyzing predictor-corrector methods in discrete diffusion models, which may be of independent interest. Unlike existing approaches that rely on the Girsanov change-of-measure technique, our method is based on an induction argument that tracks error propagation across predictor iterations while accounting for inaccuracies in the corrector updates.

中文摘要翻译: 离散扩散模型在文本及其他符号领域取得了强大的经验性能，但尤其在均匀速率模型中，生成单个样本通常需要大量步数。现有的加速方法要么依赖额外训练，要么存在混合缓慢的问题。本文提出了一种新颖的基于吉布斯采样的离散扩散模型矫正器，称为吉布斯加速离散扩散（GADD）。GADD利用具体得分函数的结构直接构造吉布斯后验似然，无需在标准得分估计之外进行任何额外训练。我们证明了GADD达到了$\mathcal{O}(\mathrm{polylog} (\varepsilon^{-1}))$的整体采样复杂度，这是均匀速率离散扩散模型中基于扩散的采样器首次获得此类速率。我们还进行了数值实验，在合成数据、零样本文本采样和零样本条件音乐生成中展示了GADD的实践优势。这些结果证实了理论分析，并表明GADD在样本质量和实际时间效率上持续优于标准基线，包括朴素欧拉方法和连续时间马尔可夫链矫正器。此外，我们的理论分析引入了一个新的框架来分析离散扩散模型中的预测-矫正方法，该框架可能具有独立的研究价值。与依赖吉尔萨诺夫测度变换技术的现有方法不同，我们的方法基于一种归纳论证，在考虑矫正器更新不准确性的同时，追踪预测器迭代中的误差传播。

</details>


### [198] [MobileMoE: Scaling On-Device Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27358)
*Yanbei Chen,Hanxian Huang,Ernie Chang,Jacob Szwejbka,Digant Desai,Zechun Liu,Vikas Chandra,Raghuraman Krishnamoorthi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MobileMoE提出了面向手机端部署的混合专家语言模型系列（0.3-0.9B激活参数，1.3-5.3B总参数），通过制定设备端MoE缩放定律并采用四阶段训练策略，在14项基准测试中以更少的计算量和参数量匹配或超越现有领先模型，并在商用智能手机上实现了首个高效MoE推理。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MoE架构虽在千亿参数大语言模型中取得主导地位，但在十亿以下参数规模（适合手机端部署）的优势尚未被充分探索，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 制定设备端MoE缩放定律，联合优化移动内存和计算约束下的MoE架构，找到适中的稀疏度配合细粒度共享专家的最优配置；2) 采用四阶段训练：预训练、中期训练、指令微调、量化感知训练，全部基于开源数据集；3) 在商用智能手机上实现首个高效MoE推理。

Result: 在14项基准测试中，MobileMoE以2-4倍更少的推理FLOPs匹配或超越领先的设备端稠密LLM；以最多60%更少的参数匹配或超越最先进MoE模型OLMoE-1B-7B；在INT4权重内存下，MobileMoE-S相比稠密基线MobileLLM-Pro实现1.8-3.8倍更快的预填充和2.2-3.4倍更快的解码。

Conclusion: MobileMoE为手机端部署的MoE语言模型建立了新的帕累托前沿，证明适中的稀疏度配合细粒度共享专家在移动约束下兼具内存和计算最优性，且通过四阶段训练和端侧推理实现落地。

Original Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) has become the de facto architecture for hundred-billion-parameter language models, yet its advantages at sub-billion scales for on-device deployment remain largely unexplored. To close this gap, we present MobileMoE, a family of on-device MoE language models with sub-billion active parameters (0.3-0.9B active and 1.3-5.3B total) that establish a new Pareto frontier for on-device LLMs. We first formulate an on-device MoE scaling law that jointly optimizes MoE architecture under mobile memory and compute constraints, identifying an on-device sweet spot - moderate sparsity with fine-grained and shared experts - that is simultaneously memory and compute-optimal. Building on the derived architectures, we train MobileMoE with a four-stage recipe covering pre-training, mid-training, instruction fine-tuning, and quantization-aware training, all on open-source datasets. Across 14 benchmarks, MobileMoE matches or exceeds leading on-device dense LLMs with 2-4$\times$ fewer inference FLOPs, and matches or surpasses the state-of-the-art MoE OLMoE-1B-7B with up to 60% fewer parameters. To bridge the last mile to mobile deployment, we provide the first efficient MoE inference on commodity smartphones with comprehensive on-device profiling. At comparable INT4 weight memory, MobileMoE-S delivers $1.8$-$3.8\times$ faster prefill and $2.2$-$3.4\times$ faster decode than the dense baseline MobileLLM-Pro.

中文摘要翻译: 混合专家（MoE）已成为千亿参数语言模型的事实标准架构，但其在十亿以下参数规模（适用于设备端部署）的优势仍鲜有探索。为填补这一空白，我们提出MobileMoE——一系列面向设备端的MoE语言模型，其活跃参数为十亿以下（0.3B-0.9B活跃参数，1.3B-5.3B总参数），为设备端大语言模型建立了新的帕累托前沿。我们首先制定了一个设备端MoE缩放定律，在移动内存和计算约束下联合优化MoE架构，从而确定了一个设备端的最佳平衡点——采用适中稀疏度搭配细粒度共享专家——这同时在内存和计算上达到最优。基于推导出的架构，我们采用四阶段训练方案训练MobileMoE，涵盖预训练、中期训练、指令微调和量化感知训练，全部基于开源数据集。在14项基准测试中，MobileMoE以2-4倍更少的推理FLOPs匹配或超越领先的设备端稠密大语言模型，并以最多60%更少的参数匹配或超越最先进的MoE模型OLMoE-1B-7B。为弥合移动端部署的最后一公里，我们在商用智能手机上提供了首个高效的MoE推理方案，并进行了全面的设备端性能分析。在可比的INT4权重内存条件下，MobileMoE-S相比稠密基线MobileLLM-Pro实现了1.8-3.8倍更快的预填充速度和2.2-3.4倍更快的解码速度。

</details>
